tamiḻ varalāṟu - 1

sanikuṇuṭakuḻūra ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.


cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭa moḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceyta
toru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etirpārppataṟ
killi. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintukoḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
*  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṅkilattil eḻutiya cāṉṟitaḻiṉ tamiḻākkam.

mukavuri
ulakamoḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram (2796) eṉak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, toṉmi, muṉmi; eṇmi (eḷimi), oṇmi (oḷimi); iḷami, vaḷami; tāymi; tūymi; cemmi, mummi; iṉimi, taṉimi; perumi, tirumi; iyaṉmi, viyaṉmi eṉṉum palvakic ciṟappukkaḷi oruṅkēyuṭiyatu tamiḻēyāyiṉum, atu attakiyateṉa iṉṟu tamiḻarālum aṟiyappaṭavilli. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam, (kāraṇam), paḻam pāṇṭināṭākiya kumarikkaṇṭattik kaṭal
koṇṭatum, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭatum mutaliru kaḻakak
kālattatumāṉa paltuṟip paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻivuṇṭatum, tamiḻa vēntar vēta āriyari nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉa nampit tammiyum tam iṉattāriyum tāḻttik koṇṭatum, vētāriyamum camaṟkirutamumākiya vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi eṉa nampappaṭṭatum, vāymit tamiḻppulavar vāḻkakiyiḻantatum, tamiḻiṉ tūymi kulivuṇṭatum, tamiḻ varalāṟu maṟivuṇṭatum, tamiḻ kōyilvaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takātamoḻiyeṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭatum, tamiḻara tamiḻi ikaḻntu puṟakkaṇittatum, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyak kulappiriviṉiyāl tamiḻar ciṉṉapiṉṉamākac citivuṇṭu oṟṟumiyaṟṟatum, pētimiyūṭṭum purāṇa meṉṉum tolkatikaḷiyē nīḷak kaṟṟuṅ kēṭṭum pakuttaṟi
viḻantatum, avaruḷ nūṟṟiṟkut toṇṇūṟṟivar taṟkuṟi
kaḷāṉatum, piṟappiḻanta pōlittamiḻar toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻik kāṭṭikkoṭuttu varuvatum, piṟkālattamiḻakatti aṭuttaṭuttup palvēṟu vēṟṟaracar āṇṭatum, ākum.
āṅkilarāṭciyālum āṅkilak kalviyālum, oru cārttamiḻarkkuk kaṇtiṟakkap peṟṟatiṉ payaṉāka, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ taṉittamiḻiyakkan tōṉṟit tamiḻ mīṇṭun taḷirttuta taḻikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āyiṉ, āṅkilar nīṅkiyapiṉ, āriyappaki putiya intit tuṇiyoṭu vantu araciyalvāyilākat tamiḻi  aṭarttuvarukiṉṟatu.  itaṉāl,  araciyal, potuviyal, palkalikkaḻakaviyal ākiya muttuṟi
yilum, tamiḻukkum uṇmikkum māṟāṉa coṟpoḻivukaḷum veḷiyīṭukaḷum toṭarntu nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻik kākkavēṇṭiya poṟuppāṉa pataviyiluḷḷa palkalikkaḻakat talimit tamiḻppērāciriyar, tamiḻik kāṭṭikkoṭuppatum ataṟku uṭantiyāyiruppatum atippaṟṟik kavalātiruppatumāka munnilip paṭṭuḷḷaṉar. avaruḷ, kāṭṭikkoṭuppavar āriya aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻik kaṟṟavar; ēṉiyar āriyatti aṭiyōṭaṟiyātavar. ivviru cārārkkum, nelli vaṭṭāra nāṭṭuppuṟat tamiḻi aṟiyāmi, contamāy, moḻi
yārāycci ceyyāmi, teṉcolvaḷan teriyāmi, tamiḻppaṟṟiṉmi, taṉṉala mutaṉmi, neñcura miṉmi ākiyavi potuvām.
inniliyil, tamiḻiṉ uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟu veḷi
varuvatu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu. tamiḻvaralāṟu eṉṉum peyaril ituvari veḷivantavi yellām, perumpālum tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟum moḻipaṟṟiya potuvāṉa ceyti
kaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟuvaṉavumākavē uḷḷaṉa. varalāṟṟiyum morinūliyum taḻuvi, mutaṉmutalāka veḷivarum tamiḻ
moḻi varalāṟu ituvē. nāṉ cēlaṅkallūrit tamiḻp
pērāciriyaṉā yiruntapōtē, tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ iti veḷiyiṭumāṟu eṉṉip paṇittārkaḷ. āyiṉum, atu ituvari niṟivēṟa vāyppillātu pōyiṟṟu.
āṅkilēyarum airōppiyarum amerikkarumāṉa mēliyar iṟṟi aṟiviyalkaḷil taliciṟantavarum vaḻikāṭṭi kaḷumāyiruppiṉum, tamiḻar naṇṇilak kaṭaṟpaṭiyiṉiṉṟu teṉṉāṭu vantava
reṉṟum, intiya nākarikam vēta āriyar kaṇṭateṉṟum, iru tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ avaruḷḷattil āḻa vērūṉṟi
yiruppataṉāl,  pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil, piṟpaṭṭa camaṟkirutatti muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻuk kaṭippaṭiyāka vit
tāyntu, āriya vemmaṇaṟ pālipparappil alintu vantu vaḻiteriyātu mayaṅki, ellāmoḻikaḷum aṟikuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉṟum, āyiramāṇṭiṟ korumuṟi moḻi
kaḷellām aṭiyōṭu māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, ataṉāl moḻit tōṟṟattik kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟum, muṭipu
koṇṭu, mēṟkoṇṭu uṇmikāṇa muṭiyāvāṟu, tam kaṇṇit tāmē iṟukak kaṭṭikkoṇṭavar. moḻinūl tiṟavukōl tamiḻilēyē uḷḷa eṉṉum uṇmiyi avar uṇarvārāyiṉ, viyakkattakka uṇmikaḷ veḷippaṭuvatu tiṇṇam. ātalāl, avar iṉimēlāyiṉum takkavāṟu tamiḻil kavaṉattic celuttuvārāka.
iṟṟit tamiḻaruḷḷum nūṟṟiṟ keṇpatiṉmar aṟikuṟi
kaḷum tāymoḻiyuṇarcci yillātavarumāyiruppataṉālum, āṅkilaṅ kaṟṟāruṭ perumpālār tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kallāmi
yālum, kallūrikaḷil tamiḻāciriyap paṇiceyap pukum iḷiñarum tamakkuḷḷa tamiḻppaṟṟi viḷakkumāṟu palkalikkaḻakac cūḻveḷi āriyavaṇukkaḷ nampiyiruppataṉālum, araciyaṟ kaṭcikaḷuḷ ti. mu. ka. tavira ēṉiya vellām intiyi yēṟṟut tamiḻipa puṟakkaṇippatālum, iṉṟu tamiḻap
paṇpāṭṭip perum pālum tāṅkiniṟkum uyarnilit tamiḻarāṉa uyarnilip paḷḷit tamiḻāciriyarum, pututtali muṟiyāka muḷiviṭṭuk kavarnteḻum māṇavamaṇikaḷum, pulavarkallūrikaḷiṟ kaḷilum maḻapulavarum, paṭṭampeṟṟa palkalikkaḻaka ārāycci yiḷiñarum, itik karuttūṉṟip paṭittu, centamiḻiṉ kūrmatinuṭpattiyum uṇarntu, tammi uṇarttuvatuṭaṉ tamiḻiyum ulakamuḻutum parappuvārāka.
innūlic cevviyāka acciṭṭuttanta, accaka urimiyāḷar tiru. mu. nārāyaṇaṉ ceṭṭiyār ākiyakaṭku, tamiḻulakaṉittum kaṭappāṭuṭiyatākum.
	cīrāyp piḻiyiṉṟic centamiḻnū lacciṭṭu
	nērāyt tarupavar nīṇilattil - ārāyntu
	pārāy peruvaḻip pārippēr accakattār
	nārā yaṇaṉceṭṭi yār.
	ettaṉi meyppēṉum innūṟ kiṭayiṉṟi
	uyttavi tāmum uṭaṉtirutti - accum
	akaṉamarn tiṭṭār avarukkuk kimmā
	ṟevaṉpuri kiṟpār emar.
kāṭṭuppāṭi,	ñā. tēvanēyaṉ
(24-2-1967)

tamiḻ varalāṟu - 1



muṉṉuri
1. varalāṟṟiṉ payaṉ
kalikaḷum (Arts) aṟiviyaṅkaḷum (Sciences) (1) taṟcār puḷḷatu (Independent), (2) maṟcārpuḷḷatu (Dependent) eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. taṟcār paṟiviyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa varalāṟu, ēṉi yaṟiviyaṅkaṭ kellām aṭimaṇiyāyum mutukantaṇ ṭāyum iruppatākum. ovvoru tuṟiyilum, toṭakkan toṭṭuvarum āciriyariṉ allatu aṟiñariṉ varalāṟṟu muṟippaṭṭa aṟivut tokutiyē oru kali allatu aṟiviyam.
kāṭciyum karuttumākiya ovvoru poruṭkum varalāṟi ruppiṉum, oru nāṭu, ataṉ makkaḷ, avarkaḷ moḻi, avarkaḷ nākarikam ākiya cilavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟē ciṟappāka varalāṟeṉappaṭum.
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu an nāṭṭiṉ paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi yum vantēṟikaḷiyum (Immigrants) pirittuk kāṭṭuvatāl, oru vīṭṭuk kāraṉukku av vīṭṭu āvaṇam ēmakkāppāvatu pōl, oru nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaṭkum an nāṭṭu varalāṟu cila vurimi vakiyil ēmakkāppām.
iṉi, varalāṟaṉṟi oru moḻiyiṉ uṇmiyāṉa iyalpi aṟivatum aritām.
2. moḻinūliṉ mutaṉmi
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu (1) eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Written 
History), (2) eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu (Unwritten History) eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nāṭāyiṉ, perum pālum eḻutappaṭṭirukkum; muṟpaṭṭatāyiṉ, eḻutap paṭṭō paṭātō irukkum. eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu, (1)aṟiyappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Known History), (2) aṟiyappaṭā varalāṟu (Unknown History) eṉa iru tiṟattatu. varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷum karuvikaḷum cāṉṟukaḷum pōtiya aḷavu iruppiṉ, aṟiyappaṭum; iṉṟēl illi. iṉi, eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟum, (1) mey varalāṟu 
(True History), poy varalāṟu (False History) eṉa iru vakittu. oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu an nāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṟṟum naṭuniliyum uḷḷavarāl eḻutappaṭiṉ perumpālum meyyāyirukkum; vēṟṟiṉap pakivarālum taṉṉiṉak koṇṭāṉmārālum (Quislings) eḻutappaṭiṉ perum pālum poyyāyirukkum. tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyaruḷ P.T. cīnivāciyaṅkārum, irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum, T.R. nrira§fhU« tavirap piṟarellārum perum pālum āriyac cārpāṉavarum naṭunili tiṟampiya varumātaliṉ, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟip perumpālum uḷḷa paṭiyum, muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟi muṟṟum tirittum eḻutiyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭatu perum pālum āriyak kalappu illatāyum, tamiḻarkkup perumi taruvatāyum, piṟpaṭṭatu āriyak kalappu uḷḷatāyum āriyarkkē perumi taruvatāyum iruppatē.
eḻutappaṭā varalāṟṟi eḻutuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭum cāṉṟukaḷ ilakkiyam, bt£blG¤J(Inscriptions) paḻam poruḷ üšfŸ(Arch-aeology) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ilakkiyattuḷ tolkāppiyamum iraṇṭoru puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ kaḷum tirukkuṟaḷum tavira maṟṟap paṇṭittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉamiyāl kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟi eḻutuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ilakkiyac cāṉṟu illātē pōyiṟṟu; itupōtuḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttukaḷ perumpālum ki.pi.4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyātaliṉ aviyum payaṉpaṭavilli. iṉi, paḻamporuṭ kaliyōveṉiṉ kaṟkālattu irumpuk kālattup parupporuṭ ceytikaḷit tavira, oḻuṅkāṉa varalāṟṟiṟkuriya nuṇkuṟippukaḷit terivikkātatā yiruppatālum, atiyum ārāytaṟkuriya mutaṟkālat tamiḻakamākiya paḻampāṇṭiya nāṭu(kumarikkaṇṭam) muḻutum muḻukip pōṉami yālum, atuvum payaṉpaṭātatē. ākavē, eñciniṟkum orē cāṉṟu moḻiyē.
tamiḻ, āriyar varukikkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarikkaṇṭattil tāṉākat tōṉṟi muḻu vaḷarcci yaṭintirunta moḻiyātaliṉ, moḻikaḷiṉ  tōṟṟam vaḷarcci tiripu uṟavu mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkum moḻinūliṉ tuṇik koṇṭē, tamiḻiṉ iyalpiyum paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟi yum oruvāṟu aṟiya muṭiyum.
3. tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam eṉpatu, mukkaḻaka (muccaṅka) varalāṟṟālum. cilappatikāra aṭiyārkku nallāruriyālum, (P.T.) cīnivāciyaṅkār, cēciyaṅkār, irāmaccantira tīṭcitar mutaliyōr eḻutiya varalāṟṟu nūlka ḷālum, eṉ mutaṟṟāy moḻi muṉṉuriyālum teḷḷat teḷiyat terintiruntum, cila tamiḻp pakivarum koṇṭāṉ mārum, tamiḻi vaṭamoḻi vaḻiya teṉak kāṭṭal vēṇṭit tamiḻar vaṭakkēyiruntu vantaṉareṉṟu pitaṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam kumarikkaṇṭamē eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:
 (1) 	tamiḻum ataṉoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa tiraviṭa moḻi kaḷum nāvalan tēyattiṟkuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkutalum; teṉ moḻi vaṭakkē cellac cellat tirintum oṭuṅkiyum ilakkiya maṟṟum iṭiyīṭu paṭṭum, teṟkē varavarat tiruntiyum virintum ilakkiya muṟṟum ceṟintum iruttalum.
 (2)	nāvalan tēyattiṟku veḷiyē tiraviṭa moḻiyiṉmiyum, mēlimoḻikaḷiluḷḷateṉ coṟkaṭkellām tamiḻilēyē vēriruttalum.
 (3) 	muḻuttūymiyuḷḷa tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭil teṉ kōṭiyil vaḻaṅkutal.
 (4) 	tamiḻnāṭṭuḷḷum tamiḻ teṟkē cellac cellat tiruntiyum ciṟantum iruttal.
	(tiruttak kalliṟkut teṟkiṭṭup piṟantavaṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkum iti uṇarttum.)
 (5) 	vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum teluṅku mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilumuḷḷa vallolikaḷ tamiḻiliṉmiyum, eṭṭum pattum paṉṉiraṇṭumāka meyyolikaḷ koṇṭa moḻikaḷ āttirēliyāvilum ataṉiyaṭutta tīvukaḷilum vaḻaṅkutalum.
 (6)	tamiḻ muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭintu muttamiḻāṉapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa talikkaḻakam, kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉ kōṭip paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkari maturiyil iruntamiyum, kumarikkaṇṭat tōṟṟattiṉ eṇṇiṟku meṭṭāt toṉmiyum.
 (7)	teṉṉamaram āttirēliyat tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟa teṉ kiḻakkut tīvukaṭkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatākac collap paṭuvatum, kumarik kaṇṭattil ēḻteṅka nāṭirun tamiyum, teṉ eṉṉuñ col teṉṉi marattiyum teṟkut ticiyiyuṅ kuṟittalum.
 (8) 	paṇṭit tamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nīrnāyum, uriyāciriyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kārōtima mum (kāraṉṉam) āttirēliyāviṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa tācu nkÅa¤(Tasmania) tīvil iṉṟumiruttal.
 (9) 	vaṇikattāl vanta iraṇṭōr ayalnāṭṭu vilaṅku kaḷum nilittiṇi (tāvara) uyirkaḷun tavira, maṟṟallāk karup poruḷkaḷum, kālavakikaḷum nilavakikaḷu mākiya mutaṟ poruḷum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappāka uriyaviyē paṇṭit  tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal.
(10)	makkaḷiṉ nākarikat toṭakkattiyuṇarttum aintiṇi makkaṭ pākupāṭum, kuṟiñci makaḷir taḻiyuṭiyum, nāṭṭāṭcikku muṟpaṭṭa ūrāṭciyum, akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭirut talum, aintiṇi nilap pākupāṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅkum aṭuttaṭuttu amin tirāmiyum.
(11)	tamiḻmakkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟkālattiliruntu teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē  toṭarntu vāḻntu vantiruttalum, avarkku vantēṟik karuttiṉmiyum.
(12)	tamiḻar piṟa nāṭṭiliruntu vantāreṉpataṟkup paṇṭit  tamiḻilakkiyattil oru cāṉṟu miṉmi.
(13)	teṉṉāṭu, teṉṉar(teṉṉāṭṭār), teṉ moḻi, teṉṟamiḻ. teṉṉavaṉ (pāṇṭiyaṉ), teṉkali eṉṉum peyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu  vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷami.
(14)	paṇṭit tamiḻar tam muṉṉōrit teṉ pulattār eṉṟaḻittamiyum; iṟanta muṉṉōriṭam teṉ pulam, teṉṉulaku eṉṟum, kūṟṟuvaṉ teṉṟicik kiḻavaṉ, teṉṟici mutalvaṉ, teṉpulakkōṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruttalum.
(15)	teṟku vaṭakkut teriyātavaṉ, teṟkum vaṭakkumāyt tirikiṉṟavaṉ, teṉvaṭal, teṉpalli, vaṭapalli (aṇikaḷ) mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷil, teṉṟici muṟkuṟikkap peṟutal.
ivaṟṟi iṉṉum viḷakkamāka aṟiya virumpuvār 
Stone Age in India, Pre-Aryan Tamil Culture, History of theTamils, Dravidian India, Pre-Historic South India, Origin and Spread of the Tamils, Tamil India mutaliya āṅkila nūlkaḷip pārkka.
pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappu
kaṭikkaḻakam kalintu pala nūṟṟaṇṭu kaṭanta piṉpum, pāṇṭiyavaraciṉ talimipōyp paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu ceṉṟa piṉpum. pāṇṭiyaṉit tamiḻnāṭaṉeṉṟu tivākaram ciṟappitta taṟkum,
nallampar nalla kuṭiyuṭittu cittaṉvāḻ(vu)
 illan toṟumūṉ ṟeriyuṭittu - nallaravap
 pāṭṭuṭittu cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ
 nāṭṭuṭittu nalla tamiḻ
eṉṉum paḻañ ceyyuṭkum ēṟpa, pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻ iṉṉum kīḻkkāṇum palavakiyiluñ ciṟantuḷḷatu.
1.	pāṇṭināṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, ciṟappākanāṭṭup puṟattiluḷḷavar, tamiḻ valliṉa meyyolikaḷit tiraviṭa āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl mika valittum eṭuttum olikkātu, paḻiya muṟippaṭiyē palukki(uccarittu) varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭacoṟkaḷi luḷḷa vallolikaḷum polivolikaḷum avar vāyil nuḻivatilli.
e-ṭu:	cākṣi - cākki, jāti - cāti
2.	coṟṟūymi pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappukaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
e-ṭu:	cikicci (va.) - paṇṭuvam (ta.)
		cuttam (va.) - tuppuravu (ta.)
pāṇṭināṭṭār cikkiḷ (cycle) eṉpati mitivaṇṭi eṉṟum, nāñcilnāṭṭārpuṉal (funnel) eṉpati vittūṟṟi eṉṟum, tūya tamiḻccollāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
3.	nī, nīr eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarkaḷiṉ muntiya vaṭivāṉa nīṉ, nīm eṉpavi, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭilēyē vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. nāmam eṉṉum tirumāliya(viṇava)k kuṟippeyariṉ mūlamāṉa irāmam eṉpatum, aṅkuttāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇ kāppu irāmavaṇakkam paṟṟi irāmam eṉṟum, kōpāla vaṇakkampaṟṟik kōpālam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kōpālam eṉpatu kōppāḷam eṉac citintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ituvum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkē.
4.	tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi, paṭiyi nāḻi eṉpatum, arippaṭiyi uri eṉpatum, kāḷiyiyum āviyum viravuppeyarāl cāttaṉ cātti yeṉṟaḻippatum, iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkām.
5.	pāṇṭināṭ ṭulakavaḻakkil vēṟeṅkum vaḻaṅkāta tūya teṉcoṟkaḷum, marapukaḷum (Idioms), vaḻakkāṟukaḷum (Usages), iṇimoḻikaḷum (Words in Pairs), toṭar moḻikaḷum (Phrases), paḻamoḻikaḷum (Proverbs) ērāḷamāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭikkaṇṇi kutirivāli mutaliya tavacaṅkaḷum karampip payaṟu, kaṟpayaṟu mutaliya payaṟuvakikaḷum, aṅkuttāṉ viḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷattal, uṇattutal, iḷavaṭṭam, ēttāppu, karaṭṭi, kāṇam, kāyal, kāmputal, kiṇṇutal, kiḷiyañ ciṭṭi, kuṭimakaṉ, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ, kuṇaṭṭutal, kutāvaṭi, kuntakkam, kentaḷippu, cavaṅkal, cavuttal, cillāṉ, ciliyōṭutal, cīyāṉ, cēṭā, takkaṉi, taṭiyam, tavval, tavattutal, tāyamāṭṭam, tikital, tuppuravu, tēri, nalavu collutal, nōṅkutal, paṇṇiyār, pattanaṭi, pataval, parital(ōṭutal), parumpu paṟamputal, pāṭupaṭutal (payiriṭutal), putuniṟam, pulli, pūṭṭaṉ, poṇṭāṉ, mayili, māṉi, mettutal, vaṭali, vatiyaḻital, vaḷḷitāy(muḻutum), vāḻkkippaṭutal, vāḻvaraci eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ nelli vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟappākum. ātalāl, tamiḻic cevviyāy aṟiyavēṇṭiṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭulakavaḻakki ārāytal vēṇṭum. iṟṟit talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyar ceṉṉit tamiḻiyum cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭut tamiḻiyumē aṟintatiṉāl, cen tamiḻip piṟaḻavuṇarntuḷḷaṉar.
tamiḻar kumarikkaṇṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpataṟkup 
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ:
(1) (teṉ)maturi
talikkaḻakam irunta iṭam teṉmaturi yeṉṉum toṉ maturiyākum.
taliccaṅkam iṭiccaṅkam kaṭiccaṅkameṉa mūvakip paṭṭa caṅkam irīiyiṉār pāṇṭiyarkaḷ. avaruḷ talic caṅka miruntār........jÄHhuhŒªjJkaṭaškoḷḷappaṭlmatuuyeṉpa”eṉpaJiṟiyaṉāraka¥poruḷuu(pa.6).
itil tamiḻvaḷarttatu eṉṟirātu `tamiḻārāyntatu' eṉṟiruppatu ūṉṟik kavaṉikkattakkatu. iṭiccaṅkamiruntār....... tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa kaṭic caṅkamiruntu tamiḻā rāyntār.... nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉpa... avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturiyeṉpa eṉṟu, iṭi kaṭik kaḻakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟē talikkaḻakamum tamiḻārāyntatākac collappaṭṭiruppatāl, talik kaḻakattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅkaḷ pēraḷavu tōṉṟi yiruntami peṟappaṭum.
(2) paḵṟuḷiyāṟu
paṉimali (imayam) pōlum kumari māmalittoṭaril tōṉṟit talikkaḻakap pāṇṭiyar talinakarākiya (teṉ) maturiyit taṉ kariyiṟkoṇṭu, kumarikkaṇṭat teṉ kōṭiyaṭutta paḻanteṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttiya, kaṅkipōlum pēriyāṟu paḵṟuḷiyām.
cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
 munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ 
 naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē	(puṟam. 9)
eṉpatu puṟappāṭṭu.
(3) kumarimali
“aṭiyil jṉṉaḷatracarkFṇartti vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpaki poṟātu
 paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
 kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
 vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
 teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi	(cilap. 11:17-22)
eṉpatu cilappatikāram. 
aṭiyil taṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti eṉpatāl, talik kaḻakak kālattilēyē oru pāṇṭiyaṉ cāvam (Java) eṉṉum cālittīvit taṉṉaṭippaṭutti, avaṉ kaṭaṟkariyilkaṭalalikaḻuvumāṟutaṉpātattippoṟittuvittāṉeṉpatum;paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟuṭaṉ.....ïkaK§ koṇṭu eṉpatāl kumarimali paṉimaliyum paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṅkiyāṟum pōṉṟaṉa eṉpatum; "vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum ... teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi'' eṉpatāl piṟkālat tup paṉimali koṇṭa karikālac cōḻaṉum kaṅkikoṇṭa irācēntirac cōḻaṉum pōṉṟu, muṟkālattup pāṇṭiya ṉoruvaṉ paṉimaliyik kumarimalik kīṭākak koṇṭu nāvalantēya muḻutum taṉ āṭciyi nāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil, kumariyōṭu vaṭavimayat torumoḻivit tulakāṇṭa cēralātaṉ1 āṭciyum, āriya nāṭṭaracōṭṭi avar muṭittali yaṇaṅkākiya pērimayak kaṟcumattip peyarntu pōntu nayantakoḷkiyiṟ kaṅkippēr yāṟṟiruntu naṅki taṉṉi nīrppaṭutti1 vanta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ ceyalum, talikkaḻaka iṭikkaḻakap pāṇṭiyariṉ pērariya vāṟṟalip peritum valiyuṟuttum.
(4) kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻnāṭukaḷ
toṭiyōḷ puvam2 eṉṉum cilappatikārat toṭarkku aṭiyārkkunallār urittuḷḷa virivuri yāvatu:
toṭiyoḷ peṇpāṟpeyarāṟ kumariyeṉpatāyiṟṟu, ākavē, teṉpāṟkaṇṇatōr āṟṟiṟkup peyarām.... mutalūḻi yiṟutikkaṇ, teṉmaturiyakattut taliccaṅkattu..nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉ patiṉmar eṇṇiṟanta paripāṭalum mutunāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyāviriyum uḷḷiṭṭavaṟṟip puṉintu terintu,nālāyirattunāṉūṟṟunāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭuirīiyiṉār,kāyciṉavaḻutimutaṟkaṭuṅkōṉīṟāyuḷḷāreṇpattoṉpatiṉmar....m¡fhy¤J, avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelliyākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉum āṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉum āṟṟiṟkum iṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvata vāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmalivāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻpiṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉi nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpat toṉpatu nāṭum kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmalināṭum, kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉ kāṟum, kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya puva meṉṟā reṉṟu ṇarka... eṉpatu.
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ  collappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭal kōḷkaḷ mottam nāṉku. avaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu talikkaḻaka irukkiyākiya teṉmaturiyik koṇṭatu; iraṇṭāvatu nākanaṉṉāṭu nāṉūṟiyōcaṉi koṇṭatu(maṇimē. 9:21); mūṉṟāvatu iṭikkaḻaka irukkiyākiya kapāṭa purattik koṇṭatu; nāṉkāvatu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiyum kumariyāṟ ṟiyuṅ koṇṭatu. kumari eṉpatu, kumarik kaṇṭattiṉ teṉ kōṭiyaṭuttirunta oru perumalit toṭarkkum, ataṉ 
1.	cilap: vāḻttukkāti, urippāṭṭumaṭi
2.   cilap. 8: 1
vaṭakōṭiyaṭuttuk kumarimuṉikkuc caṟṟutteṟkilirunta oru kāviripōlum pērāṟṟiṟkum, potuppeyarām. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam muḻukiyapiṉpum kāviriyāṟiruppatupōl, kapāṭa puram muḻukiyapiṉpum kumariyāṟiruntami vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉ kumari eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiraaṭiyālum, 
teṉāa turukeḻu kumariyiṉ teṟkum
kumariyam peruntuṟi yayiri mānti	(puṟam. 6 : 67)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. aṭiyārkku nallār uriyil taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉ kāṟum eṉpatu, akaṉṟa nīrpparappiyuṭiya kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭaperuṅ karivari, eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu. ākavē, mukkaṭal kōḷā lum muḻukkappaṭṭa tamiḻ nilapparappi yellām tokuttuk kūṟuvatu avaruriyeṉṟaṟika.
iṅṅaṉam, maṟukkavoṇṇāta teḷivāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyac cāṉṟuṇmiyāl, kumarikkaṇṭattil tamiḻa rirunta tilliyeṉṟum avar vaṭakkiruntu vantava reṉṟum, cila tamiḻp pakivarum pōlittamiḻaruṅ kūṟuvatip poruṭpaṭuttaṟka.
malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vuvaliṉ
 meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
 puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi
 valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ 	(kalit. 104)
eṉṉum kalittoki aṭikaḷum kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ perum pakutiyākiya paḻam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kaṭal koṇṭatiyum; iṭik kaḻaka virukkiyākiya alivāyiyum(kapāṭapurattiyum) kaṭal koṇṭa piṉ, ataṟkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉ nelli maturi mukavi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷākiya piṟkālap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi yāṇṭatiyum uṇarttum.
mēṟkāṭṭiya ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷāl, mutaṟkālattil nāvalantēyam muḻuvatiyum pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉē āṇṭāṉ eṉpatum kumarikkaṇṭam muḻukāta piṟkālattil, pāṇṭiyaṉ kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻ nilattiyum cēra cōḻar ataṟku vaṭak kiluḷḷa nāvalantēyap pakutiyiṉ mēlpākaṅkaḷiyum āṇṭār eṉpatum uṇarappaṭum.
tamiḻaṉiṉ piṟantakam kumarināṭeṉpati aṟiyāta mēli yaṟiñar, āriyattiyum ataṉ mutircciyāṉa camaṟkirutattiyum aṭippaṭiyāka vittārāyntu, tamiḻar naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari yiṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa muṭivu koṇṭuḷḷamiyāl iṟṟi yintiyāviluḷḷa malivāṇarellām tiraviṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷeṉṟu mayaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tiraviṭarit tōṟṟuvitta tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr kuṟiñcinilattil vāḻnta nākarikat toṭakkanili muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉ kōṭiyilēyē kaḻintuviṭṭatu. iṉṟu malivāṇarāyuḷḷa tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum cilapala nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumuṉ pōr, koḷḷi, koḷḷinōy, pañcam, kolippaḻi matat tuṉpuṟuttam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut tappi maliyēṟip piḻitta makkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉarē.
tamiḻnāṭṭil iṉṟuḷḷa malivāḻ kulattāruḷ mikap paḻamiyāṉavar, nīlamali ucciyiluḷḷa tuṭavarē. avarum, avartam muṉṉōr aṅku mutaṉmutaṟ kuṭiyēṟiyavar eṉṟē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar moḻiyum centamiḻiṉ citivāṉa oruvakik koṭuntamiḻē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali. avar erumi mantiyi vittup piḻippataṉāl, avaratu ovvoru kuṭiyirup pum mantu eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar kuṭumpatti maṉ (maṉi) eṉṟum, teyvattik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, vaḻipāṭṭu maṉiyik kōyil eṉṟum, colkiṉṟaṉar. avar moḻiyiṉ koṭun ṉmiyiyum koccit taṉmiyiyum kīḻvarum col varicikaḷāṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ nilamaṭṭam nōkki, paḷḷamāṉa tici kiḻakku(kīḻ-kīḻkku-kiḻakku) eṉṟum, mēṭāṉa tici mēṟku (mēl-mēṟku) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa. nīlamali yucciyil vāḻum tuṭavarum mēṟṟiciyi mēk(mēṟku) eṉkiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya col oṉṟiraṇṭē, avariṉ muṉṉōr kīḻiruntu ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kāṭṭavum nāṭṭavum pōtumāyiṉum, evarum eḷitil aiyantiripaṟa aṟitaṟporuṭṭup palvēṟu colvarici kaḷ kīḻk kāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṭkār taracattaṉ, aiyappaṉ mutaliyōr teṉṉāṭṭu malivāḻ vakuppārip paṟṟi eḻutiyiruppatiyum kāṇka.

4. tamiḻiṉ toṉmi
ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ epirēyattil eḻutappaṭṭa yūta aracar varalāṟṟilum nāṭpottakattilum (Chronicles) uḷḷa tuki (tōki) eṉṉuñ colli, tamiḻiṉ toṉ miyik kuṟikkum mutalilakkiyac cāṉṟāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār kālṭuvelār.
āyiṉ, ki.mu. 1200 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa āriya vētattil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ uḷḷamiyē, tamiḻiṉ toṉmikku ataṉiṉum ciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟām.
(1) cāyam
eṭu :	cāyam (sāyam) = cāyuṅkālam
	cāy - cāyum (etirkālap peyareccam)
	cāyuṅkālam = poḻutu cāyum vēḷi.
poḻutu cāykiṟatu, poḻutu cāya vantāṉ, eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakkam.
cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu, cāyaṅkālam, cāyṅkālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkil  tiriyum.
iṅṅaṉamē, cāyuntaram eṉṉum collum cāyantaram, cāyntaram eṉat tiriyum. cāytal eṉṉum col taṉittum poḻutu cāytalik kuṟikkum. ceykiyilē eṉṉum 7ām vēṟṟumi yurupēṟṟu ēkāra vīṟu peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar oru nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ cērntu, av viṉiccollāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum ceyal nikaḻum niliyi uṇarttuvatu marapu.
e.ṭu :	varacceykiyilē (varumpōtu), 
	pōkacceykiyilē (pōkumpōtu)
ic ceykiyilē eṉṉum col cilē, cē eṉṟu kocci  vaṭivuṅkoḷḷum.
e.ṭu :	varaccilē, pōkaccilē
	varaccē,   pōkaccē
ik kocci vaṭivu koṇṭa cāyaccē eṉṉum col, cāykiṟa vēḷi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōl cāykiṟaccē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa vaṭivu koṇṭa piṉ, cāyaraṭci eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum vaṭiviluḷḷa cāyam eṉṉum nili moḻiyē, cāyuṅkālam (eṟpāṭu) eṉṉum poruḷil muṉpu vaṭa tiraviṭattil vaḻaṅkip piṉpu āriya vētattiṟ pukuntu ataṉpiṉ camaṟkirutattil vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, vaṭa moḻiyāḷar iv vuṇmiyi maṟuttum maṟittum talikīḻāy māṟṟiyum sāyam eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟē cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum teṉcol vantateṉṟu tuṇintu ēmāṟṟuvar. ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakaramutaliyilum, iti vaṭacolleṉṟu kuṟittatuṭaṉ iv vēmāṟṟuk koḷkiyi valiyuṟuttavē, cāyuṅkālam, cāyuntaram eṉṉum moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam tōṉṟum vaṭivukaḷi vēṇṭumeṉṟē kāṭṭātu viṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
sāyam eṉṉum colliṟku vaṭavar poruntap poyt talākak kāṭṭum vēr muṭittaṟ poruḷiyuṇarttum sō eṉṉum col. atuvum cāy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirinta cā eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripē.
(2) tā
ic col (ilavacamākak) koṭuttal eṉṉum poruḷil, vaṭamoḻiyil jh(da) eṉṟum, ilattīṉil njh(do) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉ tamiḻp poruḷ ottōṉukkuk koṭuttal eṉpatu.
ītā koṭueṉak kiḷakkum mūṉṟum
 iraviṉ kiḷavi ākiṭaṉ uṭiya.
avaṟṟuḷ,
  īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē.
tāeṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē.
koṭueṉ kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē	(927- 30)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
putup perukku nīrik kuṟikkum veḷḷam eṉṉum col taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻuntu nīr eṉṉum potup poruḷil maliyāḷattilum, viṭi collutalik kuṟikkum ceppu eṉṉuñ col taṉ ciṟappuc poruḷiyiḻantu collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷil teluṅkilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē; ottōṉukkuk koṭuttalik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum collun taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu koṭuttal eṉṉum potup poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa teṉṟaṟika. toṉṟu toṭṭu iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varutalum, aṭippaṭiyic cērnta eḷiya collāyiruttalum, ciṟappup poruḷ koṇṭu ēṉiyiru tūya teṉcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭi miyum, tā eṉpatu tamiḻccollē eṉpataṟkut takka cāṉṟukaḷām. taravu, taruki, taraku, tattam, tāṉam ākiya coṟkaḷellām tā eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē. iṟuti yiraṇṭum vaṭa moḻiyil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālēyē vaṭa coṟpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, tā eṉṉum viṉiccol paṭarkkiyiṭattil varā teṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭiruppatum, atu tamiḻccollē yeṉpati uṇarttum.
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
 taṉmi muṉṉili āyī riṭatta. 	(tol. 512)
(3) muttu
ic col uruṇṭiyāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. itaṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya coṟkaḷ mutti, muṇṭu, muṇṭi, muṭṭu, muṭṭi, muṭṭi, muṭṭāṉ mutaliyaṉa.
muṇṭu = uruṇṭa kaṭṭi. muṇṭi - muṭṭi.
muṇṭi viḷipaḻam	 (patiṟ. 60:6)
muṇṭam = uruṇṭa kaṭṭi, ki kāl taliyillā uṭampu.
mutti = cōṟṟuruṇṭi (motti).
muṭṭu = naṭattiṟkuriya uruṇṭa paṟi.
muṭṭi = kommaṭṭi.
muṭṭāṉ = tirunīṟṟiṟkuriya cāṇavuruṇṭi, mañcaṭ kiḻaṅku.
mitukkam paḻattik kuṟumuttampaḻam  eṉpatu  karūr vaḻakku. uruṇṭu ciṟuttirukkum vitikaḷiyellām mutteṉpatu potuvāṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
ulakil muttu viḷiyum palaviṭaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu (maṉṉārkuṭā)k kaṭal.
vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
 cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
 teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
 naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭittu		(auviyār)
eṉpatu paḻañceyyuḷ. 
muttu peritāyiruppiṉ muttam eṉappaṭum. am eṉpatu oru perumipporuḷ īṟu (Augmentative suffix).
o.nō: mati = nilā. matiyam = muḻunilā
nili = tērnilipōṟ ciṟiya iṭam. niliyam= puki vaṇṭi niliyampōṟ periya iṭam
viḷakku  =  vīṭṭu viḷakkuppōṟ ciṟiyatu. viḷakkam = kalaṅ kari viḷakkampōṟ periyatu.
muttam eṉṉum teṉcolli vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mukta eṉṟu tirittu, cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutali(mukta) peṟṟateṉṟu vērp poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭa muyal vatu, kuṟumput taṉamāṉa kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) ākum. 
muttu eṉpatu, tamiḻil ciṟitāy uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum.
muttu
1. kiḷiñcilil viḷiyum maṇivaki.

uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum eṇṇeyuḷḷa vitikaḷ mutteṉṟē peyarpeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu :	āmaṇakkumuttu (muttukkoṭṭi,
	koṭṭimuttu), kurukkumuttu, vēppamuttu.
muttuccampā, muttuccōḷam eṉpaṉa uruṇṭu tiraṇṭu muttup pōliruppaṉa. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭuḷḷa kōrikkiḻaṅku muttakkācu eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.
muttukkuḷippu varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta toṉṟu toṭṭup pāṇṭi nāṭṭu maṉṉārkuṭāk kaṭalil naṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. muttūrk kūṟṟam eṉṟu ōr āḷnilap pirivum paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiliruntatu. kiṟittuviṟkumuṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muttu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāy ēṟṟumatiyāṉatum, urōm nakarap perumāṭṭiyar aḷavaṟṟa poṉṉi ataṟkuc celaviṭṭatum, varalāṟaṟinta uṇmiyākum. pāṇṭiya kavāṭam eṉṉum parumuttavaki cāṇakkiyariṉ artta cāttiram eṉṉum poruḷnūliṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. toṇ(nava) maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa muttu veṇṇiṟamuḷḷa teṉpatu, cīrkeḻu veṇmuttam (pālikkali. 9) eṉṉum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkālum, `vāytiṟantāl muttutirntu viṭumā? eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkālum aṟiyap paṭum.
iṉi, vēta āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟkuḷ (intiyā viṟkuḷ) kālvippataṟku īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, āriyam eṉṉum pērē tōṉṟuvataṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya ekipatu moḻiyilum, maṟukkamuṭiyāta tamiḻc coṟkaḷ aṭippaṭiyāyuḷḷaṉa. ākavē, mēliyulakil (tōrā. ki.mu. 5,000) mutaṉmutalāka nākarikamaṭinta ekipatu nāṭṭu moḻiyil, orucol irucol alla, pala coṟkaḷ, aviyum aṭip paṭiccoṟkaḷ, tamiḻāyiruntatē tamiḻiṉ toṉmikkut taliciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟākum.
ekipatiyat tolvaralāṟ ṟārāycciyāḷarāṉa villiyarcu öt®£L(Villiers Stuart) eṉpavar, airōppiyariṉ muṉṉōruṭ perumpālār āciyāviṉiṉṟu pāpel kªnjò(Babel Mandeb) eṉ ṉum ÚÇiz¥ò(Strits) vaḻiyāka āppirikkak kariyēṟi ettiyōppiyāvil cilakālan taṅkiyapiṉ ekipatu nāṭṭu nīlāṟṟu veḷinilattiṟkup paraviṉareṉṟum, piṉpu aṅkiruntu naṇṇilak flš(Mediterranean Sea) kaṭantu airōppāviṟ kuṭiyēṟi āriyarāy māṟiṉāreṉṟum, ekipatiyar paṉiyōli pōl eḻutu karuvi yākap payaṉpaṭuttiya gh¥ãuR(papyrus) eṉṉum tāḷ allatu tōkiyuḷḷa nāṇal mutalil apiciṉiyāvilēyē iyaṟkiyāy viḷinta teṉṟum, ekipatiyariṉ tēviyal (Sacred) uyirikaḷum 3ām 4ām 5ām āḷkuṭi (Dynasty) aracariṉ kallaṟic cuvar kaḷil variyap peṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷum apiciṉiyāviṟkum teṉ ṉāppirikkāviṟkumē yuriyaviyeṉṟum, ekipatiyak kuṟunila aracukaḷi yellām mutaṉ mutalāka oṉṟāka iṇittu ōraracu āṭci niṟuviya bkbdR(Menes) vēntaṉum teṟkattiyāṉē yeṉṟum, ekipatiya moḻi airōppiya moḻikaṭkellām mūlamāṉa mirÃiy(Monosyllabic) moḻi yeṉṟum, atu camaṟkirutattiṟku 3,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, tam ōr ekipatiya araciyiṉ paḷḷipaṭip paṭamāṭam (The Funeral Tent of An Egyptian Queen) eṉṉum tam varalāṟṟārāycci nūliṉ iṟutiyil, moḻinūl (Philology) eṉṉum talippil, takka cāṉṟu kāṭṭit tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāyk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
itiṉiṉṟu, kaṭalkōḷukkut tappiyum vēṟṟu nāṭu paraviyum ceṉṟa mutupaṇṭik kumari māntaruḷ vaṭa mēṟkākac ceṉṟa oru peru vakuppār, nrhkhÈehL(Somaliland), mãáÅah(Abyssinia) eṉṉum ettiyōppiyā, ekipatu ākiya nāṭukaḷil muṟiyē paravi, piṉpu airōppāviṟkuc ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu. ituvē, āttirēliyāviṟkum teṉṉāppirikkāviṟkum iṭippaṭ ṭirunta kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭa mēṟkāyc celvārkku ēṟṟa iyaṟkiyāṉa vaḻi eṉpati, ñālappaṭam (Atlas) nōkkik kaṇṭu koḷka.

5. tamiḻ varalāṟṟit teṟkiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkutal
intiya varalāṟṟut tantiyum intiyāviṉ muntu tuyhW' (Early History of India) eṉṉum nūliṉ āciriyarumāṉa viṉcentu áÄJ(Vincent Smith) eṉpavar, an nūṉ mukavuriyil piṉ varumāṟu varintirukkiṉṟār:
camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷ mēlum intō-āriyak karuttukaḷ mēlumāka, vaṭakkē aḷaviṟkumiñciya kālam kavaṉañ celuttap paṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu. āriya mallāta pakutiyit takuntapaṭi kavaṉip pataṟkuk kālam vantuviṭṭatu.
ip pottakam intiya araciyal varalāṟṟic curuṅkak kūṟuvataṟkē eṇṇi variyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷamiyāl, muṉ collappaṭṭa ārāycci vaḻiyip piṉpaṟṟak kūṭāta vaṉāyiruk kiṉṟēṉ. āyiṉum, mūvātu muṭivaṭinta oru peyarpeṟṟa intiyak kalvimāṉ kavaṉittaṟintavi cila, karuttāyeṇ ṇutaṟkuriya vātaliṉ, avaṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟātirukka muṭiyavilli. avi varumāṟu:
intiyā cariyāṉapaṭi teṟkiluḷḷatu

6. talikīḻārāycci
tamiḻar kumarikkaṇṭattil tōṉṟik, kaṭalkōḷ, iṉap perukkam, nāṭukāṇ viruppam mutaliya palvēṟu karaṇiyaṅ kaḷāl, vaṭakkē ceṉṟaṉar. avaruṭ palar paṉimali (imayam) vari ceṉṟu tiraviṭarāyiṉar.
vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭaruḷ orucārar, vaṭamēṟkē ettiyōp piyā, ekipatu, br¥giw¤(Cyprus) tīvu, »nu¤jh¤(Crete) tīvu, kirēkkam, ittāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷ vaḻiyāyk kāṇṭiṉēviyam vari ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟiṉar. avaruḷ oru vakuppārē, vēta āciriyarākac ciṉṉa āciyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷ vaḻiyāy intiyāviṟkuḷ (ki.mu. 2000-1500) pukuntaṉar. anniliyil, paḻiya vaṭatiraviṭam, picācam, cūracēṉam, mākatam eṉṉum mūvēṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷākat tirittiruntatu. vintiyamalikkut teṟkiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām centamiḻum koṭun tamiḻumāyiruntatiṉāl, aviyāvum tiraviṭam eṉṉum oru pirākiruta moḻiyāyk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
vēta āriyam vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉapiṉ, ataṉoṭu nāṟpirā kirutaṅkaḷiyuñ cērttamitta aricceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭimoḻiyē (Literary dialect) camaṟkirutam.
tamiḻ kumarikkaṇṭattil tōṉṟiyateṉṟu, mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟum puṟanāṉūṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya paṉuval kaḷiṉiṉṟum teḷivāy aṟiyakkiṭantum, āriyac cārpāṉa tamiḻppakivarum, tamiḻiyaṟiyāta allatu āṅkila vāyilāy arikuṟiyāyk kaṟṟa vaṭavarum mēliyarum, moḻiyārāycci yillāt tamiḻaṉparum, ākiya mut tiṟattiṉar, tamiḻar naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkariyiṉiṉṟu vantavareṉṟum, cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantava reṉṟum, maṟittum mayaṅkiyum kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ tiraviṭamākavum tiraviṭam pirākirutamākavum, āriyamum pirākirutamum tamiḻum kalantu camaṟkirutamākavum, tirintirukka, camaṟ kirutattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutamum pirākirutat tiṉiṉṟu tiraviṭam allatu tamiḻum tirintatākat talikīḻāy māṟṟik kūṟuvataṟkuk karaṇiyam, camaṟkirutam tēvamoḻiyeṉa uyarttappaṭṭatum, atiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām ataṟkē yuriyaṉaveṉṟu nampappaṭṭatum, peruvāriyāṉa āriyac coṟkaḷ pirākirutaṅkaḷil tirintu vaḻaṅkuvatum, pirākirutat teṉ coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻ vaṭiviṟkum camaṟkiruta vaṭiviṟkum iṭi nilippaṭṭiruttalumē.
``iv varuṣam īvaraṉ kirupiyāl kirāmattil cakalarum pūraṇa cukyam' eṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭilēyē vaṭacol niṟinta naṭi vaḻakkumāyiṉ, vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutattiṉ camaṟkiruta naṭi vaṇṇattic collavum vēṇṭumō? cōttam (tōtra) tiṭṭāntam (truṣṭānta) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa āriyaccoṟ citivukaḷ vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil ērāḷamākavuḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭa, vaṭṭa eṉṉum pirākirutat teṉcoṟkaḷ, kaṭṭi, vaṭṭam eṉṉum tamiḻ vaṭiviṟkum, kāṣṭa, vrutta eṉṉum camaṟkiruta vaṭiviṟkum iṭinilippaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiya palavum.
1952ām āṇṭu veḷivanta nantamōriyar kālam (Age of the Nandas and Mauryas) eṉṟa varalāṟṟup pottakattil (pak.321-2), tamiḻar nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kirēttāt tīviṉiṉṟum ciṉṉa āciyā vaḻiyāy intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu teṉṉātu vantavareṉṟum, avar moḻiyilirunta g, j, d@, d, b eṉṉum polivuḷḷa (Voiced) meykaḷ, k, c, t@, t, p eṉṉum polivilā (Voiceless) meykaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, paṇṭārakar (Dr.) cunītikumār caṭṭarci uṇmikkum uttikkum māṟāka varintuḷḷaṉar. kāytirintu piñcāṉateṉṟum, iḷavaṭṭam māṟik kuḻantiyāṉāṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē, polivoli tirintu poliviloli yāyiṟṟeṉpatu. termilar eṉṉum ōr iṉappeyar kirēttāt tīviṉiṉṟum, tiru.kaṉakacapippiḷḷi karuttuppaṭi vaṅkāya vaḻiyāc cīṉattiṉiṉṟum, avar iṅku vantaṉar4 eṉpati eṅṅaṉam uṇarttum?
oru marattiṉiṉṟu paṟikkappaṭṭa ilikaḷ vēṟor iṭattiṟ kiṭappiṉ, ammarattiṉiṉṟu avvilikaḷ vantaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatallatu. avvilikaḷiṉiṉṟu ammaram vantateṉṟu koḷvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? oru peru marattiṉ vēruntūrum aṭiyuṅkaviyum kompuṅkiḷiyum pōttuṅ kuccum kuḻiyuṅ koḻuntumpōl, ēṟattāḻa aimpatiṉāyiram

coṟkaḷ, eḷiya volippaṭṭa iyaṟkiyāṉa tōṟṟamum palvēṟu nilippaṭṭa vaḷarcciyum vērpporuṭ karaṇiyamuṅ kāṭṭi, orē toṭarpukoṇṭu varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāt toṉṟu toṭṭu it teṉṉāṭṭilēyē vaḻaṅki varavum, avaṟṟic ciṟitum ārāyātum nōkkātum, vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vantaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatu, ettuṇip poruntāk koḷkiyām!
7. kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ kāṭciyurikaḷ
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār tamiḻari allatu tiraviṭari vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vantavarākak karutiyiruntum, tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulat tiṉāl teṉmoḻiyiṉ toṉmutu muṉmiyik kaṇṭu, piṉvarumāṟu tam tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyal ilak kaṇattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār:
tiraviṭamoḻik kuṭumpatti, intō - airōppiya moḻit tokutikkum cittiya moḻit tokutikkum iṭip paṭṭa iṇippu aṇṭāka maṭṭumaṉṟi cila vakiḷil,  ciṟap pākap pakarap(patiṟ) peyar paṟṟiya vakiyil, māntaṉ moḻi varalāṟṟil intō-airōppiya nilikku muṟpaṭṭatum, cittiya nilikku muṟpaṭṭatum,  av viru kuṭumpaṅkaḷum oṉṟiṉoṉṟu pirintu pōṉataṟku muṟpaṭṭatumāṉa, oru kālattiṉ nilimi yik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷuḷ iṟavātu eñci niṟpavaṟṟuḷ taliciṟantatākak karuta iṭamilliyā?
(ceṉṉip patippu, mukavuri, pa. 10)
(2) cila tiraviṭac colvaṭivaṅkaḷum vērkaḷum, makka ḷiṉattiṉ mutaṟpeṟṟōr moḻiyiṉiṉṟu vaḻivaḻivantu intu-tiraviṭarukku moḻiyiyalurimiyākac cērntuḷḷa vaṟṟuḷ oru pakutiyāy irukkak kūṭātāveṉpatu, maṟṟoru viṉā? 
(ceṉṉip patippu, mukavuri, pa. 11)
(3) pakarappeyarkaḷ, moḻikaḷiṉ uṟaviyum moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ uṟaviyum mika viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉiṉ, mūviṭap pakarappeyarkaḷ, ciṟappākat taṉmi muṉṉili yorumip peyarkaḷ, vēṟec col vakiyiṉum mikunta nilipput tiṟaṉik kāṭṭuvaṉavākavum, pallūḻik kaṭappilum mikacciṟitē tirivatākak kāṇap paṭuvaṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa. avi eṇṇup peyarkaḷiyum vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷiyum viṉiyīṟu kaḷiyumviṭa mika nilipputtiṟaṉ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avi, vēṟetiyum pōla vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭamāṉaviyē yāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ toṭarpukaḷiyum kiḷippukaḷiyum kālattālum iṭattālum ettuṇic cēymippaṭṭiruppiṉum, ēṟattāḻa māntar moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum turuvik kāṇalām. mutaṟkaṇ orē kuṭumpat tic cērntiruntu, piṉpu kālakkaṭappiṉālum vēṟupāṭṭu vaḷarcciyiṉālum piṟapput toṭarpaṟṟupp pōṉa, cila moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpukuṟiyāka allatu  uṟavuk kūṟāka aṟiyak kūṭiya orē cāṉṟu mūviṭappakarap peyarkaḷē. ik karuttuk kuṟippu, ellāccol vakikaḷuḷḷum mika nilipput tiṟaṉuḷḷaṉavākat tōṉṟum taṉmip pakarap peyarkaḷiyē, ciṟappākat taḻuvum.
(ceṉṉip patippu, nūl, pa. 359)
(itu tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattil pakarap peyarkaḷ (Pronouns) eṉṉum pakutiyiṉ mukavuriyāka vuḷḷatu. tamiḻ mūviṭap peyarkaḷ, tirintum tiriyāmalum ēṟattāḻa ulaka moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum parantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉpati, itu kuṟippāka vuṇarttukiṉṟatu.)
(4) tiraviṭa moḻikkuṭumpam camaṟkirutattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa cila mutupaḻaṅkūṟukaḷip pōṟṟi vittirukkiṉṟa teṉṟum, ciṟappāka ataṉ cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ, camaṟkirutat tiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭāmal camaṟkirutac cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭkum intō-airōppiyak kuṭumpattic cērntu vēṟupaṭṭa piṟamoḻic cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭkum mūlamāṉa, antap paḻiya yāppettiyac cuṭṭaṭi kaḷi nikarttu niṟkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, yāṉ muṉṉamē oru karutukōḷi veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ.
(ceṉṉip patippu, mukavuri pa. 422)
(5) piṉ varuñ colvaricikaḷ, nām ilakkaṇa voppīṭ ṭiṉāl vanta muṭipiṟkē, taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil tamakkuriya aḷavil cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. am muṭipāvatu, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ, camaṟkirutattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatum intē-airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṟkut tāyākak karutappaṭuvatu māṉa anta iṟantupaṭṭa moḻiyōṭu koṇṭuḷḷa toṉmiyāṉa mika neruṅkiya toṭarpukuṟikaḷi kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē.
(ceṉṉip patippu, mukavuri pa. 565)
8. kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ kaṭuñcaṟukkalkaḷ
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār, tamiḻi āḻntu kaṟṟut tamiḻukku arum peruntoṇṭu ceyta mēliyaruḷ taliciṟanta varāyiruntum avartam ayaṉmiyālum, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiya maṉittum aḻikkappaṭṭup pōṉami yālum, iṟṟit tamiḻar ellā vakiyilum pirāmaṇarukkuk kīḻppaṭṭiruppataṉālum, tolkāppiyap payiṟciyum kaḻaka (caṅka) nūṟ kalviyum taṉit tamiḻuṇarcciyum kumarikkaṇṭak koḷ kiyum avar kālattiṉ miyālum, tamiḻarē vaṭamoḻiyit tēva moḻiyeṉa nampip pala teṉcoṟku vaṭacoṉmūlam kāṭṭi yamiyālum, tamiḻiyum tamiḻariyumpaṟṟiya pala vuṇmikaḷi aṟiyaviyalātu pōyiṉar. ātalāl, tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil palaviṭaṅkaḷil mikat tavaṟāṉa ceytikaḷik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. aviyāvaṉa:
(1) cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muttamiḻ nāṭṭuk kuṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ eṉpatu.
(2) tamiḻ nākarikam koṟkiyil toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu.
(3) tamiḻari (allatu tiraviṭari) uyarnākarikap paṭuttiyavar āriyar. āriyar varukikku muṉ, vīṭu (mōṭcam), aḷaṟu (narakam), pulampaṉ (āṉmā), karicu (pāvam) eṉpavaṟṟippaṟṟit tamiḻarukku ētēṉuṅ karuttiruntatākat teriyavilli. vaḻakkaṟiñarum naṭuvarum avarkaṭkilli. aṟivaṉum (putaṉum) kāriyum (caṉiyum) tavira, maṟṟap paḻaṅkālattārukkup potuvākat terintirunta kōḷkaḷi yellām avar aṟintiruntaṉar āyirattiṟkumēl avar kaṭku eṇṇatteriyātu, maruttuva nūlum maruttuvarum mānakarum veḷināṭṭu vaṇikamum avarkaṭkilli. ilaṅki yit tavira, vēṟentak kaṭaṟkappāli nāṭṭōṭum avarkaṭkup paḻakkamilli. tīvu allatu kaṇṭam eṉṉum karuttit teri vikkak kūṭiya col avarkaṭkilli. paṭimikkali, kaṭṭaṭak kali, vāṉanūl, kaṇiyam, paṭṭāṅku nūl tuṟikaḷ, ilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟippaṟṟi avar kaṭkut teriyātu. avarkaḷ akakkaraṇa vaḷarcciyaṭiyātirunta tiṉāl, maṉam, niṉivu, maṉaccāṉṟu, btŸî(will) ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟikka avarkaṭkuc collilli5 eṉpatu.
(4) tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkit taḻuviyamintatu. tamiḻar vaṭaveḻuttukaḷuḷ vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi
5.	ip pakutiyil vantuḷḷa taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ āciriyaṉ kuṟittavi. ātaṉ, 
uṟavi eṉpaṉavum pulampaṉik kuṟikkum tamiḻc coṟkaḷām.
maṭṭum eṭuttukkoṇṭu, vēṇṭātavaṟṟi viṭṭuviṭṭaṉar, eṉpatu.
(5) tamiḻppeyarccolliṉ vēṟṟumiyamippu muṟṟum camaṟkiruta vēṟṟumiyamippip piṉpaṟṟiyatu eṉpatu.
(6) tamiḻil cariyāṉapaṭi, ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyē illi eṉpatu.
(7) aracaṉ, āyiram, ulakam, kaṇiyam, cēraṉ, cōḻaṉ, tiru, nāḻi, pāṇṭiyaṉ, maṉam, mātam eṉpaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉpatu.
iṉi, kālṭuvelār cila pulaṉaṅkaḷi (viṣayaṅkaḷi) muṉṉukkup piṉ muraṇākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. e-ṭu : maliyāḷam tamiḻiṉ kiḷimoḻi (oldest offshoot) eṉa ōriṭattum, uṭaṉ piṟappu moḻi (sister dialect) eṉa maṟṟōri ṭattum kūṟiyiruttal.
mēṟkāṭṭiya eḻuvakic caṟukkaṟkūṟṟukaḷum uṇmikku māṟāṉaviyeṉpatu, in nūlālum itiyaṭuttu varum ēṉiya nūlkaḷālum teṟṟeṉat teriya varum. tamiḻik kāytaliṉṟi mutaṉmutal moḻinūṉ muṟiyil ārāyntu ōraḷavu perumip paṭuttiya kālṭu velārē iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyiruppataṉālum, ivar karuttē ulaka muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷa āṅkilak kalik kaḷañ ciyattil iṭampeṟṟiruppataṉālum, ayalārum veḷināṭ ṭārum tamiḻiṉ perumiyi aṟiyamuṭiyātu pōyiṟṟu. ivar nūlmuḻutum nōkkiṉ, tamiḻ nākarikattiṟku āriyam aṭippaṭi eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa karuttu, ivar uḷḷattil ēṟkeṉavē āḻa vērūṉṟiyiruntatik kāṇalām, camaṟkirutattil tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ uḷa eṉa mutaṉmutal ivarkkuk kaṇtiṟantavar, maliyāḷa moḻiyārāyc ciyil īṭum eṭuppumaṟṟa kuṇṭarṭṭu eṉṉum cerumāṉiyak kiṟittavak kuravarē. āyiṉum kālṭuvelār pakimik kaṇ koṇṭu tamiḻi nōkkiyavarallar eṉpatu, aṉivarkkum oppu muṭinta uṇmiyām. eṉiṉum, avar ārāycciyiṉ kuṇamuṅ kuṟṟamum nāṭiṉ, kuṟṟamē mikunta teṉpatu teḷivu. ātalāl aḵtu ekkālattiṟkum ēṟkumeṉpatu aṟiyār kūṟṟē. atu iṉṟu paḻamippaṭṭum paḻutupaṭṭum pōyiṟṟeṉpatu in nūlāl viḷaṅkum.
9. tiraviṭamoḻip pakuppu
teṉṉintiya moḻikkuṭumpattik kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇi yār mutaṉ mutalāka ārāyntatiṉāl, tamiḻiyum ataṉoṭu toṭar puḷḷa piṟa moḻikaḷiyum vēṟu paṭuttātu, tiraviṭam eṉṉum orē peyarāṟ kuṟittār. āyiṉ, iṉṟu teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷa nāṭukaḷ vevvēṟu pirintu pōṉamiyālum, tamiḻ oṉṟē āriyatti etirttut taṉ tūymiyip pōṟṟik koḷḷum āṟṟal peṟṟuḷḷamiyālum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ cērac cērat teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷattiṟkum, tīrattīrat tamiḻukkum uyarvu ēṟpaṭuvataṉālum, intiyum vaṭamoḻiyum paṟṟiya koḷkiyil tamiḻarkkum avartam iṉamoḻiyāḷarkkum nērmāṟāṉa karuttuṇmiyālum, teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya iṉamoḻikaḷellām mīḷat tamiḻoṭu cēra muṭiyā vaḷavu āriyavaṇṇamāy māṟiviṭṭamiyālum, ataṉāl iṉamoḻi kaḷiyum puṟak kaṇikkum nili tamiḻukku ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭataṉālum, tamiḻit tamiḻ eṉṟum, ataṉ iṉamoḻikaḷiyē tiraviṭam eṉṟum, pirittuk kūṟal vēṇṭum. avviru kūṟukaḷiyum iṇittuk kuṟippataṟkut teṉmoḻi eṉṉum colli āḷavēṇṭum. ivaṟṟiṟku āṅkilattil muṟiyē, Tamil, Dravidian, Tamilic or South Indian eṉṉum coṟkaḷi āḷalām.
tamiḻ eṉṉum col tiraviṭam eṉṟu tirintatu pōṉṟē, tamiḻam eṉṉum moḻiyum pala tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākat tirin tiruppataṉāl, tiraviṭam eṉṉum collāl tiraviṭa moḻi kaḷik kuṟippatu takkatē. vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum peyar pala vaṭa nāṭṭu moḻikaḷiyum āriyattuḷ aṭakkuvatupōl, teṉ moḻi eṉṉum peyarum  pala teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷiyum tamiḻuḷ aṭakkum.
âô¤jhÅa«(Teutonic) eṉṉum orē vakuppic cērnta cerumāṉiyam, j¢r«(Dutch), āṅkilam mutaliya moḻikaḷ eṅṅaṉam tirumpa oṉṟu cērāvō, aṅṅaṉamē teṉ moḻiyam eṉṉum vakuppic cērnta tamiḻum tiraviṭamum oṉṟucērā. pāl tirintu tayirāṉapiṉ mīḷap pālākātatu pōl, tamiḻ tirintu tiraviṭamāṉa piṉ mīḷat tamiḻākātu. iṅku moḻikaṭkuc coṉṉatu nāṭukaṭkum okkum.
tiraviṭam eṉṉuñ col mutalāvatu tamiḻiyē kuṟitta teṉṟum, nālāyirat teyvap paṉuval tiraviṭa vētameṉṟum meykaṇṭāṉ nūlukkuc civañāṉa muṉivar varinta akalavuri tirāviṭa māpāṭiyam eṉṟum peyar peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa veṉṟum, taṉit tamiḻārāycci yillāta paṇṭi nilimiyik kūṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭiyum pirintu pōṉa tiraviṭa nāṭukaḷiyum oṉṟāy iṇikka muyalpavar, kaṟanta pālik kāmpiṟ kēṟṟupavarēyāvar. āyiṉ araciyaṟ koḷki yoppumipaṟṟi, tamiḻnāṭum tirāviṭa nāṭukaḷum oru kūṭṭāṭci amikkalām.
ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē, kumarilapaṭṭar āntira - tirāviṭa pāṣā eṉṟu teluṅkit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pirittuk kūṟiviṭṭār. kaṉṉaṭamum maliyāḷamum ataṉpiṉpē tamiḻi ṉiṉṟu pirintaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭum āntira micūr kēraḷamākiya mut tiraviṭa nāṭukaḷum aṇmiyiltāṉ pirintu pōyiṉa. avi mīḷavum oṉṟucērum nilimiyiruppiṉ, muṉṉarp pirintē pōyirā. mēlum.

eṉṟu pērā. cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kūṟiyatu, paḻantamiḻip poṟuttavariyil uyarvunaviṟciyā yirātu uṇmi naviṟciyā yiruppatāl, tamiḻiyum ataṉ iṉa moḻikaḷiyum vēṟu pirittuk kūṟutaṟku maṟṟumoru karaṇiyamuḷḷatu. paḻantamiḻukkum iṟṟit tamiḻukkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu mikac ciṟitē. ilattīṉukkum ittāliyam, pireñciyam, icupāṉiyam, pōrttukkīciyam mutaliya moḻikaṭkum iṭippaṭṭa uṟavē, tamiḻukkum teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkum iṭippaṭṭa tākum.
tamiḻallāta oru moḻi tiraviṭattāyākat tamiḻukku muṟpaṭṭu iruntatēyilli. moḻittuṟikkuc coṉṉatē iṉattuṟikkum. tiraviṭa muṉṉiyar (Pre-Dravidians”) eṉṟō, mūlattiraviṭar (Protp-Dravidians) eṉṟō, tiraviṭar (Dravidians) eṉṟō vēṟupāṭiruntatāka namakkut tōṉṟavilli. ivarka ḷellām putukkaṟkālattiṉiṉṟum ataṟku muṉpiruntum vanta makkaḷiṉ nērvaḻit tōṉṟiya ōriṉattārē. eṉṟu irāmaccantira tīṭcitar kūṟiyiruttaliyum kāṇka. (Pre-Historic  South India, p. 246)
10. tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar varalāṟu
tamiḻ kumarikkaṇṭattiltāṉē tōṉṟiya moḻiyāta lāṉum, āriyar varumvari vēṟṟumoḻi yoṉṟum teṉṉāṭ ṭil evvakiyilēṉum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum, tamiḻavaṇikar moḻipeyar nāṭu cellumvari allatu ayalāroṭu araciyal allatu vaṇikat toṭarpu koḷḷum vari, moḻi allatu pēccu eṉṉum potup peyar tavira yātoru ciṟappuppeyarum tamiḻukku vaḻaṅkiyirukka muṭiyātu.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ciṟappup peyar, tamiḻ (tamiḻam), tiraviṭam eṉṉum iruvaṭivil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv viraṇṭum vevvēṟu coṟpōl tōṉṟiṉum uṇmiyil orē colliṉ iruvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyatu tamiḻ eṉpatē. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:
(1)	tirāviṭam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu tamiḻ eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkiyami
vaṭanūlār tamiḻi oru pirākirutamākak koṇṭa pōtu, atit trāviṭī eṉa aḻittaṉar. 
pākavata purāṇattil, cattiyavirataṉ eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum oru tamiḻaracaṉ, tiraviṭapati eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.
ki.pi. 470ām āṇṭil vaccirananti eṉṉum camaṇar maturiyil niṟuviya tamiḻk kaḻakam' tiraviṭa caṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu.
piḷḷi lōkācārya cīyar (500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ) periya tirumoḻic ciṟappup pāyiravuriyil, tamiḻilak kaṇattit tiraviṭa cāstiram eṉak kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟār.
nālāyirat teyvap paṉuval tiraviṭavētam eṉṟum, civañāṉa muṉivariṉ meykaṇṭāṉ nūl akalavuri tiraviṭa māpāṭiyam eṉṟum, capāpati nāvalariṉ tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟu tirāviṭap pirakāciki eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tāyumāṉavar (18ām nūṟ.) kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ eṉṉum pāṭṭil, tamiḻit tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
kiṟittuviṟku muṉ tamiḻilaṉṟit tiraviṭamoḻi yetilum ilakkiyamiṉmiyāl, tiraviṭam eṉṉum peyarāl tamiḻiyē talimiyākak kuṟittu vantaṉar, vaṭavar. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil tāṉ, kumarilapaṭṭar āntira-tirāviṭa pāṣā eṉṉun toṭarāl teluṅkit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pirittuk kūṟiṉar.
tiraviṭam eṉṉum tirinta vaṭivic cemmiyāṉa vaṭivākak koṇṭu, āriyattāl teṟkē turattappaṭṭatu eṉṟu tamiḻp pakivarum, tīviṉiyit turattuvatu eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivarum, tiruviṭam (tiru+iṭam) eṉṟu pērā. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷiyum turūvitarōṭu toṭarpu paṭutti ñāṉappirakācak kuravarum, vevvēṟu poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
(2)	tamiḻ eṉṉum colliyotta peyarkaḷē veḷi nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiyami

nōkkum pōtu, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum ciṟupāṉmi tamiḻam eṉṟu vaḻaṅ kiyatākak karuta iṭam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻam, tramila(m) tramiḷa( m), tramiṭa(m), traviṭa(m) eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷi muṟiyē nōkkiṉ, tamiḻ eṉpataṉ tiripē tiraviṭam eṉpatu pulaṉām. āyiṉum kālṭuvelār iv veḷiya muṟiyil uṇmiyik kāṇāmal, iyaṟkikku māṟākat talikīḻāy nōkki, tiraviṭam eṉṉuñ collē tamiḻeṉṟu tirintatāka muṭivu ceytuviṭṭār. āyiṉ, kiriyarcaṉ it tavaṟṟit tiruttiviṭṭaṉar.
tramiḷam eṉpatu tamiḻ eṉat tiritalum atu eṉṟu pirayōka vivēka nūlār (pa.4) kūṟiyatu, avaratu tamiḻ veṟuppik kāṭṭum cāṉṟēyeṉa aṟika.
(4)	tirāviṭam eṉṉum vaṭivam tamiḻnāṭṭulaka vaḻakkil arukiyum vaḻaṅkāmi
(5)	tramiṭam, traviṭam eṉṉum rakaramēṟṟiya vaṭivukaḷē yaṉṟi,  tapiḷa, taviṭa eṉṉum mika neruṅkiya vaṭivu kaḷum vaṭamoḻi nāṭakaṅkaḷilum camaṇa nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkiyiruttal
iṉi, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ col, kirēkka nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya termilē allatu termilar allatu `tiramilar' eṉṉum  iṉap peyariṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu ñāṉappirakācak kuravarum, vaṅkanāṭṭut tamralitti eṉṉum nakarp peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya teṉṟu kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyum; tāmam(ñāyiṟu), ellām (ilaṅki) eṉṉum iru col, muṟiyē tām īḻam eṉa maruvip puṇarnta vaṭiveṉṟu kantiyāp piḷḷiyum; tami eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭiyāyp piṟantu oppaṟṟateṉap poruḷ paṭuvateṉṟu tāmōtaram piḷḷiyum, taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭa moḻi eṉṉum poruḷ taruvateṉṟu oru cārārum, vali meliyiṭiyākiya mūviṉa meykaḷik koṇṭa nilimiyik kuṟippateṉṟu maṟṟoru cārārum, ataṟkup poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar. iṉimiyāṉatu eṉṟu poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭu vāruḷ, (S) cīnivāciyaṅkār oruvarē tam+iḻ eṉṟu pakuttu iḻ eṉṉum īṟu iṉimip poruḷpaṭum iḻum eṉṉum colliṉ citiveṉak kāṭṭuvar. piṟarellām, iṉimiyu nīrmiyun tamiḻeṉa lākum eṉṉum piṅkalanti nūṟpāvit taḻuvi, tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟkē iṉimip poruḷuṇṭeṉak koḷvar.
kirēkka nāṭṭil, eṉṟēṉum tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya teṉ pataṟkō tamiḻar vāḻntiruntār eṉpataṟkō oru cāṉṟu miṉmiyāṉum; tamiḻccoṟkaḷum tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum, kirēkka nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakattiṟ pala iṉattāriṭiyum kāṇappaṭuvatālum, kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum intiyāviṟkum iṭiyil oru tiraviṭa moḻiyum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum; termila eṉṉum collit tira-il-ar eṉṟu pirittu, kaṭaṟkariyil kuṭikoṇṭavar eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu, kēcavakkiruṭṭiṇaṉē ēcukkiṟittu eṉṟu colvatokku mātalāṉum; orē colliṉ orupuṭiyop pumipaṟṟi oru nāṭṭāri aiyāyiram kal toliviṟkappāṟ paṭṭa vēṟoru nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavarākak koḷvatu, moḻinūṉ muṟikku muṟṟum muraṇāṉatāka lāṉum; tōṟṟam mutal ituvarippaṭṭa vaḷarcci niliyellām toṭarpākak kāṭṭikkoṇṭu toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē tamiḻ vaḻaṅki vantirutta lāṉum; kirēkka nāṭṭu mūlak koḷki mikat tavaṟāṉa teṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
vaṅka nāṭṭil tiraviṭarēyaṉṟit tamiḻar orukālum vāḻn tirāmiyāṉum, iṟṟi vaṅkamoḻi āriya vaṇṇamāy māṟi yiruttalāṉum, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkut tamralitti eṉṉuñ collōṭuḷḷa toṭarpu, kāci eṉṉum peyarkkuk kāñci yeṉṉum peyarōṭuḷḷa toṭarpē yātalāṉum; tamralitti (allatu tamilapti allatu tamilūk) eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar vantateṉpatu ciṟitum poruntātu.
tamiḻar nākarikamaṭinta kālantoṭṭut tamiḻakam cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarāṭcik kuṭpaṭṭa muttamiḻnāṭāyiruntu vantami yāṉum, ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tolkāppiyarum,
vaṇṭamiḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
 nāṟpeyar elli yakam 	(tol. 1336)
eṉṟu kūṟutalāṉum, varalāṟṟuk kālam neṭukalum ilaṅki ayalārāṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa oru kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭākavē karutap paṭṭu varutalāṉum; tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē tōṉṟi viṭṭamiyāṉum; tāmamellām allatu tāmīḻam eṉpatu ilaṅkiyi nōkkip putuvatākap paṭitta poruntāp puṇarppātalālum; ap puṇarppuc colliṉiṉṟu tamiḻeṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, nāṭṭuppaṟṟiṉā leḻunta viruppak karuttēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟa teṉṉuṅ karuttu ēṉiya moḻika ḷellān tōṉṟiyapiṉ, avaṟṟōṭu tamiḻi oppunōkki ataṉ uyarvu kaṇṭu ataṟkut tamiḻ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉa reṉṟu,  ap peyarīṭṭi mikap pintiya nikaḻcciyākak kāṭṭuvatāl, atuvum koḷḷat takkataṉṟu.
mūviṉamey ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvātaliṉ, tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar atu kuṟittut tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatum tavaṟāṉatē.
ellā moḻiyāḷarum tattam moḻiyē iṉiya teṉṟu koḷvatu iyalpātalāṉum; māṭu eṉṉum coṟkuc celvam eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiyatupōl, tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟku iṉimi eṉpatu matippum paṟṟum paṟṟit tōṉṟiya vaḻip poruḷē yātalāṉum; tam+im(iḻum) eṉṉum coṟ pakuppil, tam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu toṭarpaṟṟum, iḻum eṉṉum talimic col īṟākak kuṉṟiyum, irukkavēṇṭum nilimi yēṟpaṭutalāṉum; iṉimip poruṭ karaṇiyamum ēṟṟataṉṟām.
iḻum = iṉimi.  iḻumeṉal = iṉitāyiruttal.
iḻutu = tēṉ, tittippu.
taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉuṅ karuttup poruttamuḷḷatākat tōṉṟiṉum, atuvum ēṉi moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ oppu nōkkutali vēṇṭutaliṉ ēṟkattakka taṉṟu. ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṉṉarē kumarikkaṇṭattil tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu.
tamiḻ eṉṉum collil ḻ iyalpīṟaṉṟu. amiḻ, imiḻ, umiḻ, kumiḻ, cimiḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl, tamiḻ eṉpatilum iḻ eṉpatē īṟām. atu il eṉpataṉ tiripu. taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉum poruḷil, tamiḻ eṉpatu tami+ḻ eṉṟu pirital vēṇṭum. tami = taṉimi. ḻakaram iṉitāyolippa tāl, taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉuṅ kūṟṟum, iṉimik karuttit taḻuviyatē.
iṉi, iṉimiyeṉpatu tamiḻukku ḻakarattāl maṭṭum ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu. eḻuttiṉimi pōṉṟē, colliṉimi, ceyyu ḷiṉimi eṉap piṟaviṉimikaḷumuṇṭu. ivaṟṟiṉ viriviyum viḷakkattiyum eṉ centamiḻc ciṟappu', muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cintiyuḷḷum

eṉṉum tirukkōvic ceyyuḷil, `oṇṭīntamiḻiṉ tuṟi' eṉṟu piṟamoḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilakkaṇamum, ``ēḻicic cūḻal'' eṉṟu icit tamiḻum, iṉpat tuṟikaḷāka māṇikkavācakarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttaliyum nōkkit teḷika.
aṇmiyil tamiḻppērāciriyar oruvar 7, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ collit ta+miḻ eṉṟu pirittum, tam+miḻaṟṟu eṉṟu virittum, namatu iṉiyamoḻi eṉṟu poruḷ kuṟittum, āṅkilat til oru kaṭṭuri varintuḷḷār.
muṟiyē, ēm (yām), nām, nūm, tām eṉṉum mūviṭap peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷāṉa em, nam, num, tam eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ, em, nam eṉṉum taṉmic coṟkaḷē arumip pāṭṭi yuṇarttum. itu ārvak kōḷi (Dative of Interest) eṉṉum āṅkila vilakkaṇa amitiyi orupuṭi yottatu.

7.	vē.va. irāmacāmi pārāṭṭu' malar, pak.93-5
karunaṭam(kaṉṉaṭam), kuṭaku mutaliyavi nāṭupaṟṟiyum; mali yāḷam, āṅkilam mutaliyavi makkaḷ paṟṟiyum; pirākirutam, camaṟkirutam mutaliyavi taṉmi paṟṟiyum peyarpeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻi, teṉmoḻi eṉat tici paṟṟiyum; urutu(pāḷiyam) eṉa iṭam paṟṟiyum, peyar peṟuvatu nāṭṭiṉāṟ peyarpeṟuvatu pōṉṟatē. vaḻakkaṟṟa moḻiyāyiṉ, vētamoḻi eṉpatupōl nūlālum peyar peṟum. tēvamoḻi eṉpatu ēmāṟṟup paṟṟiyatām.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
 āyiṭit
 tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattu
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu, ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkil tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavilli yeṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālatti lum innilimiyē yiruntami, kaḻakac ceyyuḷkaḷālum kaḻaka maruviya vaṉappukaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. tirāviṭam (tamiḻ), āntiram, kaṉṉaṭam, makārāṭṭi, kūrccaram eṉṉum aintiyum pañca tirāviṭam eṉṟu paṇṭikkālattil vaṭavar vaḻaṅkiya tāl āriyar vanta piṉpum vintiyamalivariyum tamiḻum ataṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭamumē vaḻaṅkiyami peṟappaṭum. avar varukik kālat tilō, vaṭintiyāvilum tiraviṭam vaḻaṅkiyati, pirākuvīyum, irācamakālum iṉṟum kāṭṭum.
oru nāṭṭil orē moḻi vaḻaṅkumāyiṉ, ataṟkuc ciṟappup peyar tōṉṟātu. pēccu allatu moḻi eṉṉum potuppeyarē ataṟku vaḻaṅkum. ōr ūril orē ōr āṟiruppiṉ, ati āṟeṉṟu potuppeyarālēyē kuṟippar. iṅṅaṉamē mali, kuḷam, maram mutaliya piṟavum oṉṟēyoṉṟā yiruppiṉ potuppeyarālēyē kuṟikkap peṟum. oru moḻi āyiraṅkal toliviṟku appāṟ paṭariṉ, palvēṟu karaṇiyampaṟṟit tirital iyalpu. at tiripu tiṭutippeṉṟu tōṉṟātu mella mellap paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟum. atu moḻi peyartal eṉappaṭum. toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻiyaṭuttu vaṭapāl vaḻaṅkum tiraviṭa moḻi teluṅkē. ataṉāl, atit tamiḻar vaṭaku eṉṟaṉar. atu piṉpu uyiricivu kh‰w¤jhš(Harmonic Sequence of Vowels) vaṭuku eṉat tirintatu. nīlamaliyil vāḻum orucār kaṉṉaṭat tiraviṭarik kuṟikkum paṭakar eṉṉum peyar, vaṭakar eṉpataṉ tiripē. teluṅki yaṭuttut tirinta perun tiraviṭamoḻi kaṉṉaṭamē.
tamiḻa vaṇikar vaṭakkiluḷḷa moḻipeyar nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟirunta pōtu, an nāṭṭār av vaṇikar pēccit tammil (tam+il) moḻi eṉṟu kuṟittirukkalām. il eṉpatu vīṭṭi yuṅ kuṭiyiyum ūriyum uṇarttum. iṟpiṟantār (kuṟaḷ. 915) = kuṭippiṟantār.
aṉpil, kiṭaṅkil, poruntil eṉpaṉa ūrp peyarkaḷ. ūr, nāṭu eṉpaṉa oṉṟiyoṉṟuṇarttalu muṇṭu. nāṭṭāṇ mikkāraṉ eṉpavaṉ ūrāṇmik kāraṉ. āḷmaṟināṭu, urattanāṭu, piṅkānāṭu eṉpaṉa ūrp peyarkaḷ.
tam il moḻiyāvatu tam vīṭṭil allatu nāṭṭil pēcum moḻi. tammil eṉpatu tamil eṉat tokkut tamiḻ eṉat tirintirukkalām. veyyil eṉpatu veyil eṉat tokku vaḻaṅkutali nōkkuka. tamiḻ eṉṉum vaṭivu orucoṟṟaṉmip paṭṭu, tamiḻ eṉṉum tiripil moḻip peyart taṉmi muṟṟi viṭṭatu.

eṉṉum kaṭikkaḻakac ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ. tamiḻa vaṇikariṉ vaṭatici moḻipeyar tēyac celavik kūṟutal kāṇka. teluṅkeṉṉum vaṭuku mutaṟkālattil tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum mika vēṟupaṭṭirukkamuṭiyātu. vēṅkaṭat tumpar moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟatu vaṭukanāṭṭiyē eṉpatu teḷivuṟu tēṟṟam. tām eṉpatu tāmu eṉṟum, tam eṉpatu tama eṉṟum, tampiṉ eṉpatu tammuṭu eṉṟum, il eṉpatu illu eṉṟum, iṉṟum teluṅkil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, tammil allatu tamil eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col akkāla vaṭukiṟku muṟṟum iyalpāṉatē.
alarmēlmaṅki eṉṉum toṭarccol piṉṉar alarmēl eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkuvatupōl, tamilmoḻi eṉpatum nāḷaṭivil tamil eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. lakaratti ṉiṉṟē ḷakaramum ḷakarattiṉiṉṟē ḻakaramum tōṉṟi yiruttalāl, lakaram nēraṭiyākavō ḷakara vāyilākavō ḻakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭu.

	maṉiviyi viḷikkum col), kal - kaḷ - kāḷ -

iṉi, taṉmāṉam tamar eṉṉum coṟkaḷil, taṉ tam eṉpaṉa paṭarkki cuṭṭātu conta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal pōl, tamil eṉṉum collilum tam eṉpatu conta eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumāṟu, tamiḻarē ap peyarit tam moḻikku iṭṭukkoṇ ṭaṉar eṉak koḷḷalum oṉṟu. cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu cāyaṅkālam eṉṟu tirintapiṉ, cāyam eṉṉum peyareccamē vaṭamoḻiyil cāyuṅ kālattik kuṟittal pōl; tamil eṉṉum peyaraṭi peyart taṉmippaṭṭu moḻiyik kuṟittateṉka. t.saha«-ï.sah«.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻ eṉṉum peyarukkuk kūṟappaṭṭa poruṭkaraṇiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, taṉiyāka ḻakarattiyuṭiyatu, tannāṭṭu moḻi, eṉṉum iraṇṭē poruttamāṉavi yeṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉum, ciṟantatu tōṉṟum vari iviyē koḷḷat takkaṉa veṉṟum eṇṇik koḷka.
vaṭuku koṭuntamiḻnili kaṭantu moḻinili yaṭinta piṉ, vaṭukar(teluṅkar) tamiḻukkiṭṭa peyar aruvam eṉpatu. atu aravam eṉat tirintatu. centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭeṉat tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  piṟkālattil centamiḻ nilapparappu mikac curuṅkiviṭṭa taṉāl, ataṟkēṟpak koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷum paṇṭic centamiḻ nilattiṟ kuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. an nilimiyiyē,

 cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr

eṉṉu miṭikkāla veṇpāk kāṭṭum.
`aruvā ataṉ vaṭakku' eṉṟatu, aruvā nāṭṭiyum aruvā vaṭatali nāṭṭiyum. ivi tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa kōṭiyil teluṅka nāṭṭi aṭuttiruntaṉa. ataṉāl nāṭupaṟṟit tamiḻari aruvar eṉa aḻittaṉar teluṅkar. ellippuṟap pakutip peyari nāṭu muḻuvataṟkum iṭuvatu ayalār iyalpu. cintuveḷi paṟṟi nāvalan tēyattip pāracīkar hintu eṉṟum kirēkkar intōs eṉṟum kuṟittatiyum; mukalāya aracar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu tamiḻnāṭṭiyuñ cērttuk karu nāṭakam eṉa aḻittati yum, nōkkuka.
