karunaṭar (kaṉṉaṭar) tamiḻar eṉṉuñ collit tikuḷar eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkuvar. vaṭamoḻi teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉ, tamiḻi ataṉoṭu oppunōkkit teṉmoḻi eṉṟaṉar.
tiraviṭam, eṉpatu tamiḻam eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu muṉṉarē viḷakkappeṟṟatu. tiraviṭam, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, tiraviṭa moḻika ḷellāvaṟṟiyum taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik koṇ ṭirunta tamiḻiṉ paṇṭit talimiyi uṇarttum.
11. mūvēntar peyar
talik kaḻakak kālattiṟku muṉpē, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar nāvalam poḻil eṉṉum intu tēyatti āḷat toṭaṅki viṭṭataṉāl, am mūvēntar kuṭit tōṟṟamum varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmittākum. itaṉālēyē, tirukkuṟaḷi vaṭanūl vaḻiyākak kāṭṭiya parimēlaḻakarum, `paḻaṅkuṭi' eṉpataṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu varukiṉṟa kuṭi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, toṉṟutoṭṭu varutal cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya reṉṟāṟ pōlap paṭippuk kālan toṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varutal eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.
mutaṟkālattil nāvalam poḻil muḻutum mūvēntar āṭcik kuṭpaṭṭiruntami,
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk 
 kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
 vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamum koṇṭu
 teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi! 
(cilap. nāṭukāṇ kāti, 19-22)
irunila maruṅkiṟ porunarip peṟāac
 ceruveṅ kātaliṉ tirumā vaḷavaṉ
 ............................   
 puṇṇiyat ticimukam pōkiya annāḷ
 acivil ūkkattu nacipiṟak koḻiyap
 pakivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu maliyeṉa
 imiyavar uṟiyuñ cimiyap piṭarttalik
 koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkiyiṟ peyarvōṉ
(cilap. intira viḻavūreṭutta kāti, 88-97)
kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayattu orumoḻi vittu ulakāṇṭa cēralātaṟkut tikaḻoḷi ñāyiṟṟuc cōḻaṉ makaḷ īṉṟa mintaṉ koṅkar ceṅkaḷam vēṭṭuk kaṅkip pēryāṟṟuk kari pōkiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
(cilap. vāḻttuk kāti, urippāṭṭu maṭi)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. āriyar varumuṉ vaṭanāṭu muḻutum tiraviṭar paraviyiruntamiyum, cila vaṭanāṭṭūrp peyar tamiḻāyiruttalum iti valiyuṟuttam.
teṟkē muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam muḻutum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭē. atu ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram kal tolivu nīṇṭatu. itēyaḷavu vaṭakkil kīḻppakuti cōḻanāṭākavum mēṟpakuti cēranāṭākavum iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaṇṇaṉ aḻitta tākac collappaṭum cōṇitapuram paḻañ cēranāṭṭic cērntatē. atu māvali eṉṉum cēra māvēntaṉiṉ makaṉ vāṇaṉatu talinakar.
tamiḻa vēntar eṉṟum mūvarēyātaliṉ, avar muc cuṭariyum tam kulamutalākak kūṟikkoṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ tiṅkaṭkulamum, cōḻaṉ katiravak kulamum, cēraṉ neruppuk kulamum āvar. pāratattiṟ collappaṭum, tiṅkaḷ marapākiya parata kulam paḻam pāṇṭiyak kiḷiyum, irāmāyaṇattiṟ collappaṭum katiravak kulam(cūriya vamicam) paḻañ cōḻak kiḷiyum ākum. mucukuntaṉ, māntātā, cipi mutaliyōr teṉṉāṭṭuc cōḻar kuṭikkum vaṭanāṭṭuk katiravak kulattiṟkum potu muṉṉōrākac collap paṭutal, ivviru kuṭikaḷum orē kulattic cērntamiyālēyē. cipiyiṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉ ātalālēyē cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ. piṟkālattut teluṅkac cōṭar(cōḻar) moḻipaṟṟip pirintu pōṉatu pōṉṟē, muṟkālattuk katiravakkula vaṭavaracarum moḻiyum cēymiyumpaṟṟip pirintu pōyiṉar eṉka.
nārataṉi mūvulaku mulāvi(tirilōka cañcāri) eṉṟatu cēra cōḻapāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷpaṟṟiyē. ulakam eṉpatu mutalāku peyarāy nāṭṭiyum kuṟikkum.
ittuṇip paḻimi vāynta cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyak kuṭip  peyarkaḷ, tamiḻāyaṉṟi vēṟu moḻiyāyiruttal muṭi yātu. āyiṉum, āriya veṟiyar intiya nākarikatti āriyamākak kāṭṭalvēṇṭi, mūvēntar kuṭippeyariyum vaṭanāṭṭut toṭarpukoṇṭa vaṭacollākak kāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatu mūvēntaruḷḷum muntiyavaṉ peyar. paḻam pāṇṭiya nāṭē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam. kumarikkaṇṭam teṉ kōṭiyi liruntatiṉālēyē, ati yāṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ teṉ ṉavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatu paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirinta teṉṟum, paḻimiyāṉavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa teṉṟum, colvatuṇṭu. pāṇṭiyaṉ mūvēntaruḷ maṭṭu maṉṟip piṟavaracari nōkkiyum paḻimiyāṉavaṉē. mōkūrt talivaṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ paṭittalivaṉumākiya oru ciṟṟaracaṉum paḻiyaṉ eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntāṉ. āyiṉum, ip poruṭkaraṇiyam attuṇip porutta māyt tōṉṟa villi.
pāṇṭi eṉpatu kāḷiyik kuṟikkuñ col. atu pāṇṭil, pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum vaṭivuṅkoḷḷum. oru maṟavaṉik kāḷi eṉpatu marapu. ataṉāl. atu cilarkku iyaṟpeyarākavum iṭappaṭum. valimi, maṟam, uḻippu, poṟuppu mutaliya arun tiṟaṅkaḷ vāynta kāḷi pōṉṟavaṉik kāḷiyeṉṟal uvamiyāku peyar. oru nāṭṭik kākkum aracaṉukku ik kuṇaṅkaḷ iṉṟiyami yātavi. ātalāl, poruṭpālil araciyaṟ pakutiyil iṭukkaṇaḻiyāmi eṉṉum atikārattil,
maṭuttavā yellām pakaṭaṉṉā ṉuṟṟa
 iṭukkaṇ iṭarppā ṭuṭittu 	(kuṟaḷ. 624)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kāḷi eṉpatu āṇmakaṉ, kaṭṭiḷamiyōṉ, pāli nilat talivaṉ ākiya mūvariyuṅ kuṟikkumeṉṟu tivākaram kūṟum. ātalāl, kāḷiyik kuṟikkum pāṇṭi eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟē, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
aruccuṉaṉ tirunīrāṭṭiṟkut teṉṉāṭu vantapōtu cittirāṅkataṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷi maṇantā ṉeṉṉum kati paṟṟi, pāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tirintateṉṟu, varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmi vāynta pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭippeyarik ki.mu. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṉaṉāṉa pāṇṭuvoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttuvatu, viṇṇaka mīṉiyum, maṇṇaka māṉiyum oṉṟā yiṇippatu pōṉṟatē. aruccuṉaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷi maṇakku muṉpē, avaṉ māmaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntamiyi, ak katiyē kūṟukiṉṟatē! iṉi, aiñciṟṟacarar tuṇikkoṇṭu pāṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭatiṉāṟ peṟṟa pañcavaṉ eṉṉum peyarum, pāṇṭavarik kuṟikkum pañcavar eṉpataṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiyataṉṟu. pāṇṭavarik kuṟikkum col eṉṟum paṉmi vaṭivilēyē niṟkum. ākavē, añcavaṉ eṉpatē pañcavaṉ eṉṟu āriyarāl tirikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. pañcavar eṉṟum aivarē. pañcavaṉē aivarōṭu cērnta āṟāmavaṉ.
pāṇṭi eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērpporuḷ vaṭṭam eṉpatē. vaṭṭam eṉpatu uruṭciyiyum tiraṭciyiyum kuṟikkum. kāḷi uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppatu. atu kuṇṭā yiruppatāl kuṇṭi yeṉṟum, viṭittiruppatāl viṭi yeṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka. viṭittal - paruttal.
cōḻaṉ
cōḻanāṭu nīrvaḷattiṟkum nelvaḷattiṟkum aṉṟum iṉṟum peyar peṟṟatu. puṉalnāṭu, vaḷanāṭu eṉṉum nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum, vaḷavaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉ kuṭip peyarum,
vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
 cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
 teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
 naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭittu
eṉṉum auviyār pāṭṭum, tañci māvaṭṭattik kuṟikkum tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum iṟṟic collum, itit teḷivāyk kāṭṭum.
cōṟeṉṟu ciṟappākac colvatu aricic cōṟṟiyē. arici nelliṉ uḷḷīṭātalāl, nel eṉpatum ciṟupāṉmi ariciyik kuṟikkum. neṟcōṟu, neṟparukki, neṟkañci, neṟ pori mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷik kāṇka. nelliṟkuc col eṉṟoru peyaruṇṭu. ataṉiṉṟē cōṟu, coṉṟi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
o. nō:
nal-naṉṟi, pal-paṉṟi.
(tel - teṉ - teṉpu - tempu - teḷivu)
tel - teḷ - teṟu - tēṟu = teḷivu.
teṟu - teṟṟu - teṟṟeṉa = teḷivāka
cul - cuṟu - cūṟu. culavutal = cuṟṟutal, 
cuḻalutal.
cūṟutal = cūḻtal. cuṟu - cuṟṟu.
cōḻanāṭu nelliṟku allatu cōṟṟiṟkuc ciṟanta tiṉāl, nellik kuṟikkum col eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu ap peyar peṟṟirukkalām.
col - (cōḷ) - cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
o.nō: kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāḻakam = karumi. kil - kīḻ,
kel - kēḻal. cul (vaḷi) - cūḻ. tul (poruntu) - tōḻam-tōḻaṉ. pul (tuḷi) - pūḻi. pol, poḷ - pōḻ.
cōḷappeyariṉiṉṟu pirittukkāṭṭavum cōḻam eṉṉum vaṭivu vēṇṭap peṟum.
cōḻanāṭu, mutaṟkālattil  nel mikutiyāy viḷikkap paṭṭatu maṭṭumaṉṟit tāṉāy viḷinta nilamākavum iruntiruk kalām. māntarāl viḷikkappaṭum payirkaḷellām, muṉpu tāmāy viḷintaviyē.
nilittiṇiyāl (tāvarattāl) oru nāṭu peyar peṟuvatu iyalpē. ēḻu tīvukaḷuḷ, nāvalantīvu, iṟalittīvu, ilavantīvu, kucittīvu, tēkkantīvu eṉṉum aintum nilit tiṇiyāṟ peyar peṟṟavi. muḻukippōṉa kumari kaṇṭattilum ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpaṉi nāṭum iruntami kāṇka.
cēraṉ
cēranāṭu maliyāṟ ciṟantatu. ataṉāl poṟiyaṉ, maliyaṉ, maliyamāṉ, malināṭaṉ eṉpaṉa cēraṉ peyar kaḷākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟanta malit toṭarkaḷ mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyum kiḻakkut toṭarcci maliyumē. ivaṟṟik koṇṭatu cēranāṭu. an nāṭṭu nilamuḻutum maliyiṉ irupakkamu muḷḷa cāralē. cāralāvatu maliccarivu nilam. ataṉāl cāral nāṭaṉ eṉpatu malināṭaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum.
vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ
 cāral nāṭa cevviyi yākumati 	(kuṟun. 18 : 1-2)
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuṭ pakutiyil  iravukkuṟi vantu nīṅkum talimakaṉ cāral nāṭa eṉat tōḻiyāl viḷikkap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
cāral eṉpatu aṉṉīṟu peṟṟuc cāralaṉ eṉṟākum. atu piṉṉarc cēralaṉ eṉat tirintu kuṭamalināṭṭu vēntaṉik kuṟittatu. cēralaṉ eṉpatu īṟṟayal tokkuc cēraṉ eṉṟāyatu. cēraṉ eṉpatum, ceyvaṉ eṉpatu ceyval eṉṟu tirintāṟpōl īṟu tirintu cēral eṉṟāyiṟṟu. māṉ eṉṉum īṟu cēriṉ, cēraṉ eṉpatu cēramāṉ eṉṟām. māṉ eṉpatu makaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu.
tamiḻmakkaḷ kumarikkaṇṭa aḷavilēyē vāḻntapōtu pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉē āṇṭāṉeṉṟum piṉpu makkaḷ peruki vaṭakkiṟ paraviyapiṉ oru pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ iḷavalār iruvar cōḻaṉum cēraṉumāki vaṭanāvali āṇṭā reṉṟum, oru cevimarapuc ceyti vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. mūvēntaruḷ muntiya pāṇṭiyaṉi āriyaṉākkiviṭiṉ, ēṉiyiruvarum tāmāka āriyarāyviṭuvar eṉpatu tamiḻp pakivar karuttu. av vēmāṟṟu ārāycciyum urimi yuṇarcciyum mikka ikkālattuc cellā tākum.
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu,
 teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi	(cilap. 11 : 21-2)
eṉpatu mūvēntaruḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉ muntiyavaṉ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟām.
12. paḻantamiḻaka iṭappeyarkaḷ
kumari
kumari eṉpatu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamāṉa paḻam pāṇṭiyiṉ teṉ kōṭiyaṭutta oru māperu malittoṭariṉ peyarum, ak kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyaṭutta oru pērāṟṟiṉ peyaru māyiruntatu. ip peyariyum, kumariyāṟṟiṉ maṟupeyarāṉa kaṉṉi eṉpatiyum, kumarimaliyiṟ piṟantōṭiya paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅ kariyilirunta talikkaḻaka irukkiyāṉa pāṇṭiyar mutal talinakariṉ maturi eṉṉum peyariyum, vaṭa collākak kāṭṭiviṭiṉ, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, intiya nākarikam āriya nākarikam eṉṟum, āyviṭum eṉṉum karuttuṭaṉ, tamiḻp paki varāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṉṟutoṭṭu ak kumarik kaṇṭa iṭappeyarkaṭku vaṭa coṉmūlaṅ kaṟpittu varalāyiṉar.
kumari eṉpatu kum eṉṉum tūya tamiḻ mutaṉili yiṉiṉṟu piṟanta tamiḻccol.
kummutal = kuvital, kūṭutal, tiraḷutal, paruttal.
kum - kummal - kummali = paruttavaḷ. kum - kumukku = kūṭṭam.
kum - kumar = kūṭaṟkēṟṟa iḷami allatu parumi, atiyuṭiya 		kaṉṉi, kaṉṉimi, iḷami, aḻiyāt taṉmi.
kumar - kumaraṉ = kūṭaṟkēṟṟa iḷiñaṉ, iḷañaṉāṉa murukaṉ. 
o. nō: muruku = iḷami, murukaṉ. muruku - murukaṉ.
kumar - kumari = iḷiñi, kaṉṉi, kaṉṉiyākak karutappeṟum  		kāḷi, ōr āṟu, oru mali.
kūṭaṟkēṟṟa niliyil ellā vuyiriṉaṅkaḷum atataṟ kēṟṟavāṟu paruttiruttal iyalpu. itaṉālēyē, āṭu māṭu mutaliya cila vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum kōḻi mayil mutaliya paṟavi yiṉaṅkaḷum, parutta iḷaminiliyil viṭiyeṉappaṭum. viṭittal = paruttal. viṭi - viṭali = iḷiñaṉ, pālinilat talivaṉ. virgin eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkum, virgo, to swell eṉṟu poruṭkaraṇiyaṅkāṭṭuvar. oruvaṉik kumaraṉ eṉṟum oruttiyik kumari eṉṟum collumpōtu, iḷamik karuttuṭaṉ parumi allatu valimik karuttum kalantiruttali nōkkuka.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
 tāṉalan tirukat taṉmiyil kuṉṟi
 mulliyum kuṟiñciyum muṟimiyil tirintu
 nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
 pāli eṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum 	(cilap.11: 62-6)
kumari eṉṉuṅ kāḷi toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭup pāli nilat teyvam. pāli eṉpatu mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyum mutuvēṉilil aṭiyum vaṟaṇṭa nili. kumarimali kuṟiñcinilat taṉmiyāl ap peyar peṟa urimiyuḷḷatu. kāḷi pōril veṟṟi tarum koṟṟavi yeṉṟum, kuru eṉṉum koppuḷa nōy varuvikkum ammi yeṉṟum, karutap paṭṭapiṉ, avaḷ vaḻipāṭu ēṉi nilaṅkaṭkum potuvāyiṟṟu. vaṭanāṭṭilum tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē perumpālarā yirukkiṉṟamiyiṉ, aṅkum kāḷi vaṇakkam iruntu varukiṉṟatu. varalāṟaṟiyātavar vaṭakkiliruppa tellām āriya vaḻakkameṉat tavaṟākak karutik koṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. kumari tamiḻar potut teyvamāṉa piṉ, avaḷ peyar ōr āṟṟiṟkum iṭappaṭṭatu.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil kumāra, kumāri eṉa nīṇṭu, muṟiyē makaṉiyum makaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vaṭavar kumāra eṉṉum collik ku+māra eṉṟu pirittu, cāvillātavaṉ. ataṉāl iḷamiyāṉavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. āṟiluñ cāvu, nūṟiluñ cāvu. ātalāl, aṅṅaṉam poruḷ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. kumari eṉṉum coṟku uṭal tiraṇṭa iḷiñi eṉpatē iyaṟporu ḷeṉpatu, kōṭic cēlikkoru veḷḷi, kumarip peṇṇuk koru piḷḷi  eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl teriyavarum. iḷami paṟṟiyē īṉāvāḻi kumarivāḻi eṉap paṭutalum eṉka.
kaṉṉi
muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyil kumari yeṉṟoru pēriyāṟiruntami, muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
eṉṟu tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyirattilum,
teṉāa turukeḻu kumari	(puṟam. 6)
eṉṟu puṟappāṭṭilum kuṟikkappaṭṭatu kumariyāṟē.
kumari, kaṉṉi eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷātalāl, kumariyāṟu kaṉṉiyāṟu eṉavumpaṭum.
maṉṉu māli veṇkuṭiyāṉ
vaḷiyāc ceṅkō latuvōccik
 kaṉṉi taṉṉip puṇarntālum
pulavāy vāḻi kāvēri                               
eṉṉum cilappatikārak kāṉalvarip pāṭal, kumariyāṟṟik kaṉṉi yeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṉṉi eṉpatu kāḷiyiṉ peyarē.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum col, makkaḷiṉattup peṇṇik kuṟikkum pōtu, pūppaṭintu maṇamākāta peṇṇiyē kuṟikkum.
oru peṇ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇamākātirukkalā mātalāl, iḷaṅkaṉṉi kaṉṉiki yeṉappaṭuvāḷ. ki eṉpatu oru ciṟumip poruḷ piṉṉoṭṭu (Diminutive suffix).
e-ṭu:	kuṭi (vīṭu) - kuṭiki (ciṟuvīṭu) - kuṭici.
pūppaṭiyāta ciṟumiyiyum maṇamāṉa peṇṇiyum kaṉṉi yeṉṟu collum vaḻakkamilli. kaṉṉikaḻital kaṉṉi yaḻital eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum collik kaṉyā eṉṟum, kaṉṉiki eṉṉum collik kaṉyakā eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tirittuc ciṟumi, makaḷ eṉṟa poruḷkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvar. ataṟkēṟpa, kaṉ (tikaḻ), kaṉa (ciṟu) eṉpavaṟṟi vērākak kāṭṭuvar.
tikaḻtalik kuṟikkum kaṉ eṉṉuñ col valintu poruttuvatākum. ciṟumiyik kuṟikkum kaṉa eṉṉuñ col pūppaṭinta peṇṇiṟkup poruntātu.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vērākak kāṭṭum kaṉ, kaṉa eṉṉum iru collum teṉcol tiripē.
kal - kaṉṟu. kaṉṟutal = erital, karukutal,ciṉattal.
kal - kaṉ - kaṉal = neruppu. kaṉalutal = erital, ciṉattal.
kaṉali = katiravaṉ.
kal - kaḷ - kāḷ = kāḷam (kāḷavāyil) = cuṇṇāmpuk kal nīṟṟum cuḷḷi. kāḷ-kāy. kāytal = erital, ciṉattal, tikaḻtal
kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇa - kaṇappu=neruppu. tikaḻtal neruppiṉ 	toḻil. kaṇ - kaṇi = cūṭu. kaṉ - va. kaṉ.
kul - kuṉ- kuṉṉi = mikac ciṟiyatu. kuṉṉutal = ciṟuttal, 	oṭuṅkutal.
kul - kuṉṟu - kuṉṟi. kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷḷam, kuḷḷi. kuḷ - kuṭṭi. kuḷ - kuṟu- kuṟṟi -kucci. kuṟu - kuṟuku. kuṟu - kuṟaḷ - kuṟaḷi.
mikac ciṟiyavaṟṟi naṉṉiyuṅ kuṉṉiyum eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
kuṉ-kaṉ-kaṉa(va.)
kaṉṉutal eṉpatu paḻuttalik kuṟikkum ōr aruntamiḻc col. veppattiṉālāvatu aḻuttattiṉālāvatu uḷḷaṅ kiyilum uḷḷaṅkālilum arattaṅkaṭṭic civantuviṭṭāl, arattaṅ kaṉṉi viṭṭatu eṉpar. kaṉṉutal vēṟu; kaṉṟutal vēṟu. muṉṉatu paḻuttal; piṉṉatu valuttal. kaṉi (paḻam) eṉṉum col kaṉṉi (paḻuttatu) eṉpataṉ tokuttalē.
o.nō: kiḷḷi - kiḷi, maṇṇi - maṇi.
kiḷḷutalāvatu kāykaṉikaḷik kottutal. maṇṇu talāvatu kalli mācaṟak kaḻuvutal. kiḷappatu kiḷi eṉpatu ārāycciyillār kūṟṟeṉa aṟika.
kaḷḷutal eṉṉum viṉi vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ, kaḷi eṉṉum viṉip peyar allatu viṉimutaṟpeyar mutaṉiliyāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, kaṉi eṉṉum viṉimutaṟpeyarum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉa aṟika.
pūppu eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē kaṉṉutal eṉṉum collum nilittiṇik kuriyatāyiruppatum, (mature) eṉṉum āṅkilac collum paḻuttal pūppaṭital eṉṉum iruporuḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatum iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
kaṉinta kaṉipōṉṟa pūppaṭinta peṇṇum nukarccik kēṟṟavaḷ eṉpatiyē, kaṉṉi eṉṉum col uṇarttum camintavaḷ(pakkuva māṉavaḷ) eṉṉuñ colliyum nōkkuka.
maturi
maturiyeṉṟu mutalāvatu peyar peṟṟatu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅ karit talikkaḻaka irukkiyē. atuvum iṭikkaḻaka irukki yākiya kapāṭapuramum(alivāy?) muḻukip pōṉa piṉpē, iṟṟi vikik kari maturi amintatu. atuvum mūṉṟām maturiyē.
kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē kaṇṇaṉ maturiyiyum amittaṉar. ataṟku ap peyar iṭṭatu tam muṉṉōriyum avar nakariyum niṉivukūrtaṟkē. ap peyar annāṭṭu moḻiyiyalpiṟkēṟpa maturā eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. kaṇṇaṉ kālamākiya pāratak kālattil viki maturiyiṉmiyāl, nāvalan tēyattil iraṇṭāvatu ēṟpaṭṭa maturi vaṭanāṭṭu maturiyē. ātalāl, ati amittavar talikkaḻaka mirunta nilappakutiyi muḻukkiya mutaṟ kaṭal kōḷiṉiṉṟu tappiyavarāyiruttal vēṇṭum. viki maturi pāratak kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. ataṟkum pintiyavi māṉavīraṉ  maturiyum tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṭamaturiyum eṉka. vaṭanāṭṭu maturi nōkkiyē talikkaḻaka maturi teṉmaturi yeṉap paṭṭateṉa aṟika.
maturi eṉṉum iṭappeyar, tiṅkaḷik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum colliliruntu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. talikkaḻaka maturi yamitta pāṇṭiyaṉ, taṉ kulamutalākak koṇṭa cuṭarppeyari aṭiyākakkoṇṭu, matiri eṉṟu an nakarkkup peyariṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. kuti eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu kutiri eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka. matiri eṉpatu piṉpu olippeḷimipaṟṟi maturi eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
o: nō: etirki - etuki.
kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ vēṇṭukōṭkiṇaṅki, civa peru māṉ taṉ caṭimuṭiyiluḷḷa matiyiṉiṉṟu matuvip poḻinta iṭam maturi yeṉappaṭṭateṉṟu, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu tolkati muṟippaṭṭa uṉṉippuc colliyal (Guessing Etymology) eṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
ticitici tēṉārkkun tirumaruta muṉṟuṟi 	(kalit.26)
vikik kariyic cārntatātaliṉ, maruta muṉṟuṟi eṉpatu paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅkari maturip peyarukkuk karaṇiya mākātu.
1
ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
 ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
 miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
 taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ. 	(taṇṭiyalaṅkāra vurimēṟkōḷ)
iyaṉilip paṭalam
(tōrā. ki. mu. 50,000-10,000)
1. muṟpaṭi
i kumarikkaṇṭam (tōrā. ».K.?-5,500)
intumāvāri oru kālattil cantāt tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, āciyāviṉ teṉkari vaḻiyāy āppirikkāviṉ kīḻikkarimaṭṭum paraviyirunta oru nilapparappāyiruntatu. kiḷēṟṟar ip paḻam peruṅ kaṇṭatti, atil vatintirunta kuraṅkotta uyiri(pirāṇi)paṟṟi ilemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟaḻik kiṉṟār. ik kaṇṭam māntaṉiṉ piṟantakamā yirukkak kūṭumāta liṉ, mika mutaṉmiyāṉatu eṉṟār pēraṟiñar ekkēl.
oru kālattil teṉṉāppirikkāviyum intiyāvi yum iṇittukkoṇṭu oru toṭarnta nilapparappiruntatu eṉṟār aṟiñar ōlṭukām.
kāṭṭu eliyaṭṭu eṉpavar eḻutiyuḷḷa maṟinta ïbyKÇah(Lost Lemuria) eṉṉum nūliluḷḷa nilappaṭatti liruntu, oru peru malittoṭar mēlikkaṭalil toṭaṅkit teṉvaṭalākak kumari muṉikkut teṉpālirunta nilappakutiyil neṭuntolivu ceṉṟu, piṉpu teṉpā lirunta nilappakutiyil neṭuntolivu ceṉṟu, piṉpu teṉmēṟkākat tirumpi, maṭakācukkar eṉṉum āppirikkat tīvuvari ceṉṟatākat terikiṟatu eṉṟār pērā. kā.cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi.
kaṭalnūl (Oceanography) eṉṉum taṟkālak kali, oru kālattil, teṉamerikkāviṉiṉṟu āppirikkāvi yoṭṭi yum intiyāvi yoṭṭiyum āttirēliyāvari paṭarntirunta tum, `kāṇṭuvāṉāk kaṇṭam' eṉṟaṟiyappaṭṭatumāṉa, oru muḻukiya viyaṉilattippaṟṟi, viyakkattakkavuṇmikaḷi aṇmiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu 29-07-1934-l veḷivanta intiyap paṭaviḷakkak kiḻamiyitaḻc ceytiyākum.
kōṭiyāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ-oruvēḷi ataṟku munti-oru peruṅkaṇṭam āppirikkāviyum intiyāviyum iṇittuk koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟār tiruvāḷar yōvāṉ iṅkilāntu.
ii kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ tōṟṟam (tōrā. ki.mu. 500,000)
ituvari ulakiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa paḻiya māntaṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷuḷ, rhÈ¤ÔÉš(Java) 1891-l tūpāyicu eṉpavarāl eṭukkappaṭṭataṟkuriya nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropos Erectus) kālam ki.mu. 500,000 eṉṟu kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 1961-l teṉṉāppirikkāvil taṅkaṉiyikkāvil ilīkki (Leakey) eṉṉum āṅkila māntaṉū laṟiñarāṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa īrelumpuk kūṭukaḷuḷ, oṉṟaṟkuriya koṭṭiyuṭippāṉ (Nut-cracker Man or Sinjanthropos Boisi) iṟṟikku 6,00,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vaṉ eṉṟum, iṉṉoṉṟaṟkuriya, iṉṉum peyariṭappaṭāta, naṉimika muntiya māntaṉ, kuṟinta pakkam 17,50,660 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iti amerikka māntaṉūṟ pērāciriyar cilar maṟuttuḷḷaṉar. uṇmi eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, cālit tīviyum teṉṉāppirikkāviyum taṉ ṉuṭaṉ iṇittuk koṇṭirunta nāṭē kumarikkaṇṭa mātaliṉ, an nilattu māntaṉ tōṟṟam ki.mu. 500,000 āṇṭukaṭku muntiya teṉṟu maṟuppacca miṉṟik kūṟalām.
iii kumari māntar moḻiyaṟṟa nili (tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 1,00,000)
kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ, mutaṟkālattil niliyāṉa maṇavuṟa viṉṟi vilaṅkupōl avvappōtu taṉ vēṭki kaḷit taṇittuk koṇṭu, moḻiyum moḻiyuṇarcciyumiṉṟi iyaṟki yāṉa uṇarcci yolikaḷiyum viḷiyolikaḷiyumēyuṭiyava ṉāy, perumpālum cikikaḷālēyē taṉ karutti veḷip paṭutti vantāṉ. ākiyāl, avaṉ moḻi ciki moḻiyākavē (Gesture language or Sign language) iruntatu. uṭaṟciki, uṟuppuc ciki eṉac ciki iru tiṟappaṭu mātalāl, mukaccikiyākiya valiccamum (grimace) cikiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
moḻiyaṟṟa niliyil, māntaṉ karuttum eṇṇamum uruvalippākavē (imagination) iruntuvantaṉa. uruvalippāvatu, ōr iṭattiyō, poruḷiyō, nikaḻcciyiyō uḷḷattiṟ paṭam piṭittal.
moḻittuṇiyiṉṟik karuttu nikaḻāteṉpatu ārāycci yillātār kūṟṟē. uṇarcci, vēṭki, niṉippu, eṇṇam, tīrmāṉam, akakkāṭci, iṉpuṟavu, pontiki (tirupti), makiḻcci, tuṉpuṟavu, ciṉam allatu veṟuppu ākiya palvēṟu uḷanikaḻcci kaḷum, namakkiruppatu pōṉṟē moḻiyaṟṟa māntaṉukkum iruntaṉa. ivvuṇmiyi iṉṟum ūmiyariṭattuk kāṇka. moḻiyaminta piṉpum, māntaṉ karuttiṟ perumpakuti uruva lippē yeṉpati ōrntuṇarka.
iv iyaṟki moḻi (tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000-50,000)
eḻuttup palukkamum (uccarippum) coṟporuttum iṉṟip perumpālum iyalpākap pēcappaṭum olittokuti, iyaṟki moḻiyām (Natural Language). itu muḻittalmoḻi (Inarticulate Speech) eṉappaṭum, itaṉ olikaḷ eḻuvakiya:
(1) uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds)
iṉpa tuṉpa uṇarcciyi veḷiyiṭum olikaḷ uṇarcci yolikaḷ.
e-ṭu: ā, ī, ū, ō, ai
(2) viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative Sounds)
piṟari viḷikkum olikaḷ viḷiyolikaḷ. viḷittal -kūppiṭutal.
e-ṭu: ē, ēy, ō, ēlā, ellā.
(3) oppolikaḷ (Imitative Sounds)
irutiṇip poruḷkaḷum ceyyum olikaḷi ottolippavi oppolikaḷ.
e-ṭu: kū (kūvu), kā kā-(kākam), kākkā (kākki), im (imiḻ), ur (uraṟu), ūḷ (ūḷi), kur (kuraṅku), mā (māṭu), cīttu (cīṟu), ō (ōci), kar (kari), caracara (cāri), cal- (calaṅki-cataṅki), kīr-kīri.
kalakala, kiṇ, kaṇīr, kiriccu, caḷ, caḷār, calacala, tiṇ, maṭakku, viccu, vīr eṉpaṉavum, ivi pōṉṟa piṟavum, oppolikaḷē. ivi iṉṟu olikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ōkkaḷi-ōkkāḷam, umaṭṭu-uvaṭṭu, kuṟaṭṭi, cappu, tuppu, tummu, vikku, mukku, ici, ciri mutaliyaṉa oppoli yaṭippaṭiyiṟ piṟkālattup piṟanta coṟkaḷām.
iṉi, vāyoliyiṉṟi vāyvaḻiyum mūkkuvaḻiyum cevippula ṉākum kāṟṟumaṭṭum veḷivarum ceyalkaṭkum, oppoli muṟiyil coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: ā-āvi, ū-ūtu, mūcu-mūccu.
koṭṭāvi viṭumpōtu āveṉṟum, nerupperik kavum viḷakkaṇikkavum cūṭāṉa poruḷi āṟṟavum nōvu tīrkkavum ūtumpōtu ūveṉṟum olittaṟkēṟpa, vāyiṉiṉṟu kāṟṟu veḷi varutalum; urakka mūccu viṭumpōtum iḷikkum pōtum, mūcu eṉṟu kēṭkumāṟu mūkkiṉiṉṟu kāṟṟu veḷivarutalum; kāṇka.
	āvittal = koṭṭāvi viṭutal, perumūccu viṭutal, vāy viṭutal.
	āvi = koṭṭāvi, uyirppu, neṭṭuyirppu,uyir, ātaṉ (āṉmā),  nīrāvi, puki, naṟumaṇam, uyireḻuttu.
	ūtutal = ūtuvatu pōl turuttiyāl kāṟṟeḻup putal, ūtiya turuttiyum piyum pōl vīṅkutal, paruttal, mikutal, vaṇṭu kuḻalūtu vatu pōl icittukkoṇṭu tēṉi nukartal, ūtukuḻalpōl vaṇṭu marattit tuḷittal.
	ūtu-ūti. ūtu-ūtili = makuṭi, viḷiyāṭṭūti.
	ūtu-ūtiyam (ilāpam). celaviṉum mikuntatu ūtiyam.
	ūtu-ūttu-ūttam,
	ūtu-ūtāri = tukaḷi ūtit tuṭittal pōl celvatti vīṇ celaviṭṭup pōkkupavaṉ.
	o - nō:	blow the expense = to spend recklessly.
		   	blow = to squander, spend (sum) recklessly.
	ūtu-ōtu.ōtutal = kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.
	mūcutal = urakka mūccuviṭutal, mūccuviṭutal, mōppam piṭittal, mukartal.
	mūcu mūceṉṟu iḷikkiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
	mucu-mūccu. o.nō: pēcu-pēccu.
	mūcu-mūñcu-mūñci=kuraṅku nāy mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup pōl mūkkoṭu muṉ nīṇṭa mukappakuti (muzzle).
	mūñcu-mūñcuṟu-mūñcūṟu = etiyum mōntu pārkkum vīṭṭelivaki. mūñceli eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
(4) kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds)
vaḻakkappaṭi cila karuttukaḷip piṟarkkut teriyum vakiyil kuṟikkum olikaḷ kuṟippolikaḷām.
eḻutamuṭivaṉavum eḻutamuṭiyātaṉavumāka, kuṟippoli kaḷ iruvakiya. ūm(ūṅkoṭṭutal), cī, pū eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi eḻutamuṭivaṉa. moccaṭṭamum (moccak koṭṭutal - smacking), muṟkum (clucking), vīḷiyum (cīḻkki- whistling) pōṉṟavi eḻuta muṭiyātaṉa.
tō tō (tuvā tuvā) eṉṟu nāyiyum, pēpē (pō pō) eṉṟu kōḻiyiyum, vēcu vēcu eṉṟu pūṉiyiyum, pāy pāy eṉṟu āṭṭiyum viḷikkum olikaḷ viḷiyoli kaḷāyiṉum vaḻakkampaṟṟik kuṟippāl uṇarappaṭuvatāṟ kuṟippolikaḷām.
(5) vāycceyki yolikaḷ (Gesticulatory Sounds)
māntaṉ iyaṟkiyākavum ceyaṟkiyākavum taṉ vāyiṉāṟ ceyyum cila ceykikaḷum cikikaḷum, ovvōr oliyip piṟappittaṟkēṟṟa vāyvaṭivi amittu, av volikaḷiṉ vāyilāy ac coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa coṟ kaḷip piṟappit      tirukkiṉṟaṉa. ac coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa av volikaḷ vāyc ceyki yolikaḷām.
e-ṭu: ā-a-aṅkā. aṅkāttal = vāytiṟattal.
         av-kav-vav. av-avvu-kavvu-vavvu.
oṉṟik kavvutaliyotta vāycciki nili, av eṉṉum oliyit tōṟṟuvittaṟ kēṟṟatātal kāṇka. mēlvāyp pal kīḻutaṭṭoṭu poruntuvatē kavvum niliyām. in nili vakaramey yolippiṟkē ēṟkum.
piṟkālattil avvutal eṉṉum col maṉattiṉāṟ paṟṟu talukkum, kavvutal eṉṉum col vāyiṉāṟ paṟṟutalukkum, vavvutal eṉṉum col kiyiṉāṟ paṟṟutalukkum variyaṟukkap peṟṟaṉa. āyiṉum, iṉṟum, avvutal ulaka vaḻakkil vāyiṉāṟ paṟṟutali uṇarttum.

(6) kuḻavivaḷarp polikaḷ (Nursery Sounds)
kuḻavivaḷarppil kuḻavikaḷ iyalpāka olikkum olikaḷi ṉiṉṟu tāymār cila coṟkaḷi amittuk koḷvatuṇṭu. attakiya oli kaḷ kuḻavivaḷarppolikaḷ. avi mikac cilavē.
e-ṭu: iṅku - iṅkā = pāl.
kuḻavip paruvattiṉum periyatu kuḻantip paruvam. ap paruvattil, cōṟu eṉpatic cō, cōy, cōci eṉṟu kuḻan tikaḷ kocciyāyc colpavi kuḻavi vaḷarp polikaḷākā. ṭum ṭum (tom tom), Õ¥Õ(pipe) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi kuḻantikaḷ collum oppolic coṟkaḷ.
kuḻavikaḷ mama, papa eṉṟu iyalpāka aṭukki olippatiṉiṉṟu āṅkila naṟṟāyarum cevilit tāyarum mammā (mamma), pappā (papa) eṉṉum muṟippeyarkaḷi amittukkoṇṭatāka moḻi nūlāciriyar cilar kūṟuvar. āyiṉ, āṅkilam tōṉṟu muṉpē, vaṭacēmiyak kiḷiyic cērnta aramiya (Aramic) moḻiyil ammā, appā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cumēriya nākarikam tamiḻa nākarikattiṉ tiripātalālum, kumarināṭṭu makkaḷē āppirikka vaḻiyāka airōppā ceṉṟu paravi yiruppatākat terivatālum, ammā appā eṉṉuñ tamiḻccoṟkaḷē āṅkilattil mammā pappā eṉṟu tirintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām. akaramutal pakaramutalākat tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum kūṭat tiritaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	añcavaṉ-pañcavaṉ = aiñciṟṟaracarik koṇṭu   	āṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ,
	appaḷam-pappaṭam (ma.)
tamiḻa māntar kuṟiñci nākarika niliyilēyē māṭṭip pāliṟkāka vaḷarkkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatākat terivatāl, tāyi viṭṭu nīṅkiya kaṉṟukkuṭṭiyiṉ kataṟaliṉiṉṟu, ammā eṉṉuñ collit tamiḻar aṉṉiyik kuṟikka amittukkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvatu mikap porutta mām. piṉpu, atu appā eṉṟu valittut tantiyik kuṟittirukkiṉṟatu. melliṉa meyccol meṉmi mikka tāyiyum, valliṉa meyccol vaṉmi mikka tantiyiyum, kuṟippatu iyaṟkiyē.
(7) cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds)
cuṭṭikkāṭṭum olikaḷ cuṭṭolikaḷ.
cēymiyic cuṭṭutaṟkēṟpa vāyi virivāyt tiṟan tolikkum ākāramum, cēymikkup piṟpaṭṭa aṇmiyic cuṭṭutaṟkēṟpa vāyip piṉṉukkiḻut tolikkum īkāramum, cēymikkum aṇmikkum iṭippaṭṭa muṉmiyic cuṭṭutaṟ kēṟpa itaḻkaḷi muṟpaṭak kuvittolikkum ūkāramum, muṟiyē cēymiyaṇmi muṉmic cuṭṭolikaḷāyiṉa. iviyē mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil tōṉṟiya uyirkaḷ. ivi uṇmi yil vāycciki yolikaḷākum. āyiṉum, ciṟappu nōkki vēṟu pirikkappeṟṟaṉa.
mutaṟkaṇ mūviṭaccuṭṭu kiccikiyiṉāl maṭṭum nikaḻntatu. piṉpu, kiccikiyōṭu kūṭiya vāyccikiyi ṉāl nikaḻntatu, ataṉpiṉ, vāyccikiyiṉāl maṭṭum nikaḻntu varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, av vāyccikit taṉmi iṉṟu maṟin tuḷḷatu, muccuṭṭolikaḷum vāycciki yolikaḷāyiruppa taṉālēyē, avi tamiḻil ek karaṇiyattiyiṭṭum piṟamoḻi kaḷiṟpōl iṭamāṟic cuṭṭuvatilli. itaṉāl, tamiḻoṭu ciṟitum peritum toṭar puḷḷa piṟa moḻic cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭ kellām, tamiḻc coṟkaḷē mūlameṉpatit teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷḷalām.

2. tamiḻt tōṟṟam (ceyaṟki moḻi)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000)
mūvakic cuṭṭolikaḷiṉiṉṟuñ coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyatē tamiḻt tōṟṟamām. itu ceyaṟki moḻi (Artificial Language) allatu iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉappaṭum.
muntiyal māntarmoḻi yellām icippāṭṭākavē tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatu ecuppercaṉ karuttu. atu tavaṟām. uṇarcci viñciya kātal, iṉpam, veṟṟi, makiḻcci, tuṉpam mutaliya maṉa nilikaḷilēyē muntiyal māntar-avaruḷḷum icivuṇarcciyum, moḻiyāṟṟalum mikkavarē-icimoḻi vāyilāyt tam karutti veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ikkālattum, iṉpa tuṉpavuṇarcci viñciya pōtu, tattam aṟivunilik kēṟpa icippāṭṭākavum ceyyuḷākavum tam karuttic cilar veḷiyiṭutal kāṇka. mēlum, poruḷum oḻuṅku maṟṟa orucila iciyolit tokuti kaḷiṉiṉṟu vevvēṟu poruḷum ilakkaṇa voḻuṅkumuḷḷa peyar viṉiyiṭic coṟkaḷum, mūviṭap peyarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭa īṟukaḷum, vēṟṟumi urupukaḷum kāla viṭinilikaḷum, piṟavum, amintaṉa veṉṟal, uttikkup poruntāta kūṟṟām.
uri, ceyyuḷ (allatu pāṭṭu) eṉṉum iruvaki moḻi naṭiyuḷ, mutalil tōṉṟiyatu muṉṉatē. atuvum taṉittaṉi collākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ākavē, collē moḻiyalakām (Unit of speech). uḷḷattil tōṉṟum karuttu, atit terivikkum coṟṟoṭarkku muṟṟum ottataṉṟu. amiya vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kēṟpa, oru collē pala coṟṟoṭarkkuriya pala karuttit terivikkalām. oru kuḻanti allatu nōyāḷi taṇṇīr eṉṟāl, atu,
(1)	eṉakkut taṇṇīrvēṇṭum;
(2)	atō pār! taṇṇīr;
(3)	veḷḷam vīṭṭiṟkuḷ vantuviṭṭatu;
(4)	taṇṇīr koṇṭuvā;
(5)	intap pāl taṇṇīr kalantatu;
(6)	ic col taṇṇīr;
(7)	itaṉ poruḷ taṇṇīr;
(8)	iv viṭukati viṭi taṇṇīr;
(9)	ip paṭattiluḷḷatu taṇṇīr;
(10)	(Cold) water eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟku nēr teṉ col `taṇṇīr
eṉṟu pala karuttit terivippatuṭaṉ, nī kuṭippatu eṉṉa? eṉpatupōṉṟa viṉāviṟku viṭiyākavumiruttal kāṇka. naṉṟāyp pēcat teriyāta kuḻantiyum, pēccu valimiyaṟṟa nōyāḷiyum, tāymoḻiyoṉṟē terinta ayal nāṭṭāṉum, perumpālum taṉic coṟkaḷālēyē tam karuttit terivittal kāṇka. in niliyilēyē moḻi vaḷarcciyuṟāta muntiyal māntaṉum iruntāṉ.
moḻi eḷiya niliyil tōṉṟip paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarnta māntaṉ amippēyaṉṟi, iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkappeṟṟu iyaṟkiyāka aṟiyappaṭṭataṉṟu. moḻi iyaṟkiyāṉatāyiṉ ellā makkaḷum kallāmalē orē moḻi pēcutalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamilli. makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ nākarika nilikkut takkavāṟu, moḻikaḷ vevvēṟu vakippaṭṭum nilippaṭṭum amippuk koṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. cila anākarika māntar moḻikaḷ paṟavi vilaṅkolikaḷiṉum caṟṟē uyarntaviyākum. ettuṇi uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāyiṉum, taliciṟanta aṟiviyal vaḷarcci peṟṟa makkaḷāyiṉum, ovvoru collāy mella  mellak kaṟṟālaṉṟi oruvarum tam tāymoḻiyip pēcaviyalātu.
moḻittuṇiyiṉṟiyum māntarkkuk karuttu nikaḻum. ūmiyar nili itaṟkut taliciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭām. aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyum itaṟku oruvakic cāṉ ṟākum. moḻiyiṉṟik karuttu nikaḻāteṉpatu, ārāycciyil lār kūṟṟē. viṉikkut tuṇikkoḷvatu pōṉṟatē. uṇarcci, vēṭki, niṉippu, cūḻvu, tīrmāṉam, iṉpam (uvaki), tuṉpam (aḻuki) mutaliya eṇ allatu toṇ (oṉpāṉ) cuvinili, aiyam (cantēkam), teḷivu ākiyaviyē maṉattoḻilkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṟku moḻittuṇi tēviyilli. kāṭcipporuḷka ḷellām maṉattilum tōṉṟumātalāl, niṉippum cūḻvum cUtÈ¥ghfnt(imagination) nikaḻum.
moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭinta piṟkālattilum, coṟṟoṭar kaḷiluḷḷa ellā ilakkaṇak kūṟukaṭkum otta pakutikaḷ uḷḷak karuttililli. pāṟkuṭam eṉpatip pāliyuṭiya kuṭam eṉa virippar ilakkaṇiyar. av virippiluḷḷa aikāravēṟṟumi yurupum uṭiyaveṉṉum kuṟippup peyareccamum, uḷḷak karuttililli. atiluḷḷavi yellām pālum atik koṇṭa kuṭamumē. iṅṅaṉamē, viṉimuṟṟiluḷḷa aṟuvaki yuṟuppukaḷuḷ, mutaṉili yiṭinili yiṟutinilikkuriya karuttu kaḷē uḷḷattiluṇṭu. aviyum moḻiyil oruṅkē tōṉṟa villi. mutalil mutaṉiliyē irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭaṅ kaṭkum uriya ellā viṉivaṭiviṟkum potuvāyiruntatu; piṉpu pālīṟum kāla viṭi niliyum eccamuṟṟu vēṟupāṭum muṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. iv vuṇmiyellām tamiḻpōṉṟa iyaṉ moḻi vāyilākavē aṟiyamuṭiyum; camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa tirimoḻiyi yum ceyaṟkimoḻiyiyum aṭippaṭiyāy vittārāyiṉ, airōppiyarāyiṉum amerikka rāyiṉum kāriruḷiṟ kāṭṭuvaḻic celvārpōl oṉṟuṅ kaṇṭaṟiyār.
cuṭṭaṭiccollākkam
muccuṭṭukaḷuḷ, cēymiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēymik karuttut tavira vēṟoṉṟum piṟattaṟ kiṭamilli; aṇmiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu, aṇmi, piṉmi, iḻiki mutaliya orucila karuttukaḷē piṟak kiṉṟaṉa; āyiṉ, muṉmic cuṭṭiṉiṉṟē, tōṉṟal (muṉvarutal), muṉmi, muṟcellal, neruṅkal, poruntal, vaḷital, tuḷittal, turuval ākiya eṇperuṅ karuttukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟku iṭippaṭṭaṉavum ivaṟṟiṟ kiḷittaṉavumāṉa ettuṇiyō nuṇkaruttu kaḷum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi yellām eṉ `mutaṟṟāy moḻi'yuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka. iṅku eṇperuṅ karuttukaḷē iṉṟiyamiyāta aḷavu viḷakkappeṟum.
tōṉṟal eṉpatu, tāyiṉiṉṟu kuḻaviyum marattiṉiṉṟu tuḷirum tōṉṟuvatupōṉṟa iyaṟkit tōṉṟalum; vīṭṭi ṉiṉṟu māntaṉum vaḷiyiṉiṉṟu eliyum tōṉṟuvatu pōṉṟa ceyaṟkit tōṉṟalum, āka iruvakippaṭum. tōṉṟutal muṉ varutalātaliṉ, tōṉṟaṟkaruttilēyē taṟkiḻami piṟitiṉ kiḻami ākiya iruvaki muṉmik karuttum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. muṉkiḷi yum muṉkiyum pōṉṟavi taṟkiḻami muṉmi; muṉ poṟiyum (muṉpāramum) muṉtūtaṉum pōṉṟavi piṟitiṉkiḻami muṉmi.
tōṉṟiyapiṉ, uṭaṉēyō kālañceṉṟō, ōr iṭam nōkkic celavu allatu palaticiyum paṭarcci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. iyaṟkiyāṉa celavellām muṉṉōkkiyatē yātaliṉ, tōṉ ṟalum celavum, muṟiyē, muṉvarukiyum muṟcelavu mēyākum. iv viru poruṭkum aṭippaṭiyāṉa muṉmik karutti ūkārac cuṭṭē uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ūkāratti allatu ukaratti olikkumpōtu, itaḻ kuvintu muṉṉiṭattic cuṭṭuvati allatu nōkkuvatik kāṇka. cēmparār āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akara KjÈÆš(Chamber’s Etymological Dictionary), move eṉṉum coṟku moḻipporuṭkaraṇiyam itaḻ muṉṉōkkik kuvin tolittal eṉṟu kuṟittiruttilik kūrntu nōkkuka.
muṟcellaccella, cēravēṇṭiya iṭattiṟku nerukkam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. cērntapiṉ poruntal nērkiṉṟatu. iṭivaḻiyiṟ cuvarum kallum maliyumpōlat taṭi ēṟpaṭiṉ, vaḷiya allatu pakkamākat tirumpa nērkiṉṟatu. taṭuttaporuḷiyum iṭattiyum tuḷikka muṭiyumāyiṉ, eli cuvariyum māntaṉ maliyiyum tuḷittal nērkiṉṟatu.. tuḷittu maṟupuṟaṅ kāṇiṉ atuvē turuval. ataṉpiṉ tōṉṟal mutaliya paḻiya nilimikaḷē mīṇṭum nikaḻum. maṇṇil vēr iṟaṅkutalum, marattil āṇi patitalum, pottakattiṟ puḻuvarittalum pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷāyiṉ, vaḷitaliṉṟiyē tuḷit talum turuvalum nikaḻum.
irutiṇi yuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyiyum nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ ceyalkaḷellām iv veṇvakik karuttu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyē amikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟik kūrntu kavaṉitta paṇṭit tamiḻar, tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṉāl, itupō tuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟṟokutiyuḷ ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟpaṅki ūkārac cuṭṭaṭiyākavē tōṟṟuvittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.

ukaraccuṭṭuccoṟkaḷellām paṭarkkipōṟ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭiṉum, pēcuvāṉukku muṉṉiṭattiyum atiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyumē cuṭṭum. ātalāl ukaraccuṭṭu eṉṟum muṉmic cuṭṭēyaṉṟi, cēymikkum aṇmikkum iṭip paṭṭa iṭimic cuṭṭaṉṟu. itu nīṇṭakālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil ōraḷavu vaḻakkiluḷḷatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭiccoṟkaḷ
ukaraccuṭṭuvēr taṉittum ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma eṉṉum aṟu moḻimuta leḻuttukaḷōṭu kūṭiyum varum. ñavayameykaḷ ukarattōṭukūṭi moḻimutal varāmiyāl, avi piṟavuyir kaḷōṭu kūṭi moḻimutal varum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa, ukarac cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷiṉ tiripākavē yirukkum.  uyirukaram mutalāka varuvatu mutalaṭi; uyirmey yukaram mutalāka varuvatu vaḻiyaṭi. collākkattil, poruḷmayakkatti nīkkutaṟku, uyirum meyyum palvē ṟeḻuttāyt tirikiṉṟaṉa. at tiripukaḷellām caṟṟup piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum. iṅku, uyirttiripil u-i, u-o eṉpavaṟṟiyum, meyttiripil l-r, l-ḷ, ḻa-ka eṉpavaṟṟiyum uḷḷattil iruttutalvēṇṭum.
1. tōṉṟutal (muṉvarutal)

	6. nilattil tōṉṟum poruḷ. karuveṉa moḻipa           (tol. 964)

JU:	turu-turuttu eṉpatu iṉṟu muṉtaḷḷutalik kuṟikkum.
	nuru = piñcu. nuru-noru = iḷaṅkatir, piñcu.
puru: 	puru = kuḻanti. puru-(piru)-piruku = muṟṟāp paṉaṅkiḻaṅku.
	piruku - pirukku.
	ciṟu piñcukaḷip piñcum pirukkum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
muru:	muru-muruku = iḷami, iḷamiyāṉa murukaṉ. muruku - murukaṉ. muru-muruntu = kuruttu, taḷir.
	muru-muṟi=taḷir. muṟital = tuḷirttal. muṟinta kōlamukiḻ (cīvaka. 2358)
	muru - maru - maruvu. maruvutal = tōṉṟutal.
uru eṉṉum aṭikku muntiyatu ul eṉpatākum. lakaram  ḷakaramākat tirivatu pōṉṟē ḷakaram ḻakaramākat tiriyum.
	ul-ullari = taḷir, ulavi=taḻi. īyulaviyiṉōppi (iraku. tēṉu. 35).
	kul - kuḷ - kuḷaku = taḷir, taḻi. kuḷ - kuṭṭi. kuḷ - kuḻa - kuḻi =    taḷir. kuḻa - koḻuntu. kuḻa - kuḻavu - kuḻaku - kuḻakaṉ. kuḻa - kuḻanti. kuḻavu - kuḻavi.
	cul-cūl = karu, muṭṭi. cul-cil-ciṉṉa-ciṉi. ciṉittal = tōṉṟutal, arumputal, karukkoḷḷu tal, taḻittal. ciṉi=  moṭṭu, marakkiḷi, cūl, muṭṭi.
	tul-tuḷ-tuḷir-taḷir. tuḷ-taḷ-taḻu-taḻukku, taḻukkutal= taḻittal. taḻu-taḻi = kuḻi.
	nul - (nuḷ) - nuḻu - nuḻuval = iḷampākku. nuḻu - nuḻāy = iḷam pākku, nul-(nuṉ)-nuṉi-naṉi = arumpu. naṉital = arumputal, tōṉṟutal.
	pul-(pal)-pallavam = (va.) = taḷir, pul-(puḷ)-piḷ-piḷḷi . piḷ = piḷḷimiyaḻaku, piḷ - pīḷ = iḷaṅkatir, iḷami, karu. puḷ-pūḷ-pūṭṭi = iḷaṅkatir. pūṭṭi - pīṭṭi.
	mul-muḷ-muḷi. muḷittal = tōṉṟutal. muḷiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ-muḷ-muṭṭu-moṭṭu. muḷ-maḷ-maḷḷaṉ =   iḷiñaṉ.  maḷ - maḻa - maḻavu - maḻavaṉ = iḷiñaṉ. 
maḻavum  kuḻavum iḷamip poruḷa 	(tol.uri.14)
	maḻa - maḻali - matali = iḷami, piḷḷi.   
	ḻakaram cilaviṭattuk kakaramākat tiriyum.
	e-ṭu: toḻuti - tokuti, muḻi-muki.
	ivvakiyil uḻu eṉṉum aṭi uku eṉṟākum.
uku -  (uki) - aki. akital = taḷirttal. 		      
koykuḻi akikāñci  	(kalit. 74)
	kuku - kuki - kukiccu (puṟṟu)
	cuku - cuki (kottal, kiḻittal) - cukam (kiḷi)
	tuku - (teku) - tiki - (muṭivu, elli)
	nuku - nukumpu = paṉaṅkuruttu. nuku - nuṅku = paṉañcuḷi, 		iḷampaṉaṅkoṭṭi.
	puku - 	pokil = arumpu. pokil - pōkil = arumpu. 			pōku - pōkku = marakkaṉṟu (piṅ.)
	puku - 	pō-pōttu = iḷaṅkiḷi.
		pō - pōntu (piṅ.) = iḷampaṉi. pōntu-pōnti 		= (tivā.) iḷampaṉi.
	muku - mukiḻ arumpu. mukiḻtal = arumputal. mukiḻttal = 		tōṉṟutal, arumputal, īṉutal. muku-muki = arumpu. 		muku- mokku-mokkuḷ = arumpu.
	mukiḻ - mukiḻam = pērarumpu. mukiḻ - mukiḻi. mukiḻittal = 		arumpu pōṟ kuvital.
mukiḻam eṉṉum col, arumpu eṉṉum poruḷil, mukula, mukura, makula, makura eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
kumarikkaṇṭa muḻukkiṉālum, mutaliru kaḻaka vilakkiya muḻutum kaṭikkaḻaka vilakkiyap perumpakutiyum aḻintu pōṉamiyālum nīṇṭakālamākat tamiḻar tamiḻip pēṇāmi yālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉālēyē, pala mutalaṭic coṟkaṭkum vaḻiyaṭic coṟkaṭkum eṭuttukkāṭṭa iyalavilli yeṉṟaṟika. iṟantupaṭṭa coṟka ḷellām piṟikkōṭṭuḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷa. oru poṟiyaṟiñaṉ oru poṟiyilillāta uṟuppik kaṇṭukoḷvatu pōṉṟē, oru moḻiyaṟiñaṉum am moḻiyil iṟantupaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi avaṟṟoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa piṟacoṟkaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu aṟiyaviyalum eṉka.
2. muṉmi
ūṅku = muṉpu
ummi = muṟpiṟappu. ummiviṉi.... oḻiyātu   (maṇimē. 26:32)
kuṉi = kūriya muṉpakkam allatu mēṟpakkam.  nuṉi - muṉi.
nuṉi - nuti = muṉ. naṭantāḷ nuti   	 (cīvaka. 1933, uri) 
nuti - tutu = muṉ, nuṉi. tutikki = yāṉiyiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa allatu kūriya nuṉiyuḷḷa ki.
muṉ -muṉi. muṉ-muṉi=muṉpakkam. muku-mukam = muṉpakkam. mukam-mukar-mukari. mukam- mukaṉ = mukaṉi-mōṉi.
mukam eṉṉum col muṉmikkaruttiyum tōṉṟaṟ karuttiyum iṇittukkāṭṭutal kāṇka. ic col vaṭamoḻiyil aramāya eṉṟu tiriyum.
mukañceytal	1.	muṉṉātal. tōṟṟiṉāṉ 		 		mukañcey	kōlam       	 (cīvaka.675)

3. muṉcellutal (cellutal)
ukital = cellutal, ukittal = celuttutal.
uki-aki. akittal = celuttutal. utittal = celuttutal.
iccili yutittakōṟ kilakkam  	(kamparā. kārmuka.9)
untutal = celuttutal.
uytal = cellutal, tappic cellutal. uyttal =   celuttutal 
uy-oy. oytal=celuttutal,
uy-iy-iyal. iyalutal=cellutal, naṭattal.
iy -iya. iyattal = cellutal, kaṭattal.
iya - iyavu = celavu.
iya - iyaṅku. iyaṅkutal = acital, cellutal.
iya-ē-ēku. o.nō: viyar- vēr.
(cul)-cal-cel.
ca-ce. o.nō: cattāṉ-cettāṉ.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻil, cal eṉṉum aṭiyum cellutal viṉiyik kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟu atu ap poruḷil, vaṭatiraviṭa vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
turattal = celuttutal. turattutal = virivākac celuttutal.
muṉmikkum muṟcelaviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa karuttu muṉ taḷḷutal.
muṉtaḷḷutal
uttutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, kaḻittal.
untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal. untal - uñcal - ūñcal- ūcal.
uṉṉutal = muṉ taḷḷutal.
(tuḷ)-taḷ. o.nō: tuḷir-taḷir.
tuntu - tunti = muṉ taḷḷiya vayiṟu. tunti - tonti.
turuttutal = tonti vayiṟupōl muṉ taḷḷutal.
turutti = kāṟṟi muṉ taḷḷum kollulit tōṟkaruvi  allatu icikkaruvi. turutti - tutti - titti = icikkaruvi.
tūṇṭutal = viḷakkut tiriyi muṉ taḷḷutal. tūṇṭu = tīṇṭu
nuntutal = tūṇṭutal. nuntā viḷakku=tūṇṭā viḷakku. nuntu-nontu.
nūkkutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
uḷḷatti muṉ taḷḷutal (ūkkutal)
uy-uyal-uyaṟṟu-uñaṟṟu.
uñaṟṟutal = muyaṟcikkut tūṇṭutal, varunti yuḻittal.
uḷḷutal = uḷḷatti ūkkutal. uḷḷam=ūkkam.
ūkkutal = uḷḷatti muṟceluttutal. ūṅku-ūkku.
tūṇṭutal = ūkkutal, ēvutal.
nūkkutal = tūṇṭutal.
muyalutal = taṉ uḷḷatti ūkkutal.
Push, shoot, usher, urge, duct, duke, thrust mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷiyum ukara vaṭiviyum, ivaṟṟuṭaṉ oppu nōkka.
4. neruṅkutal, ceṟital, kūṭutal
pala poruḷ neruṅkutal ceṟitalām.

(kuḷ)-(kuṭṭu)-kiṭṭu. kiṭṭutal = neruṅkutal.
(cuḷ)-(ceḷ)-ceṟu. ceṟuttal = ceṟital.
ceṟu-ceṟi.
tul-tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = neruṅkutal. tuṉ - tuṉṟu. tuṉṟutal =  neruṅkutal. 
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ. naḷḷutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal.        	           naṇṇutal = neruṅkutal.
(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku.
mul - muḷ - muru - maru - maruvu. maruvutal = kiṭṭutal.
mul- mal - mali - malital = neruṅkutal. niṟital. mikutal.
(muḷ) - (maḷ) - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. 
maṇṭu - maṇṭakam = makkaḷ kūṭum kārikkūṭam. maṇṭakam - maṇṭapam-(va.) maṇṭapa.
o-nō: vāṇikam-vāṇipam.
5. poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal
ul-u
uttal=poruntutal. utti=viḷiyāṭṭil iruvar  cērntu varum cērkki, aṟiviṟkup poruttamāṉa ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟi.
ul-ol. ollutal=poruntutal. ol-oṉ.
oṉṉutal = poruntutal. oṉ-oṉṟu.
oṉṟutal = poruntutal. ol-(oḷ)-oṇ.
oṇṇutal = poruntutal. oḷ-oṭṭu.
ol-o. ottal = poruntutal.
kul-kulavu. kulavutal = kūṭutal.
kul-(kuḷ)-kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = ottal, kūṭutal.
kaḷḷa = pōla. kaḷ-kaḷam = kūṭṭam kūṭumiṭam. ērkkaḷam,  pōrkkaḷam, avikkaḷam eṉpavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
(kuḷ)-kuḻu - kuḻuvu. kuḻu - kuḻumal = kūṭṭam.
kuḻu-kuḻumpu = tiraḷ. kuḻu - kuḻām = kumpal.
(kuḷ) - (kūḷ) - kūṇṭu - kūṭu - kūṭṭam.
(cuḷ) - ceḷ - ceru - cēr.
tul - tulliyam = oppu. tul - tuli = oppu.
tul - tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = poruntutal.
tul - tuṉṟu. tuṉṟutal = poruntutal.
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ - naṇpu. naḷ - naṭpu.
pullutal = poruntutal, ottal, taḻuvutal, kūṭutal.
pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu - pottu  - poṭṭu.
pol - pōl. pōlutal = ottal.
poru - poruvu = oppu.
pul - puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇar. (puṇ) - pūṇ - pūṭṭu.
pul - (pur) - puri. purital = ottal.
mul - muḷ - muḷku. muḷkutal = taḻuvutal.

6. vaḷital
ul - ulā - ulavu - ulāvu. ulavutal = cuṟṟivarutal.
kul - kulā - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷital.
kul - kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṭu - kōṭṭam.
cul - culā - culavu. culavutal = vaḷital.
tul - (til) - tir - tirum. tirumutal = vaḷital, cāytal, mīḷutal.
tirum - tirumpu.
(nuḷ) - neḷ - neḷi - neṟi.
neḷital = uṭal vaḷital. neṟital = mayir curuḷtal.
pul - puru - puri. puru - puruvam = kaṇ mēlmayir vaḷivu.
purital = vaḷital. valampuri iṭampuri eṉṉum caṅkukaḷi nōkkuka.
(mul) - muru - muruku = vaḷinta kātaṇi. muru - muṟu - muṟṟu.
muṟṟutal = vaḷital, cūḻtal, muṟṟukiyiṭutal.
muru - muri. murital = vaḷital.
ul - (ur) - uruḷ, to roll. uruḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu kuruḷ (to curl), curuḷ (to coil) mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantirut taliyum, av vaṭiyi whirl, swirl, twirl eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟkaḷ oruvāṟot tiruttaliyum nōkkuka. puruḷ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu puruṭi (ãUil, turning key of a lute string) eṉṉum collum, muruḷ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu KUF(knot in wood) eṉṉum collum, tirintuḷḷaṉa. uḻal (to revolve) eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu FHš(to curl), RHš(to rotate) eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttalum nōkkat takkatākum.
7. tuḷittal
uḷ - uḷu = tuḷikkum puḻu. uḷuttal = puḻu marattit tuḷittal.
uḷu - uḻu. uḻutal = nilattik kīṟutal.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻi - kuṭi. kuṭital = tuḷittal.
kuḻi - koḻu - koḻutu - kōtu. kōtutal = kuṭital.
koḻu = nilattit tuḷikkum ērūci.
koḻu-kōḻi = kiḷikkum paṟavi.
kuḷ- kuḷi. kuḷittal = uṭpukutal, muḻukutal, nīrāṭal.
cuḷ - cura - curi = uṭṭuḷi. cura - curaṅkam.
curikkāy = kāyntapiṉ uṭṭuḷiyamiyuṅ kāy.
tul - tuṉ = elivaḷi. tuṉṉal = uḻutal.
tul - tuḷ - tuḷi. tuḷ - toḷ - toḷḷi - toḷi.
toḷ - tōḷ - tōṇṭu. toḷ - toṭu. toṭutal = tōṇṭutal. 
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷḷi = kuḻivu, kaṭkuḻivu.
noḷ-noḷḷal-ñeḷḷal  = paḷḷam.
nul-nuḻi - nūḻi = tuḷi, vāyil.
pul = uṭṭuḷi, uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷa payirvaki allatu                               mūṅkil. pullāṅkuḻal = mūṅkiṟkuḻal.
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa.      	(tol. 1585)
pul-puḷ-puḻu = tuḷikkum ciṟṟuyir. puḻuttal = 	puḻuttuḷittal.
puḷ-poḷ-poḷi-poṟi.
poḷḷal = tuḷi, tuḷittal. poḷittal = tuḷittal, veṭṭutal, kiḻittal.
poṟittal = kuḻitteḻututal.
muḷ-(muḻu) = muḻuku - mūḻku. muḻukutal = uṭpukutal, nīriṟ kuḷittal.
(muḻu)-muḻi = kuki. muḻittal = tuḷittal.
muḻi-mūḻi = akappi.
muḻuku-muḻuṅku-muṅku. muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
viḻuṅkutal = vāykkuḷ muḻukkutal.
muḷ - moḷ - moṇṭi-monti.
moṇṭi - maṇṭi. moḷḷutal = nīrpporuḷukkuḷ muḻukki yeṭuttal.
8. turuvutal
uḷ-uru - uruvu. uruvutal = kikkuḷ oṉṟu turuvumāṟu iḻuttal.
kuḷ-(kuru)-kōr-kō. kōrvi-kōvi.
kōrttal = nūl ūcik kātilum pācit tuḷiyilum turuvac ceytal.
cuḷ-curu. curuṅki = nilattiṉkīḻt turuvic cellum vaḻi.
curu-curuvu-curuvam = uṇavuk kalattuḷ turuvum akappi.
tuḷ-turu-turuvu. turu-tūr - tūrtal = ūṭu cellutal, vācaluṭ pukutal. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukutalit tūrtal eṉpatu vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaḻakku.
tūr-tūri = nīrturuvic cellum mīṉpoṟi.
tūr-tōr - tōraṇam = teruvūṭu kaṭṭum aḻakut toṅkal.
tōr-tōraṇi=kōrvi.
puḷ-puḻu-puku-pukutu-pukuru-pūr. pūrtal = uṭpukutal.
pūr-pūrāṉ = maṇṇiṟkuḷ pukum naccuppūcci. pūr - pūṟu. pūṟutal = uruvattuḷittal. mūkkuppūṟi-mūkkuppīṟi. pūṟu-  pīṟu.
mul-mūlam=vāyil (means).
3. tamiḻ vaḷarcci
i urinaṭi
kiḷaviyākkam
1. coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷ
kiḷavi eṉpatu nālvaki ilakkaṇaviyaṟ colliṟkum potuvāṉa col. kiḷattal-collutal. kiḷappatu kiḷavi.
māntaṉ nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarak karuttukaḷ palkukiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭik karuttukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷ kiḷikkum pōtu, muṉṉavaṟṟik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyil tiripaṭikiṉṟaṉa. allākkāl palaporuḷoru brhšY«(polysemy) palacol byhUtoî«(homonymy) mikkup poruḷmayakku ēṟpaṭum. atit taṭukkac colvaṭivi māṟṟa vēṇṭiyuḷatu. cuḷ eṉṉum aṭi poruntaṟ poruḷiṟ ceḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, cuṭutaliyum uṟittaliyum kuṟikkuñ colloṭu ati mayakka nērum. ceḷ eṉṉum vaṭiviṉiṉṟē ceṇṭu, ceṇṭi, ceṭi, ceṇṇu, ceru, ceṟu, ceṟi, ceṟṟi, ceḻi, cēr mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. nuḷ eṉpatu neruṅ kaṟporuḷil naḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, kiḷḷutalik kuṟikkum colloṭu ati mayakka nērum. mēlum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa karuttukaṭku vevvēṟu collum colvaṭivum vēṇṭiyiruppa tāl, vērccollum aṭiccollum palvēṟu tiripukoṇṭā loḻiya moḻi vaḷarccikkiṭamilli.
(1) aṟuvakit tiripu
	e-ṭu:	valittal	:	ūṅku-ūkku, kuṇṭu-kuṇṭam-kuṭṭam.
                   			   nantu-natti, kuṟiñci-kuṟicci.
		melittal	:	oppu-ompu, kuttu-kuntu, pōkku - pōṅku.
		nīṭṭal	:	uṇ-ūṇ, kuṭa-kuṭā. nakarakam-nākarikam.
		kuṟukkal	:	āṅku-aṅku, tēvu-teyvam, vaṇaṅku-vāṅku. vaṅku-vaṅki=vaḷinta katti,     	neḷi vaḷiyal.
		tokuttal	:	turutti-tutti, peṭṭi-peṭi, veymmi- vemmi, vēyntōṉ-vēntaṉ.
		virittal	:	paravar-paratavar, māṭam-māṭakam.
(2) mukkuṟi
		mutaṟkuṟi	:	cippi-ippi, nīram-īram, uki-ki.	kittal=celuttutal.
		iṭikkuṟi	:	kūṇṭu-kūṭu, muḻuṅku-muṅku.
		kaṭikkuṟi	:	cāy-cā, nal-na.
(3) mummiki
		mutaṉmiki : ēṇ-cēṇ, iḷi-ciḷi.
		iṭimiki : ilaku-ilaṅku, piṟaku-piṟakku, mūcu-			mūñcu, metu-mettu

(6) palvaki īṟukaḷ
poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭu meṉpatu, collākka aṭippaṭi neṟimuṟi. allāk kāl, poruḷ atuvō ituvō eṉa mayaṅka nērum. maṇ eṉṉuñ col maṇalik kuṟit taṟku allīṟu peṟṟu maṇal eṉṟāṉamiyum, kampu eṉṉuñ col, marakkōlik kuṟiyātu kaṉiya (ulōka)k kōlik kuṟittaṟku ikara vīṟu peṟṟuk kampiyeṉ ṟāṉamiyum kāṇka. pakkattiyum iṭuppi yum kuṟikkum maruṅku eṉṉum col, maruṅkul eṉa ullīṟu peṟṟē taṉippaṭa iṭuppik kuṟittatu.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya īṟukaḷ, cuṭṭukaḷum cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷumākavē yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ, mutaṟ kālattil peyareccamāka maṭṭumaṉṟip peyarākavum irun tirukkiṉṟaṉa.
v-L: M=mJ(bg.), anta (pe.e.). ikkālattum atupōtu eṉṟu, cuṭṭuppeyar peyareccamāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
uyirolikaḷit taṉittolikkumpōtu, kuṟiliṉum neṭilē iḷañciṟārkkum muntiyal māntarkkum eḷitām. kuṟili  viṭṭolikkumpōtu mūccuppi muyaṟci mikku vēṇṭi yiruttaliṉ, kīḻvakuppu māṇavar kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyē uyireḻuttu kaḷi olittup pōtal kāṇka. itaṉāl uyireḻut tukaḷ mutalil neṭilākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
a i uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu 		(31)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār, caṟṟup piṉpu,
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōrēḻut torumoḻi. 	(43)
kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē 	(44)
eṉṟu ati orumaruṅku maṟuttiruppatum. viṉā veḻuttukaḷik kuṟikkumiṭattu,'
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa 	(32)
eṉa ekaratti viṭṭiruppatum, tolkāppiyam neṭukalum āyiṭi'' eṉṉum toṭarē payiṉṟu varuvatum, āṇṭu īṇṭu eṉṉum coṟkaṭkuk kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉmiyum, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku iṉakkuṟil pintit tōṉṟiyuḷḷamiyum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum.
cuṭṭīṟukaḷ
ā-uṇā, nilā, irā, viḷā, kaḷā, nuṇā, kaṭā, miḻā. 
ī-kurī (kurīi = kuruvi).
ū-koḷ-koḷum-koḷumū-koṇmū(mukil).
ciṟu paṟavi (kuruvi) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum kurī eṉṉum collip piṟkālattār kurīi eṉa aḷapeṭiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
koṇmū= kaṭalnīrik koḷḷum mukil. koḷḷutal= mukattal. koḷumutal = mukattal. itaṉ viḷakkattic coṟ paṭi vaḷarcci eṉṉum talippiṉkīḻk kāṇka.
āvīṟu muṟiyē a, eṉat tiriyum.

iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyiru cuṭṭaṭikaṭkum oṭṭikkoḷka. eṭuttuk kāṭṭillaṉa iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
iṟantupaṭṭirukkum akaraccuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaṭ kotta ikara allatu ukarac cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷuṭ cilapala, iṟantu paṭātirukka lām.
e-ṭu: itam-tappitam	icci-puḷicci
         iccu-variccu	icci-paṉicci
         iccal-variccal	iñci-kuṟiñci
         iccam-veḷiccam      
aku, acu, aṭu, atu, apu, avu, aṟu; akku, accu, aṭṭu, attu, appu, aṟṟu ; aṇ, am, ar, al, aḻ, aḷ, aṉ eṉṉum īṟukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟ kotta ikaravukaramutal vaṭivukaḷumē, iṉṟu muṟiyē ku, cu, ṭu, tu, pu,vu, ṟu; kku, ccu, ṭṭu, ttu, ppu, ṟṟu; ṇ, m, r, l, ḻ, ḷ, ṉ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām uṇmiyiṟ cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṉ, aḷ, ar, atu, avi, a eṉpaṉa pālīṟukaḷalla; aṇ, aḻ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu iyalpāṉa potu vīṟukaḷē.
cila iṭappeyarkaḷ cilaviṭattuc cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ pōl tōṉṟum.  avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu	: 	cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	akam-kaḻakam eṉpatiṟpōl
	 	oru peyarīṟu (aku+am).
		iṭappeyar	:	akam = maṉi, iṭam. kallakam = 						kalvic cāli (kaṟkumiṭam), 						kaṉmali, kalluḷḷa iṭam.
		cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	iṭam-ōr īṟu, e-ṭu: kaṭṭiṭam = maṉi.
		iṭappeyar	:	iṭam-oru peyarccol.
				e-ṭu:	iruppiṭam = irukkumiṭam. 						kaṭṭiṭam = kaṭṭukiṉṟa iṭam.
ōr īṟu pala vakiyil tōṉṟalām.
e-ṭu	:	avu	:       	irā-ira-iravu, cel-celavu
		aṉ	:       	am-aṉ, al-aṉ.
       		antu	:	am-antu, atu-antu,attu-antu.
pala coṟkaḷil lakaram takaramākat tirivatālum, mel eṉṉuñ col metu eṉṟu tirintiruppatālum, atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu al eṉpataṉ tiripākavē koḷḷap paṭṭatu. aḵtu eṉpatu atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟkiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉpataṉ vali iraṭṭiya vaṭivamāṉa attu eṉpatu, paḵtu eṉpatōṭoppa āyta miṭiyiṭṭa maṟuvaṭivu koṇṭateṉ patē poruttamām.metu metteṉṟāyatupōl atu atteṉ ṟāyatu; patu patteṉṟāyatu. āytatti yaṭutta takaram, aḵṟiṇi, kaḵṟītu, paḵṟi, paḵṟuḷi eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl ṟakaramākat tirivatē puṇarcciyilakkaṇa mātalāl, aḵtu paḵtu eṉpavaṟṟi luḷḷa āytam iyalpāṉa iṭiyeḻut taṉṟeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iṭikkālat tirippeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
collīṟukaḷ tiriyumpōtu, meli valiyākalām; vali meli yākalām.
e-ṭu:  nīṅku-nīkku, pōkku-pōṅku.
palvakik kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ
accam, virivu, ōci, oḷi, vaṇṇam, acivu, ūṟu mutaliya palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ, ōroḻuṅku paṭṭa īṟukaḷik koṇṭirāviṭiṉum, makkaḷ karuttit teḷivāyp pulappaṭuttaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 
accakkuṟippu	-	kapīr, tiṭukku, tuṇukku, verukku.
virivukkuṟippu	-	avakku, kupīl-kupīr, carēl-carēr, tiṭum,			tiṭīr , paṭakku, pucukku, pocukku, vicukku, 			viruṭṭu, veṭukku.
ōcikkuṟippu	-	kaṇīr, kiṟiccu, kīccu, caḷār, taṭāl-taṭār,
			turuṭṭu, puḷiccu, paṭār, molōr.
oḷikkuṟippu	-	paḷiccu, paḷīr, miṉukku.
vaṇṇakkuṟippu	-	civīr, cevēr, veḷēl-veḷēr.
acivukkuṟippu	-	kiṇukku, kiṇukkaṭṭi, cavakku, 					totukkup putukku.
ūṟṟukkuṟippu	-	kaṭukku, cuṟukku, cuḷīr, parapara, potukku, 			noḷukku, metukku.
(7) valiyiraṭṭal
e-ṭu: paku-pakkam, pēcu-pēccu, neṭu-neṭṭi, karutu-karuttu, kuṟu-kuṟṟam, kuṟṟi. neṟu-neṟṟi.
eḻuttiraṭṭal (Gemination or Doubling of Consonants) eṉṉum collamiti, āriya moḻik kuṭumpak kiḷikaḷuḷ airōp pāviṉ  vaṭamēlik kōṭiyiluḷḷatum, āṅkilatti uḷḷiṭ ṭatumāṉa, tiyūttāṉiyattiṟkē ciṟappāṉateṉṟu (i) riccārṭu māricu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
taṉikkuṟiliyaṭutta mey uyirōṭu puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭu vatu, tamiḻukkum āṅkilattiṟkum potuvām.
e-ṭu: tamiḻ-col+aḻaku=collaḻaku, iṉ+ici= iṉṉici
E. thin+er = thinner, sit+ing = sitting.
(8) pōli (Interchange of Letters)
oru collil, ōriṭattil, oru taṉiyeḻuttiṟku allatu iṇiyeḻuttiṟkup pakaramāka(patilāka) muṟiyē maṟṟoru taṉiyeḻuttu allatu iṇiyeḻuttu vantu, poruḷ māṟātiruppatu pōliyām. pōliruppatu pōli. pōlutal-ottal. iṭam nōkki mutaṟpōli, iṭippōli, kaṭippōli eṉap pōli mūvakippaṭum.
e-ṭu:	mutaṟpōli	:	nāyiṟu-ñāyiṟu, maṭali-vaṭali.
	iṭippōli	:	kuṭavaṉ-kucavaṉ-kuyavaṉ,neyavu- 			necavu,aracu aricu, nīntu-nīñcu.
	kaṭippōli	:	kaṭam-kaṭaṉ, kūtal-kūtar.
poruḷ māṟātirukkum pōliyāl, ceyyuḷil etuki yamippiṟkaṉṟi moḻivaḷarccikkiṭamilli. āyiṉ, pōli muṟiyit taḻuvip poruḷ vēṟupaṭum coṟkaḷ moḻiyi mika vaḷampaṭuttum.
e-ṭu:	tarukku-cerukku, paku-vaku, mīṟu-vīṟu, katali-kacaḷi,kuḻal-kuṭal , paḻam-payam-payaṉ, vāyil-vācal, motti- mocci, iṭam -iṭaṉ, nāḷi-nāḻi, nāḷi-nāṭi.
ilakkaṇappōli (Metathesis)
oru taṉiccolliṉ eḻuttukaḷum oru kūṭṭuc colliṉ uṟuppukaḷ allatu uṟuppuccoṟkaḷum, muṉ piṉṉāka muṟimāṟi  variṉ ilakkaṇappōli eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	taṉiccol	:	alari-araḷi, koppuḷam- 				pokkuḷam, cati-taci, ñimiṟu-			miñiṟu, viciṟi- civiṟi.
	kūṭṭuccol	:	ilvāy-vāyil, ilmuṉṟu-				muṉṟil- muṟṟam, katuvāli-				kavutāri, tāṉimuṉ-muṉṟāṉi.
(9) mūvakic coṟkiṭakki
taṉiccol, kūṭṭuccol, toṭarccol eṉac coṟ kiṭakki mūvakippaṭum. avi muṟiyē oru collum iru collum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pala collumākum .
e-ṭu:	nilam; taṇṇīr; ceṅkāl nāri, vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa 			pāṇṭiyaṉ.
kūṭṭuccol vakikaḷ

  (2)	iraṭṭitta col	,,	cekkac cevēr,ciṉṉañciṟu

  (6)	mikavuccol	,,	āṇmakaṉ,peṇ peṇṭāṭṭi  
  (7)	muraṇpaṭucol	,,	peṇmakaṉ, peṭṭippacaṉkaḷ
  (8)	iṇimoḻi	,,	kāykaṟi, vāḻvutāḻvu
  (9)	aṭipeṟṟa col	,,     	naṉcey, talikkaḻakam

	(eccattoṭar) 		intiyoḻiyiṉ

	(muṟṟuttoṭar) 		uyarvāṉ
aṭukkuttoṭar oṟṟiṭi mikkum tirintum tokkum varum.
e-ṭu:	palappala, paṟpala, meṉmēlum, nannāṉku, vevvēṟu. 	cila coṟkaḷ taṉiccoṟkum kūṭṭuccoṟkum potuvāyirukkum.
oru poruḷ tantu oru collāyum iruporuḷ tantu iru collāyum iruppatu potuccol.
aḷittēṉ = tantēṉ (orucol), vaṇṭuttēṉ (iru col).
ceyvāṉ = purivāṉ (orucol), ceyaṟki maḻi (iru col).
toṭarccol vakikaḷ
(1)	aṭukkukkiḷavi	e-ṭu:	kuṭukuṭukuṭu.
(2)	mīmiciccol    	  ,,	mutupaḻantoṉ(moḻi).
(3)	aṭukkuttoṭar             	,,	tīttīttī.
(4)	mikavuccol                	,,	ariñāṇkayiṟu, peṇ 					piḷḷip piḷḷi.
(5)	aṭipeṟṟa col 	,,        	tūṅkeyileṟinta 						toṭittōṭcempiyaṉ, 					maturikkaṇakkāyaṉār 					makaṉār nakkīrar.

(10) poruṭkēṟṟa oliyamippu
potuvāka, mellōcic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ meṉmi yiyum  vallōcic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ vaṉmi yiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:	kuttutal = catiyuḷḷa iṭattil muṭṭiyāl allatu kattiyāl tākkutal. kuṭṭutal = vaṉmiyāṉa maṇṭiyil muṭṭiyāl tākkutal.
	muttutal = mukattōṭu allatu vēṟuṟuppoṭu mukam mellap poruntutal. muṭṭutal = maṇṭi yoṭu maṇṭi iṭittal.
	arital = ciṟitāy naṟukkutal. aṟuttal =  peritāy  naṟukkutal. arivatu arivāḷ; aṟuppatu aṟuvāḷ. 
	kaṟittal = kaṉṟu iḷampulli metuvāyk  kaṭittal. kaṭittal = peritāṉa vilaṅku mutirnta payiri valitākak kaṭittal.
	caṉṉam = melliyatu, tiṇṇam = tiraṇṭatu.
	kuñcu = mellitāṉa iḷampaṟavi. kuccu =  vallitāṉa ciṟukōl.
	tiṇṭu = melliya pañcaṇi, tiṭṭu =vaṉṉilamāṉa ciṟumēṭu.
	karuppu = iyalpāṉa karuniṟam. kaṟuppu-ciṉattāl allatu mutircciyāl nērum iruṇṭa niṟam.
(11) marūu
cila taṉiccoṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum toṭarc coṟkaḷum,  vaḻakkāṟṟil maruvi, atāvatu ilakkaṇiyarāl oppukkoḷḷap peṟum vakiyil kuṟukiyum tirintum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
taṉiccol : elumpu-eṉpu, teṉku-teṅku, naṭuvaṇ-nāppaṇ, nerunal-nerunaṟṟu-nēṟṟu, pakuti-pāti, peyar-pēr, perumakaṉ- perumāṉ-pemmāṉ, pirāṉ; poḻutu-pōḻtu-pōtu, yāṉi-āṉi, viyar-vēr.
kūṭṭuccol : ariyuḻakku-āḻākku, cumiyaṭi-cumaṭu-cummāṭu, cōḻa nāṭu - cōṇāṭu, teṅkaṅkāy-tēṅkāy, pukaviṭu-pukaṭṭu-pōṭṭu, puḻikki-pūḻkki-pūṭki, pōkaviṭu-pōkaṭu-pōṭu, vari yāṭu-varuṭi, māryāppu-mārāppu, mukakkūṭu-mukkāṭu.
toṭarccol
arumaruntaṉṉa-arumānta-arumanta.
karivalam vanta nallūr-karuvi.
pūṭki, pōṭu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷ, mūlaniliyil kūṭṭuc collāyiṉum, marūu niliyil orucoṟpōl niṉṟu orē poruḷ tarutalāl, taṉiccollākavē koḷḷappaṭum.

	tiru 	tirumāl,  tirunāvukkaracu, 				tirukkuṟaḷ, tirunīṟu, 					    tiruvaraṅkam.

tamiḻakattil piṟappōṭu toṭarpuṭiya āriyak kulap piriviṉi ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉiṟa vuyirikaḷ pārppāra eṉṟum karuniṟa vuyirikaḷ paṟi eṉṟum aṭipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:	pārppāra nākam, paṟi nākam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpik kāṭṭum aṭimoḻi, ap poruḷkaḷic ciṟappākak kiyāḷum vakuppāriṉ peyarākum.
e-ṭu: 	kuṟavaṉ pōkaṇi = periya pōkaṇi. araca  maruttuvam = aracarukkuc ceyyum vilakkūṇillā maruttuvam.
cila karuvippeyarkaḷiṉ aṭimoḻi, ak karuviyāṟ ceyyap paṭum allatu ceppañ ceyyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyarākum.
e-ṭu:	kōṇiyūci = periyavūci.
(13) piṉṉoṭṭukaḷum īṟukaḷum (Suffixes) 
poruḷ	piṉṉoṭṭu	eṭuttukkāṭṭu

	ki	kaṉṉi-kaṉṉiki, kuṭi- kuṭiki- 			kuṭici

			nili-niliyam
iṭam	āram           	koṭṭāram, paṇṭāram 				(paṇṭacāli), vaṭṭāram
iṟiyilinilam	puṟam	aṭiciṟpuṟam, aṟappuṟam,
			putukkuppuṟam
(14) puṇarcci
irucoṟ puṇarvatu puṇarcci. moḻi potumakkaḷāl ākkam peṟṟatākaliṉ, ataṉ puṇarcciyum avaratu amippē. kavaṉittaṟiyappeṟātu ēṟkeṉavē yirunta coṟkūṭṭu neṟimuṟi kaḷiyē, puṇarcci neṟimoḻikaḷ (vitikaḷ) eṉa eṭuttukkūṟiṉar ilakkaṇiyar. iyalpupuṇarcci, tiripupuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iruvakippaṭum. oruvaki vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi iyalpāyiruppatu iyalpu puṇarcci; ētēṉumoru vakiyil tirivatu tiripu puṇarcci. tirital- vēṟupaṭutal. atu tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉa mūvakip paṭum. muṉṉillāta eḻuttō aciyō tōṉṟuvatu tōṉṟal; ōreḻuttu maṟṟōreḻuttāka māṟuvatu tirital; muṉṉuḷḷa eḻuttō aciyō maṟivatu keṭutal.
puṇarcciyiṟ putitāyt tōṉṟum aci, irucoṟ kaḷi allatu oru colliyum oru colluṟuppiyum cārntu niṉṟu iyippatāl (icippatāl), cāriyi eṉappaṭum.
iyalpu puṇarcci
cāttaṉ+vantāṉ = cāttaṉ vantāṉ
nalla+piyaṉ = nalla piyaṉ
pāl+iṉitu = pāliṉitu
tiripu puṇarcci
tōṉṟal (Insertion)
vāḻi+kāy = vāḻikkāy
avari+pantal = avarippantal
kaḷā+paḻam = kaḷāppaḻam,kaḷāmpaḻam
pū+ceṭi = pūcceṭi (pūkkum ceṭi)
pū+ceṭi   = pūñceṭi(aḻakiya ceṭi)
paṇatti+koṭu = paṇattik koṭu
ūrukku+pō = ūrukkuppō
vara+col = varaccol
i+nāḷ = innāḷ
veḷ+āṭu = veḷḷāṭu
nāṭu+āṇmi = nāṭṭāṇmi
ivi eḻuttut tōṉṟal.
cekkār+kuṭi = cekkārakkuṭi
puḷi+paḻam = puḷiyampaḻam
kaṇ+potti = kaṇṇāmpotti
ivi cāriyit toṉṟal.
tirital (Mutation)
nal+cey = naṉcey
veḷ+kalam = veṇkalam
cem+tāmari = centāmari
vem+nīr = vennīr
vēmpu+ili = vēppili
taṇ+nīr = taṇṇīr
uḷ+nākku = uṇṇākku
kal+tāḻi = kaṟṟāḻi
nal+nilam = naṉṉilam
uḷ+tu = uṇṭu, īṉ+tu = īṟṟu
ivi tirital.
keṭutal (Omission)
maram+vēr = maravēr
tolkāppiyam+nūl = tolkāppiya nūl
ivi keṭutal.
aṉṟu+kūli = aṟṟikkūli
maṇ+kaṭṭi = maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi
ivi tōṉṟalum tiritalum.
paṭṭiṉam = piḷḷiyār = paṭṭiṉattuppiḷḷiyār
nakaram+āṉ = nakarattāṉ
ivi tōṉṟalum keṭutalum.
vēmpu+kāy = vēppaṅkāy
paṉi+tōppu = paṉantōppu
ivi tōṉṟalum tiritalum keṭutalum.
marūup puṇarcci
cila coṟkaḷ olineṟippaṭi oḻuṅkāyp puṇarātu maruvip puṇarum. ap puṇarcci marūup puṇarcciyām.  maruvutal, neṟi tiṟamputal. 
e-ṭu: 	ātaṉ+tā = (āntā)-ānti
	akam+ki = aṅki.
	nāḻi+uri = nāḻuri-nāṭuri
	maka+kaḷ = makkaḷ
uḷḷaṅki (uḷ+aṅki) eṉpatu aṅkiyiṉ naṭuppakuti. itil am eṉpatu cāriyiyaṉṟu.
enti, tanti eṉpaṉa marūup puṇarcciyalla. ivi em+tā, tam+tā eṉap piriyum. tā eṉpatu tantiyik kuṟikkum peyarc col.
tā+tā = tātā-tāti (tantiyiṉ tanti)-iyalpuppuṇarcci
tā+tā = tāttā (tantiyiṉ tanti)-tiripuppuṇarcci
tāti eṉpatiṟ pōṉṟē, enti, tanti, ānti, pūnti    eṉpavaṟṟilum ti eṉpatu tā eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
o.nō:	empi(ṉ)	eṅki	evvi	emmuṉ	enti
	numpi(ṉ)	nuṅki	nuvvi	nummuṉ	nunti
	tampi(ṉ)	taṅki	tavvi	tammuṉ	tanti
empiṉ = emakkup piṉ piṟantāṉ. ki = taṅki (piṅ.). eṅki = emakkuc ciṟiyavaḷ. em + avvi = evvi. itil avvi eṉṉum aṉṉiyiṉ peyar aṉṉi pōlum akkiyik kuṟittatu. ic col cila paḻañceyyuḷkaḷil taṅkiyik kuṟippatu, kātal paṟṟiya marapu vaḻuvamitiyeṉa aṟika. eṉ tanti, eṉ tampi, eṉ taṅki eṉpaṉavellām vaḻakkup paṟṟiya iṭavaḻuvamitiyē.
kārkālam, vaṭakōṭu, kōvūrkiḻār, nāṭukiḻavōṉ eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, kēṭukālam, pēṟukālam, viṟakutaliyaṉ, viṟaku kāṭu, vaṭakari, maḻikālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkilum, valimikātu vaḻaṅku vatālum; nāṉku, veṭku eṉṉum tiripu vaṭivukaḷuṭaṉ nālku, veḷku eṉṉum iyalpu vaṭivukaḷum vaḻaṅki varuvatālum; palku, palporuḷ, cilkāl, valci, vaḷpu eṉac cila coṟkaḷ eṉṟum iyalpākavē yiruppataṉālum; mutaṟkālattil tōṉṟal tiritaṟ puṇarcci attuṇik kaṇṭippā yiruntatilli yeṉa uyttuṇaralām.
mūvakip puṇarcciyum oliyicivi maṭṭumaṉṟi, iru poruṭ kiṭippaṭṭa neruṅkiya toṭarpiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
(15) ōriyalamippu (Uniformity of Words)
orutuṟippaṭṭa coṟkaḷiyellām, mutalō īṟō etukiyōpaṟṟi ottavaṭivākkuvatu ōriyalamippām.
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, toṇṭu (oṉpatu), pattu, nūṟu eṉṉum mutaṟkālattu eṇṇup peyarkaḷellām ukarattil iṟutal kāṇka.
teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku, mēṟku eṉṉum ticip peyarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē.
pēti, petumpi, maṅki, maṭanti, arivi, terivi eṉṉum peṇpāṟ paruvap peyarkaḷ aikārattil iṟukiṉṟaṉa.
atō, anta, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi, ampar, aṅku, avaṇ, atōḷ-atōḷi.
itō,inta,ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivi, impar, iṅku, ivaṇ, itōḷ-itōḷi.
utō, unta, uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi, umpar, uṅku, uvaṇ, utōḷ-utōḷi.
etō, enta, evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evi, empar, eṅku, evaṇ, etōḷ-etōḷi.
ittakiya ōriyaloḻuṅkupaṭṭa cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷ vēṟem moḻiyilum illi. atā, antā, aṉṉā, aṉṉa mutaliya coṟkaṭkum iṅṅaṉamē oṭṭikkoḷka.
(16) oppumi yamippu (Analogy)
oru colliṉ etuki vaṭivil iṉṉoru colli amippatu oppumi yamippām.
etirmaṟic coṟkaḷ 
iyaṟki eṉṉum collamippip piṉpaṟṟi, ceyal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ki yīṟu cērttuc ceyaṟki eṉṟamittatu oppumi yamippē. ēṉi yeṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:
aṟam, maṟam; iṟappu, piṟappu; iti (putukkolli), muti (paḻaṅkolli); iṇaṅku, piṇaṅku; iṉpu(iṉpam), tuṉpu (tuṉpam); kuṟṟam, naṟṟam; ciṟu, peru; ciṟṟu, peṟṟu (periyatu); ciṉṉañciṟiya, peṉṉamperiya; tāy, cēy; teruḷ, maruḷ; nalam, polam; naṉcey, puṉcey; nāṭu, kāṭu; pēraṭi, cīṟaṭi; nāṭṭam, pāṭṭam; muntu, pintu.
iṉapporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nāṟṟicik  kāṟṟuppeyarkaḷuḷ, vāṭi kōṭi eṉpavum koṇṭal teṉṟal eṉpavum oppumi yamippām.
oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ
koṟṟam, veṟṟam.
toṭarpaṟṟa coṟkaḷ
viṭi-viṭali, kaṭi-kaṭali, toṭi-toṭali, muṭi-muṭali; ai-(aintu) añcu, pi-pañcu, mi-mañcu, ni-nañcu.
(17) nāṉilak karupporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nilittiṇi (tāvaram), iyaṅkutiṇi (caṅkamam) eṉṉum iruvakuppuyirikaḷuḷ, cila pala ovvoru nilattiṟkum ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum. muntiyal tamiḻar kuṟiñcinilattil vāḻntirunta pōtē, mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyar, viṉāp peyar, muṟippeyar, vaṇṇamum vaṭivum aḷavum cuviyum ūṟumpaṟṟiya kuṇap peyar, iṉṟiyamiyāta viṉic coṟkaḷ mutaliya aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ amintu viṭṭaṉa. payir pacci, vilaṅku paṟavi, uṇavu vaki, toḻilmuṟi, karuvipaṭikkalam, paḻakka vaḻakkam, koḷki kōṭpāṭu, kuṭiyiruppiṭam mutaliya palvēṟu karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa, ataṉpiṉ kuṟiñciyaṭutta mulli nilattiṟkup paraviya pōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟ kaḷ piṟantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ mulliyaṭutta marutanilattiṟkup paraviyapōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ, marutatti yaṭutta neytalnilattiṟku maruta nakar mīṉpiṭiyāḷar kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta pōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
kuṟiñciyum mulliyum mutuvēṉilil vaṟaṇṭa niliyē pāliyātaliṉ, an nilattup piṟkālat tuṇṭāṉa coṟka ḷellām toḻilum karuviyum paḻakkavaḻakkamum koḷkiyum paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kuṟiñciyaṭuttum mulliyaṭuttum neytalirukkavuṅ kūṭumā yiṉum, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu āṟṟiyoṭ ṭiyē paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyatākat terivatāl, marutanilikkup piṉpē neytal ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṟkāla māntar ceyaṟki nīrnili amikkat teriyātirunta kālattil, iyaṟki nīrnili yāṉa āṟṟiyē nampiyiruntaṉar. iṟṟit tamiḻakattiṟ pōṉṟē paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarikkaṇṭattilum, peru malit toṭarkaḷ mēlkōṭiyilē iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum pōṉṟa āṟṟi yaṭuttē makkaḷ paraviṉ, kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal eṉṟa muṟiyilēyē nilaṅkaḷi aṭintirukka muṭiyum. in nilimi iṟṟit tamiḻakat tiṟkum ēṟṟatātal kāṇka. muntiyal māntar munnili nākarikattiyum muṟiyē aṭitaṟ kēṟṟavāṟu, kuṟiñci mulli marutam ākiya munnilamum aṭuttaṭut tiruttal tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅ kaṇumilli.
veṟpaṉ, kuṟumpoṟināṭaṉ, ūraṉ, tuṟivaṉ, viṭali eṉṉum aintiṇit taṉiyūrt talivarāṭcikkup piṉ, palavūrk kiḻami pūṇṭa vēḷiriṉ kuṟunila āṭci vantu, ataṉpiṉ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉ perunili āṭci tōṉṟiyapōtu, tamiḻ aintiṇic coṟkaḷum cērnta peruvaḷa moḻiyāyiṟṟu, marutanilattil nakarkaḷ tōṉṟiya nākarikam vaḷarntu palvēṟu toḻilkaḷum kalikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ, tamiḻ mēṉmēlum vaḷarntōṅkiṟṟu.
tamiḻ aintiṇi makkaṭkum potuvāyirukkum iḵ toṉṟē, tamiḻarellām ōriṉameṉṟum, toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē vāḻntu varupavareṉṟum, toḻil taḻuvātu piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyak kulappiri viṉiyāl tūya māperun tamiḻiṉam ciṉṉa piṉṉamākac citivuṇṭateṉṟum, terivikkap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
2. poruṭṭirivu muṟikaḷ (Semantic Changes)
(1) uvamiyāku peyar
cila peyarkaḷ uvami yaṭippaṭiyil amintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	māṅkāy-māṅkāypōṉṟa neñcāṅkuli
	takkaṭṭi-takkaṭṭikkāy allatu kaṉipōṉṟa paṭṭik f£o(sty).
	erumi nākku-eruminākkup pōṉṟa mīṉ.
	kiḷimūkku-kiḷimūkku vaṭivāṉa māmpaḻam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar, paṇpiyākupeyar muṟiyil ap poruḷkaḷiṉ niṟattik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: cāmpal vāḻi, kākkip piciṉ, pavaḻac cōḷam, kurutikkāntaḷ, poṟkoṉṟi, mayiṟkaḻuttuc cēli. cila peyarkaḷ uvamit tokiyāy amintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	ārālmī ṉavari. kōḻikkāl tiyal, pāmpuk kaṟṟāḻi, vuvāl mīṉ.
cila peyarkaḷ uvami yaṭippaṭiyil amintu, teḷivupaṟṟi īṟu peṟṟum tirintum uḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	āṉikkompaṉ-oruvakic campānel.
	kāṭikkaṇṇi-kāṭikkaṇ pōṉṟa oruvakic ciṟu tavacam (tāṉiyam), ataṉ payir.
	kutirivāli-kutirivālpōṟ katirtaḷḷum oru vakic ciṟu tavacap payirum at tavacamum.
	nuṇā-nuṇal (maṇal tavaḷi) pōṉṟa kāy kāykkum maravaki.
(2) mūvakit takuti vaḻakku
iṭakkaraṭakkal (Euphemism), maṅkalam (Euphemism), kuḻūuk kuṟi (Conventional terms) eṉṉum mūvakit takuti vaḻakku muṟi yilum, cila coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	iṭakkaraṭakkal	: 	oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkuppōtal, pavvī.
	maṅkalam	: 	koṭittaṭṭutal = pāmpu kaṭittal.
			perumpiṟitu = cāvu.
	kuḻūukkuṟi	:	centali = arikkāl. 					karuntali=kāl. taṅkāṉ = ari. 				arumpu = arici.
(3) ōriṉappaṭuttalum (Generalisation) vēṟiṉappaṭuttalum (Discrimination).
ōriṉappaṭuttal
palvēṟuvakip poruḷkaḷi, oru potuttaṉmipaṟṟi oru collāṟ kuṟittal ōriṉappaṭuttalām.
e-ṭu: 	māṭu-peṟṟamum (kāḷiyum āvum) erumiyum.
	māṉ-uḻi, pulvāy, navvi, pukar (puḷḷimāṉ), mari, varuṭi, kaṭami, miḻā, kācaṟi, kavari, kutiri, mutaliyaṉa.
	tumpi-muṉtūmpu (proboscis) uṭiya yāṉiyum kuḷaviyum.
vilaṅku, paṟavi, maram, mīṉ eṉpaṉa mika virivāka ōriṉap paṭuttum coṟkaḷ.
	perunelli, arinelli, kīḻvāynelli eṉpavaṟṟi nelli yiṉappaṭuttiyatum ōriṉappaṭuttalē.
vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
orē iṉamāṉa allatu otta tōṟṟamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi, nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi vevvēṟu collāṟ kuṟittal vēṟiṉap paṭuttalām.
e-ṭu: 	ili-ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli.
	nilittiṇi-pūñcaṇam, pācam, pāci, kāḷāṉ, pul,  pūṇṭu, kōri, payir, taṭṭi, koṭi, tūṟu, ceṭi, maram. nuḷampu-nuḷḷāṉ, ulaṅku.
	camitta tavacavuṇavu-cōṟu, kañci, kūḻ, kaḷi.
iṉi, ciṟappuccoṟkaḷaṉṟip potuccoṟkaḷē vev vēṟaṭi peṟṟup poruḷkaḷi vēṟiṉappaṭuttalu muṇṭu.
e-ṭu:	ciṟṟeṟumpu, poṭiyeṟumpu, āyeṟumpu, cev veṟumpu, maḻi yeṟumpu,  maṇṭi yeṟumpu, piḷḷiyār eṟumpu, kaṭṭeṟumpu.
(4) aivakip poruḷtiripu

(5) potuppikki (Generalisation)
ciṟappup poruṭcollip potupporuḷil āḷvatu potuppikkiyām. itu ciṟappikkiyiṉ maṟutali.

	allatu toṭṭilil
	uṟaṅkutal
poruṭperukkamum poruḷiḻappum
oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttut tōṉṟuvatāl, potu vākac coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ iraṇṭum palavumākap perukuva tuṇṭu. ētēṉumoru ciṟappupporuḷ kuṟitta aṭipeṟṟa collip potupporuḷil vaḻaṅkiṉ, ac ciṟappup poruḷaṭi taṉ poruḷi iḻantuviṭum.
