(2) coṟṟoṭarākkam (tōrā. ki.mu. 25,000)
toṭariyam eṉṉum muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭar, ikkālattiṟ pōṉṟē mutaṟkālattilum (eḻuvāy payaṉiliyeṉṉum) īruṟuppu kaḷiyō, (avaṟṟoṭu ceyappaṭuporuḷum cērnta) mūvuṟuppu kaḷiyō koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, avi īṟum iṭi niliyumaṟṟa pakāccoṟkaḷākavē yiruntirukkum. aṟi eṉpatu aṟivu eṉṟum, keṭu eṉpatu kēṭu eṉṟum, cey eṉpatu ceyki yeṉṟum, iṉṟum poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
aṟikoṉ ṟaṟiyā ṉeṉiṉum 		(kuṟaḷ. 638)
keṭuviṉṟu maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar		(puṟam. 39:7)
kaḷiṟu kaḷampaṭutta peruñcey yāṭavar	(neṭu. 171)
aṭi, viḻi, taḷ, col mutaliya ettuṇiyō viṉi yaṭikaḷ, iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peyarākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil nal (nallu) eṉṉum paṇpaṭi naṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭukiṉṟatu. uṇṭu illi eṉpaṉavum, ivi pōṉṟa piṟavum, iṉṟum mukkālat tiṟkum potuvām. kuṟippu viṉikaḷ muṉpiṉ varum coṟṟuṇikoṇṭu ikkālattil oru kālatti uṇarttutal pōṉṟē, terinili viṉiyaṭikaḷum mutaṟkālattil uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivākavē yiruppiṉum, potumakkaḷ pēccu eṉṟum urinaṭiyē yeṉpati maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu.
ii ceyyuḷ naṭi (tōrā. ki.mu. 15,000)
tamiḻa māntar nīṇṭa kālamāka urinaṭiyilēyē pēcivanta piṉ, mati viḷakkattālum uṇarveḻucciyālum tūṇṭappaṭṭu teyvappaṟṟu, kātal, pōr, vāḻttu, kati, varalāṟu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷpaṟṟi, eḷiyanaṭiyil teṉpāṅkum cintum pōṉṟa icippāṭṭukaḷum, akavalum veṇkalippāvum irāmappayyaṉ ammāṉiyum pōṉṟa ceyyuḷkaḷum, iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. mōṉiyetuki yamippum coṟcurukkamum poruṭceṟivum avar uḷḷattik kavarntatiṉāl, palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya paḻamoḻikaḷum ovvoṉṟā yeḻuntaṉa.
(4) nālvaki eḻuttu (tōrā. ki.mu. 12,000)
aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkip pēccum paṇṭamāṟṟuriyāṭṭum kātalaṟivippum, vāymoḻiyāka nikaḻttuvatu nērilaṉṟit toliviṟ kūṭāmiyiṉ, nāḷaṭivil nālvakiyeḻuttu muṟikaḷ oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiṉa. avi,
(1) paṭaveḻuttu (Pictograph),
(2) karutteḻuttu (Ideograph),
(3) aciyeḻuttu (Syllabary),
(4) oliyeḻuttu (Phonetic Characters)
eṉpaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpataṉ mutaṟporuḷ paṭam allatu ōviyam eṉpatē. eḻututal-varital. iṉṟum paṭameḻututal eṉṉum vaḻakkiruttal kāṇka.
(5) eḻutappeṟṟa ilakkiyam (tōrā. ki.mu. 11,000)
oliyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyapiṉ kati, varalāṟu, kaṇakku, aṟam, araciyal, maṇaviṉpam, pōr, maruttuvam, kaṇiyam, ici,nāṭakam, camayam, pukaḻcci, vaḻuttu mutaliya palatuṟikaḷ paṟṟip paṉuvalkaḷum eḻuntaṉa.
ilakku eṉpatu kuṟi allatu kuṟikkōḷ. makkaḷ vāḻkki yiṉ ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷi eṭuttukkūṟi, avarit tiruttuvatum uyarttu vatumē paṉuvaliṉ allatu nūliṉ nōkkamātaliṉ, aviyiraṇṭum ilakkiyam eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ilakku+iyam = ilakkiyam.
paṇṭi viṟpayiṟciyāḷar, muṟiyē parupporuḷ, nuṇ poruḷ, cuḻalāp poruḷ, cuḻalporuḷ ākiya nālvaki ilakkiṉūṭu eytu payiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiyē,
peruvaṇmi ciṟunuṇmi calam niccalam	(vāraṇā. 56)
eṉṟu pāratam kūṟum. peruvaṇmi eṉpatu marattiṉ aṭi pōlvatu; ciṟu nuṇmi eṉpatu marattiṉ mēluḷḷa ciṟṟili pōlvatu; calam niccalam eṉpaṉa marat tucciyil amikkap peṟum tiripaṉṟiyum cuḻalāc cakkaramum pōlvaṉa.
iliyi ilakkeṉpatu iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. ciṟunuṇmikkup perumpālum iliyiyē kuṟiyākak koṇṭamiyāl, ilakku eṉṉum coṟkuk kuṟi eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
(6) ilakkaṇam (tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000)
ilakkaṇamum, moḻiyiṉ allatu ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta amippiyum naṭiyiyum eṭuttukkūṟuvatiyē kuṟikkō ḷākak koṇṭamiyiṉ, ap peyar peṟṟatu. ilakku+aṇam = ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻintati nilaṉeṉak
 koḷḷu meṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē	(tol. 993)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil, kuṟi eṉṉuñ col ilakkaṇattik kuṟittal kāṇka. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucollum orē mutaṉiliyē. poruḷ vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi īṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. iviyiraṇṭum mutaṉili yiṉālum īṟṟiṉālum tūya teṉcollē yeṉṟu teḷika. ivi vaṭamoḻiyil muṟiyē laṣya lakṣṇa eṉṟu tiriyum. āyiṉ, aṅku  ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷik kuṟippavi vyākaraṇa, sāhitya eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē. lakṣya lakṣaṇa eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāka vaṭa moḻiyil kāṭṭap peṟum lakṣ eṉṉum coṟku, ilakku(kuṟi) eṉpatē poruḷ. ākavē, ivviru vaṭacollum teṉcol liṉ tiripeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. mēlum, tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl āriya varukikku 8000 āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉum uṇmiyum, iti valiyuṟuttuvatu kāṇka..
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟoru peyar kuṟik kiṉṟatu cintāmaṇi nikaṇṭu(103). katirivēṟpiḷḷi avarkaḷ tokutta tamiḻmoḻi yakarātiyil, añcaṇaṅkam-pañca vilakkaṇam. añcaṇaṅkiyam-pañcavilakkiyam eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum irucoṟkum, aṇaṅku eṉpatu mutaṉiliyā yiruttal vēṇṭum. aṇaṅkutal-olittal.
mikiyaṇaṅku meynniṟīi mīḷi maṟavar
 pukiyaṇaṅkap pūmāri cinti	(potuviyaṟpaṭalam, 8)
eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli . aṇaṅku eṉpatu eḻuttiyuṅ kuṟikkumāyiṉ, aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, ilakkaṇattiyum ilakkiyattiyuṅ kuṟippatu poruttamē. āyiṉ, ic coṟkaḷ tamiḻakattil eṉṟum vaḻakkiliruntatākat teriyavilli. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉavē toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉa.
eḻuttu-eḻuttoliyam (Phonemics)
tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ, (1) mutal, (2) cārpu eṉa iruvakippaṭum. mutalāvatu iyalpākat tōṉṟiya eḻuttukaḷ mutaleḻuttukaḷ; piṉpu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpākat tirinta eḻuttukaḷ cārpeḻuttukaḷ. uyire ḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum meyyeḻuttup patiṉeṭṭumāka, mutaleḻuttukaḷ muppatām. cārpeḻuttukaḷ mūṉṟām. ākavē, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ allatu olikaḷ mottam muppattu mūṉṟām.
uyireḻuttukaḷ, kuṟilintum neṭilēḻumāka iruvakip paṭum. meyyeḻuttukaḷum, valliṉam āṟum melliṉam āṟum iṭiyiṉam āṟumāka mūvakippaṭum.
(1) mutaleḻuttukaḷ (Phonemes)
māntaṉ taṉ vāyit tiṟantavuṭaṉ iyalpāka olikkum oli akaram allatu ākāram. kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ aḻum oliyum muḻittal muṟippaṭṭa akara ākāramē. ākavē, mutalāvatu māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya uyiroliyē; atu akaramē.
vāy tiṟavāta niliyil, mūkkuvaḷiyāl olikkum oli makaram. ākavē, meyyolikaḷuḷ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyatu, itaḻ maṭṭum porutti eḷitāka volikkum makaramākum.
iṅṅaṉam mutaṉmutalākat tōṉṟiya akaramum makaramuñ cēriṉ, am eṉṉum aci piṟakkum. orukāl itiṉiṉṟu, amma eṉṉum muṟippeyar kuḻavivaḷarp poliyāyt tōṉṟi yirukkalām. pāluṟuppik kuṟikkum ammam eṉṉum col marumam eṉpataṉ tiripākum. marumam-mammam-ammam.
māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya moḻiyolikaḷuḷ, uyirel lām muṉpum meyyellām piṉpumākat tōṉṟavilli, uyirum meyyum oḻuṅkiṉṟi māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. am muṟiyi iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. āyiṉum, uyiriṉattiyum meyyiṉatti yum vēṟu pirittu ovvōri ṉattiyuñ cērnta olikaḷ tōṉṟiya muṟiyi oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
uyir tōṉṟiya muṟi
mutal tōṉṟiyavi	:	neṭil-ā, ī, ū
iṭittoṉṟiyavi	:	neṭil-ē, ō
		kuṟil-a, i, u
kaṭittōṉṟiyavi	:	neṭil-ai, au
		kuṟil-e, o
uyirkaḷuḷ, neṭil muṉpum kuṟil piṉpum tōṉṟiṉa. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷvataṟku ētukkaḷ muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. mutalil tōṉṟiya mūvuyirum, muṟiyē, cēymi yaṇmi muṉmic cuṭṭukaḷākavē tōṉṟiṉa.
aṇmic cuṭṭiṉum muṉmic cuṭṭu pintiyatātalālum, caṟṟu mikunta muyaṟci vēṇṭutalāṉum, īkāram ūkārattiṟku muntiyatātal vēṇṭum.
ikara īkārattiṉ mōṉiyāka ekara ēkāramum, ukara ūkārattiṉ mōṉiyāka okara ōkāramum tōṉṟutalāṉum; kocci vaḻakkil iṭam eṉpatu eṭam eṉṟum, uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum, ikaram ekaramākavum ukaram okaramākavum olippatālum, aṇmic cuṭṭi liruntē ekara ēkāramum, muṉmic cuṭṭiliruntē okara ōkāramum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
aḷapu vakiyil, 
āī  ūē  aiō  aue(ṉ)ṉum
 appāl ēḻum
 īraḷa picikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉpa
eṉṟum, amippu vakiyil,
akara ikaram aikāra mākum. 	(54)
akara ukaram aukāra mākum  		(55)
eṉṟum, tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, akara ikaram cērntu aikāramum akaravukaram cērntu aukāramum puṇarolikaḷāyt (diphthongs) tōṉṟiṉaveṉa aṟiyalām. ikaram ukarattiṉum muntiya tātaliṉ, ikarak kūṟuḷḷa aikāram ukarak kūṟuḷḷa aukārattiṉum muntit tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum,
vaṭanūlār puṇarccit tiripip paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiyum puṇarolikaḷākak koḷvar. ak koḷki tamiḻukku ēṟkātu. muṟkūṟiyavāṟu, avi mōṉit tiripāy eḻuntaviyē.
ōriru coṉmutalil varum akara ikaram aikāramākavum eḻutap peṟum.
e-ṭu: 	vaḷ-vay-vayir-vayiram-viram.
ōriru coṉmutalil varum akara ukaram aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e-ṭu: 	katuvāli (= kuṟukiya vāliyuṭiya paṟaviyiṉam)-kavutāri (ilakkaṇappōli)-kutāri.
iṉi, akarattiyaṭutta yakarameyyum ellāviṭattum aikāra mākavē eḻutappeṟum.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
 aieṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.  	(tol. 56)
e-ṭu: 	vaḷ (=kūrmi)-(vay)-vi = kūrmi.
	ḷakara mey yakara meyyākat tirivatu peruvaḻakku.
e-ṭu:	koḷ-kōḷ-kōy, toḷ-toy, noḷ-noy, piḷ-piy, māḷ-māy, veḷ-vey- veyyōṉ = virumpukiṉṟōṉ. vey-	veymmi-vemmi=viruppam.
vemmi vēṇṭal.		(tol. uri. 36)
yakara meyyīṟṟu okara mutaṟcol īreḻuttuc collā yiruntu akara mutalāy māṟiṉ, aikāra vaṭivu peṟum.
e-ṭu:	poḷ-poy (uṭṭuḷi)-(pay)-pi,
akaratti yaṭutta vakaramey, oru cila coṟkaḷil aukāra mākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e-ṭu: 	avviyam-auviyam, kavvu-kuvu, vavvāl - vuvāl
auvi eṉṉum col avvi eṉpataṉ tiripēyā yiṉum, oru pulattiyāriṉ iyaṟpeyarātaliṉ, aukāra mutalatākavē eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ammi-avvi-auvi.
av, avvāṟu, cavvu, tavvu mutaliya cila coṟkaḷil, akara maṭutta vakaramey aukāravīṟāka eḻutappaṭuvatē yilli. āyiṉ, ay eṉṉum vaṭivam ay eṉṉum aciyik kuṟittālaṉṟi, eḻuttuttamiḻil nikaḻtaṟku iṭamēyilli. iti nōkkip pōlum, 
`akarat timpar vakarap puḷḷiyum
 aueṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum'
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟātuviṭṭatu!
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ ai, au iraṇṭum puṇaroliyaṉkaḷ (diphthongs or compound phonemes); ēṉiya taṉiyoliyaṉkaḷ (monoph - thongs or simple phonemes).
uyir tōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
(1) aṅkāttal (vāytiṟattal) - a, ā
(2) cuṭṭal - ā, ī, ū
(3) mōṉittiripu - i-e, u-o
(4) uṇarcciyoli - ai!
(5) vāycceyki - av-au
(6) kuṟukkam - ā-a, ē-e
(7) oppoli - ū (kū)
aṅkāttal eṉpatu maṟṟellā vakikaṭkum aṭip paṭit tuṇiyēyaṉṟi, taṉivakiyaṉṟu.
mey tōṉṟiya muṟi
ma
pa, ka, ṅa
la, ra, ta, na
ca, ña, ya, va
ṭa, ṇa, ḷa, ḻa
ṟa, ṉa
kuṟippu: 
(1)	uyirum meyyum tōṉṟiya muṟiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa īroḻuṅkum, tōrāya (uttēca) muṟiyē yaṉṟi, immiyum vaḻuvāta aṟiviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tulliya muṟiyalla.
(2)	uyirum meyyum vevvēṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭirup piṉum, uyirellān  tōṉṟiyapiṉ mey tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟākātu. iruvakiyum ovvoṉṟāy allatu oṉṟum palavumāy māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa.
	mutalil ākāravuyirum atiyaṭuttu makarameyyum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
yakaram tōṉṟumuṉ īkāra ikaramum, vakaram tōṉṟu muṉ ūkāra ukaramum, tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa tōṉṟumuṉ ellā vuyirkaḷum tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
	(3) melliṉa meykaḷuḷ, makaram oṉṟē taṉiyē  tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyavellām ṅka, ñca, ṇṭa, nta, ṉṟa eṉat taṉtaṉ valliṉameyyuṭaṉ iṇintē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
kuḻavikaḷiṉ toṇṭiyil ṅka eṉṉum oli piṟattal kāṇka.
valliṉameykaḷ taṉittaṉi tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
(4) 	māntaṉ allatu muntiyal tamiḻaṉ pēciya iyaṟki moḻi, uyir, mey eṉṉum pakuppuṇarcciyiṉṟi, ōraciyum palavaci yumākiya olikaḷiyum ōcikaḷi yumē koṇṭiruntatu.
meytōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
(1) oppoli	-	k (kākkā), ṇ(kiṇ).
(2) vāycceyki	-	v (av), m (am).
(3) iṉattōṉṟal	-	ṅ (ṅka), ṉ(ṉṟa).
(4) puṇarcci	-	ñ (pū+cōli=pūñcōli)
(5) tiripu	-	n (porum-porun), r(l-r)
(6) mutircci	-	ḻ (ḷa-ḻa), ṟ (ra-ṟa).
(7) iyaṟki	-	t, p.
aḷavu (māttiri)
eḻuttukaḷiṉ oliyaḷavu aḷapu allatu māttiri yeṉappaṭum. māttal - aḷattal. itu oru vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉi. māttiram eṉṉum collum itaṉaṭip piṟantatē. mā eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarām.
ōr imi allatu noṭiyaḷavu oru māttiri. uyirāyiṉum uyirmeyyāyiṉum, kuṟilukku oṉṟum neṭilukku iraṇṭum māttiriyām. uyirmeyyil uyirum meyyum ākiya īreḻut tiruppiṉum, am meykku māttiriyilli. taṉimeykku māttiri ari.
ai, au eṉṉum iruneṭilum taṉittu nillātum aḷapeṭukkātum colluṟuppāy variṉ, oṉṟari māttiri yāyk kuṟuki yolikkum. aikāram colliṭiyil oru māttiri yākavuṅ kuṟukum.
(2) cārpeḻuttukaḷ
uyirum meyyum ākiya mutaleḻuttukaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpiṉāl tōṉṟuvaṉa cārpeḻuttukaḷ. cārtal - oṉṟi yoṉṟu aṭuttal. uyiriṉattic cērntavi iraṇṭum mey yiṉattic cērntatu oṉṟum āka, cārpeḻuttukaḷ mottam mūṉṟām. avi kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉpaṉa vākum.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiyavāṟu im mūṉṟē cārpeḻuttukaḷ. naṉṉūlār uyirmeyyiyum vēṟu cila eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅ kaḷiyum aḷapeṭiyiyum cērttut tavaṟākap patteṉa virittuviṭṭār. uyirmey uyirum meyyum cērnta kūṭṭeḻut tēyaṉṟi vēṟeḻut tākātu. eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅkaḷi yellām cārpeḻutteṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, āytakkuṟukkattic cārpiṟ cārpeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭi eṉpatu eḻut toli nīṭṭamēyaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttākātu. ivvuṇmi kaḷi yellām nōkkātu, cār peḻuttut tokiyip perukki yataṟku, māṇavari mayakkutalaṉṟi vēṟoru payaṉumiṉṟām.
kuṟṟiyalikaram
ariyaḷapāyk kuṟukiyolikkum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram. atu mūvakiyil tōṉṟum.
(i)	yakarattōṭu puṇarnta kuṟṟiyalukarat tiripu.
	e-ṭu:    varaku + yātu = varakiyātu.
(ii)	puṇarcciyāl aminta miyā eṉṉum muṉṉiliyacic col. 
	kēḷum + aiyā = kēḷumiyā-kēḷumiyā- kēṇmiyā. o.nō: maruḷum-maruṇm.
(iii)	melliṉa iṭiyiṉa meyyīṟu yakarattōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcci viḷivu.
	maṇ+yāṉi = maṇṇiyāṉi
	vēḷ+yāvaṉ = vēḷiyāvaṉ
	ivvakip puṇarcci ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram
taṉineṭiṟkum iru allatu palaveḻuttiṟkum piṉ, valliṉa meyyōṭu kūṭic collīṟāy varum ukaram, itaḻ kuviyātu, ikarattiṟkum ukarattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭu, ari māttiriyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum atu kuṟṟiyalukaram.
e-ṭu:	māṭu, cekku, paruppu, piṇṇākku, vantataṟku.
āytam
taṉiccollilum puṇarccollilum, kuṟilukkum valliṉa uyirmeykkum iṭiyil, nuṇṇiya kakaramāka olittu niṟkum eḻuttu āytamām. āytal-nuṇukutal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāaytal
 āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam   	(tol. 813)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka.
āytam piṉvarumāṟu nālvakiyil tōṉṟum.
(i)	puṇarccit tiripu
  	colluṟuppup puṇarcci:
	e-ṭu	:	al+ku = alku-aḵku
			veḷ+ku = veḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal. veḷkutal = virumputal. veḷ-veṇṭu(ācippaṭu). veḷ-vey-veyyōṉ = virumpi yōṉ.
coṟpuṇarcci:
	e-ṭu	: 	pal+tuḷi = paḵṟuḷi
			kal+tītu = kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu
			muḷ+tītu = muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu
			av+kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya
(ii)	taṉiccol tirippu
	e-ṭu	: 	attu-aḵtu, pattu-paḵtu
			pakuti-paḵti
(iii)	cāriyip puṇarppu
	e-ṭu	: 	aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ
(iv)	iṭiccerukal (oṟṟilvaḻi yoṟṟu)
	e-ṭu	:	ilaku-ilaḵku, vilaku-vilaḵku
ivvaki ceyyuṭkē yuriyatām.
āytam ōruyirōṭuṅ kūṭātu taṉittu niṟpatāl taṉinili eṉṟum, puḷḷivaṭivāyiruppatāl puḷḷi eṉṟum, muppuḷḷi eṉṟum, taṉakkuppiṉ varum valliṉa meyyi melivippatāl nalipu eṉṟum  peyarpeṟum.
pattu, paḵtu; kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu eṉṉum colliṇikaḷi olittuk kāṇka.
iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻukkuriya āytam, tirimoḻikaṭ kuriya polivolikaḷip (Voiced Sounds) piṟappikkum eṉṉum kūṟṟu, tiripuṇarcciyiṉ viḷiveṉa aṟika. āytavoliyiṉ kakaraviṉat taṉmiyi aḵkēṉam, maḵkāṉ eṉṉum cāriyip puṇarppālum kaṇṭukoḷka.
cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalu karamum tām niṟkumiṭattu vēṟoṉṟaṟkum iṭantarāmiyāl, av viraṇṭiyum maṟuvoliyaṉkaḷ (Allophones) eṉṉalām. āytam attuṇi oḻuṅkum yāppuṟavum peṟāmiyiṉ maṟuvoliya ṉākātu.
eṇ
uṇmiyil, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mutaleṉappaṭum muppatē. ataṉālēyē,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
 akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
 muppaḵ teṉpa
 cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē		(tol. 1)
eṉat tolkāppiyar, nūṉmarapu eṉṉum mutal iyalil, muntu nūlār moḻintavāṟu mutaleḻuttiṟkē ciṟappuk koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.
mūṉṟuṟaḻnta patiṟṟeḻuttāṉ muḻuvatumāy uṉakkiṉitāyt
 tōṉṟiṭumat tamiḻ 			(kāñci. taḻuva. 244)
eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. 
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppateḻuttu kaḷi maṭṭum koṇṭa tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, ataṉoṭu 216 uyirmeyyeḻuttuṅkoṇṭa tokutikku neṭuṅ kaṇakku eṉṟum peyar. paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṇ meyyoṭuṅ kūṭa, uyirmey mottam irunūṟṟup patiṉāṟām.
ellā moḻikaḷilum uyirmeykaḷ kalantutāṉ olik kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭṭu vaṭivam mutaṉmutal amit tavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyē vaṭamoḻi yilum piṟa intiya moḻikaḷilum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ amintaṉa. iṉṉum mēli āriyattilum cēmiyattilum uyirum meyyum taṉittaṉi niṟuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. amerikku, cappāṉ mutaliya orucila moḻikaḷil maṭṭum, taṉi varikkum kūṭṭu varikkum iṭippaṭṭa acivarikaḷ (Syllabaries) ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aviyum kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭaviyē.
āytam uyirēṟappeṟāta meyyāyiṉum, ataṟkut taṉi vaṭiva muṇmipaṟṟi atiyum ōr eḻuttākak koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mottam irunūṟṟu nāṟpattēḻām.
peyar
cevippulaṉāṉa moḻiyolikaḷ kaṭpulaṉāṉa vari vaṭivil eḻutap peṟṟataṉāl, eḻutteṉap peyarpeṟṟaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivik kuṟippiṉ toḻilākupeyar; olivaṭivik kuṟippiṉ kuṟiyākupeyar.
viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
 muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum		(tol. 1594)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷapaṭi, tamiḻil mutalilakkaṇa nūliyaṟṟiyavaṉ, muṟṟat tuṟantavaṉum meypporuḷaṟivu nirampiya vaṉumāṉa muṉivaṉāvāṉ.
muṉital = (ulaka vāḻvi) veṟuttal, veṟuttut tuṟattal. muṉi, muṉi eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē colliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamām. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ; muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ.
muṉivumuṉi vākum. 	(tol. col. 386)
iṉṟīm pāli muṉiyiṉ	(perumpāṇ. 180)
ulakiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ ellām, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉa mūvaki. uyir tāṉē iyaṅkum kāṟṟu vaṭiviṉatu; mey uyiriṉ utaviyiṉṟi iyaṅkāta uṭampu allatu kaṉapporuḷ; uyirmey uyiroṭu kūṭiya uṭampu. pirāṇi (pirāṇaṉi yuṭiyatu)  eṉṟa vaṭacol tamiḻil vaḻakkūṉṟiyapiṉ, ataṟku nērāṉa uyirmey eṉṉum tamiḻccol vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉatu ṭaṉ, taṉ poruḷiyum iḻantatu. tamiḻil mutaṉūl kaṇṭa muṉivaṉ, mūvaki yeḻuttukaḷum oliyiyakkattil mūvakip poruḷkaḷi ottiruppatu kaṇṭu, ap poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷiyē av veḻuttukaṭkum uvamiyāku peyarāka iṭṭāṉ. tāṉē olikkum eḻuttu uyireḻuttu; uyiroṭu kūṭiyallatu olikkāta eḻuttu meyyeḻuttu; uyirmeyyip pōṉṟa eḻuttu uyirmeyyeḻuttu. ittakiya amippu vēṟem moḻiyilum kāṇpataṟkaritām.
kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉpaṉa veḷippaṭi.
uyir, āvi eṉpaṉavum; mey, uṭal, uṭampu, uṟuppu, oṟṟu, puḷḷi eṉpaṉavum oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
muṟiyum piṟappum
tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭuḷ, muṟiyum oṉṟām. itu mēli moḻikaṭkilli. vaṭamoḻiyuṭpaṭa ēṉi yintiya moḻikaḷellām neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyil tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟi yaṉavē.
uyir, muntit tōṉṟiyatupaṟṟiyum, tāṉāy olippatu paṟṟiyum, neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṉ vikkappeṟṟatu. kuṟumi paṟṟik kuṟil muṉṉum, neṭumipaṟṟi neṭil piṉṉum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ā, ī, ū mūṉṟum, muṟiyē cēymic cuṭṭākavum aṇmic cuṭṭākavum muṉmic cuṭṭākavum iyalpākat tōṉṟiṉamiyāl, piṟa vuyirkaḷukku muṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ākāram vāy tiṟanta maṭṭil olippatāl mutalilum, īkāram vāyiṉ piṉpakkattil aṭinā viḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip poruntiyolippatāl iṭiyilum, ūkāram vāyiṉ muṉ pakkattil itaḻ(utaṭu) kuvintolippatāl kaṭiyilum vikkappaṭṭaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu, vāyiṉ piṉpuṟattiliruntu muṉpuṟam nōkkiya variciyil tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ amikkappaṭṭati aṟiyalām.
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ muṟiyē īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷiṉ mōṉit tiripātalāl, iyalpākat tōṉṟi mutal mūṉṟuyirkaṭkum piṉ    vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ēkāram aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip poruntiyolippatāl muṉṉum, ōkāram itaḻ kuvintolippatāl piṉṉum, vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ai, au iraṇṭum muṟiyē ai, au eṉṉum puṇaroli kaḷātalāl, aṭinā viḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip porunti yolikkum aikāram ēkārattiṉ piṉṉum, itaḻ kuvintolikkum aukāram ōkārattiṉ piṉṉum vikkap paṭṭaṉa.
āytam nuṇṇiya kakaramātalāl, kakarattiṉ muṉ vikkap paṭṭatu. 
meykaḷuḷ valliṉamum melliṉamum etiriṉaṅkaḷātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiyaviyellām valiyum meliyumāka iv viraṇṭāy muṉ amikkappeṟṟaṉa. iṭiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum piṟa meykaḷ oḻuṅku paṭuttap paṭṭataṟkup pintit tōṉṟiyamiyāl, iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
valliṉamelliṉa meykaḷuḷ, ka ṅa aṭiyaṇṇap piṟap piṉavum, ca ña iṭiyaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ṭa ṇa muṉṉaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum ta na pallaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, pa ma itaḻakap piṟappiṉavum ātalāl, am muṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
makaramoḻinta melliṉa meykaḷellām taṉtaṉ valliṉa meykkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyamiyālum, valiyolitta piṉ meli yolippatē muyaṟcik keḷimiyātalālum, valiyum meliyum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟpatu olivēṟṟumiyit teḷivāyk kāṭṭu vatālum, valimuṉṉum meli piṉṉumāka iṇiyiṇiyāy niṟuttappeṟṟaṉa veṉa aṟika.
iṭiyiṉa voliyiṉ iṭittaṉmi valloliyum melloliyum aṟinta piṉṉarē aṟiyak kiṭattaliṉ, valimeli kaḷiṉ piṉ iṭiyiṉam vikkappaṭṭatu. iṭiyiṉa meykaḷuḷ, yakaram iṭiyaṇṇattilum, rakaram muṉṉaṇṇattilum, lakaram pallaṇṇattilum, vakaram pallitaḻilum piṟappatāl, avi am muṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭattil neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭaṅkiyapōtu, atil amintirunta eḻuttukaḷ ittaṉiyē. piṉṉar, nāḷaṭi vil ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉālēyē avi iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
an nāṉkaṉuḷ, ḷa ḻa muṉtōṉṟiyamiyāl muṉṉum;         ṟa ṉa piṉtōṉṟiyamiyāṟ piṉṉum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum muṉṉaṇṇa varuṭolikaḷāyiṉum (Linguals or Cerebrals), ḻakaram ḷakarattiṉum caṟṟup piṉṉiruntu varuṭap peṟuvatāl, muṉvikkappeṟṟatu.
iṟutimey nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiṉa veṉpataṟku ētukkaḷāvaṉa:
(1)	ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum iṭiyiṉattuḷḷum lakarattiṟku muṉṉum vikkap peṟāmi.
(2)	ṟa, ṉa iraṇṭum valimeli yiṇikaḷuḷ ta na iṇikku muṉ vikkap peṟāmi.
(3)	lakaram tiraṇṭu ḷakaramum ḷakaram tiraṇṭu ḻakaramum ātal.
e-ṭu: kal-karumi. kal+tu = kaḵṟu. kaḵṟeṉal= karumik kuṟippu. kal-kāl=karumi. kāl kāḷ=karumi. kāḷ- kāḻ = karumi.
(4)	rakaram tiraṇṭu ṟakaram ātal.
	e-ṭu: ar-aṟu, oḷir-oḷiṟu, muri-muṟi.
(5)	ṉakaran tōṉṟumuṉ nakaramē colliṟutiyilum    vaḻaṅkiyami.
	e-ṭu: verin, paḻunu
(6) tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum ēṉiyolikaḷiṉ meṉmiyum.
ṟakaram tōṉṟiyapōtu ataṟkiṉamākavē ṉakaramum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. tannakaram ṟakarattiṟku iṉamākāmi kāṇka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi, teṉmi, muṉmi, meṉmi mutaliya taṉmikaḷi aṟiyāmiyāl, kālṭuvel uḷḷiṭṭa mēli yārāycciyāḷar āriya ēmāṟṟi nampit tamiḻic camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭiyilāyntu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku camaṟkirutattip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟu muṭivu koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl cila koṇṭāṉ mārum tamiḻp paṟṟaṟṟa tiraviṭat tamiḻp pulavarum ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum vaṭamoḻiyi lillāmiyāl neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu, pitaṟṟalāyiṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliṉ miyē iṟutivippiṟkuk karaṇiyamāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻi yilillāta ekara okaramum iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉa miṉmiyiṉ, atu pōli yuriyeṉa maṟukka.
uruvam
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyarē, tamiḻukku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟu muṉṉarē eḻuttiruntamiyi uṇarttum. eḻutuvatu eḻuttu. eḻututal-varital.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl  	 (kuṟaḷ. 1285)
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttunili maṇṭapam		(paripā. 1953)
eḻuvutal eṉpatu icikkaruviyiṉiṉṟu oliyeḻup putalik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi, eḻututalik kuṟikkātu.
paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttukaḷellām pirāmiyiṉ tiripāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, kiranta veḻuttut teṉṉāṭṭil piṟ kālattil vaṭamoḻikkeṉ ṟēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉṟum, potuvākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, eḻuttuvaṭivampaṟṟiya cila tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiyum naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷiyum, paṇṭi yēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiyum nōkkumiṭattu, ik karuttup poruttamuṭiyatāyt tōṉṟavilli. iṟṟi vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttumuṟiyē paṇṭivaḻakkilum iruntateṉṟum, paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭṭeḻuttu veṭ ṭeḻuttu eṉap peyarpeṟṟirukkalā meṉṟum, kirantaveḻuttu paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttiṉ tiripē yeṉṟum, atu ēṭṭiṟkuriya āṇi yeḻutteṉṟum, koḷvatē poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum ētukkaḷumāvaṉa:
(1) mikap paḻimiyāṉa tamiḻēṭṭilum iṟṟi yeḻuttu vaṭivēyiruttal.
(2) ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iṟṟiyeḻuttu vaṭivit toṉṟu toṭṭateṉak kūṟal.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ(ṭu)
 eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi		(naṉ. 98)

(3)	paṭṭayaveḻuttu vaṭṭamāyirāmi. vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉpatu vaṭivupaṟṟiyatāyiṉ, atu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟkē (kiranta vaṭiveḻuttiṟkē) poruntuvatākum.

intiya nākarikam āriyarateṉṟum, tamiḻ ellā vakiyilum vaṭamoḻiyip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟum, mēli yaṟiñar kuruṭṭut taṉamākak koṇṭirunta aṭippaṭit tavaṟukaḷē, tamiḻeḻuttup pirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu koḷḷaṟ kiṭantantaṉa.

ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ neṭilkaṭkup pintit tōṉṟi yamiyāl, avaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttikkāṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷip pēcuvōriṉ muṉṉōr, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ tamiḻil tōṉṟumuṉ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tiṉālēyē, am moḻikaḷil iṉṟum ak kuṟilkaḷ illi. am moḻikaḷiṉ mūlamākiya pirākirutaṅkaḷi vēta āriyar pēciṉatiṉālēyē, avar vētattilum avi illi.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ(ṭu)
 eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi	    	(naṉ. 98)
eṉṟu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattiṉatāṉa naṉṉūl  kūṟuvatāl, tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuva teṉat tuṇiyalām. `āṇṭu' eṉpatu akkālattu (muṟkālattil) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatākum. ekara okara uyirum uyirmeyyum puḷḷipeṟṟa oṉṟē, paṇṭiyeḻuttiṉ vēṟṟumi yeṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār pavaṇanti muṉivar. avar kālattilum avi puḷḷipeṟṟaṉaveṉiṉ, ati ēṉ vitantu kūṟal vēṇṭum? tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum eṉpatilēyē atu aṭaṅkiviṭumē! ātalāl, atu uriyaṉṟeṉka.
`eytum' eṉpatu vaḻakkam paṟṟiya kālavaḻuvamiti.
piṟkālattil vēṟṟaracu vantuviṭṭataṉālum, tamiḻik kākkum poṟuppuc centamiḻppulavar kiyiliṉmiyālum, ilakkaṇamaṟiyā māntar ēṭṭilum kallilum tappuntavaṟu māy eḻuti vantatiyē, vīramā muṉivar muṉṉi muṟippaṭi tirut tiṉār eṉa aṟika.
māttiri
eḻuttukaṭku māttiri iṉṉiṉṉa aḷaveṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭiruppiṉum, kūppīṭu (viḷi), oppāri (pulampal), icippāṭṭu, teruviṟpaṉi (paṇṭamāṟṟu) mutaliya ulakiyaṟ ceytikaḷil, avi cevippāṭu, tuyaram, iṉimi mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, vēṇṭumaḷavu allatu iyaṉṟa aḷavu nīṇṭolikkum. atu aḷapeṭi eṉappaṭum. aḷapeṭuttal - māttiri kaṭantolittal. uyiraḷa peṭi, oṟṟaḷa peṭi eṉa aḷapeṭi iruvaki. oṟṟu - mey.
uyiraḷapeṭiyi varivaṭiviṟ kuṟikkumpōtu neṭiliṉ piṉṉum kuṟiliṉ piṉṉum, iyalpāka vuriya aḷaviṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ovvoru māttirikkum ovvoru muṟiyāka, avvak kuṟil allatu iṉakkuṟilkaḷ vaṭivu cērtteḻutappaṭum. iciyil aḷapeṭik kuṟilkaḷ toṭarntum viṭṭum olikkum; piṟa vaṟṟil toṭarntē olikkum.
oṟṟaḷapeṭiyāyiṉ, ellāviṭattum, kūṭiya ovvōr arimāttirikkum oru muṟiyāka avvam meyvaṭivē cērt teḻutappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttukaḷuḷ valliṉam aḷapeṭukkā.
ici kuṟintaviṭattu ati niṟikkac ceyyuḷilum aḷapeṭi nikaḻum.
ulakavaḻak kaḷapeṭiyum ceyyuḷvaḻak kaḷapeṭiyum piṉvarumāṟu peyar peṟum:
ulaka vaḻakku	ceyyuḷ vaḻakku
iyaṟki yaḷapeṭi                                                                	         ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭi
ulakiya laḷapeṭi                 		ceyyuḷiya laḷapeṭi
ceyyaḷiṟ kuṟil aḷapeṭuppiṉum, neṭilvaṭiviṉ piṉṉarē kuṟil vaṭivu kuṟikkappaṭum. valliṉattuṭaṉ ḷakara ḻakaramum ceyyuḷil aḷapeṭukkā.

palukkoliyam (Phonetics)
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ, mūkkoliyuyir allatu melliṉa vuyir (Nasal vowel) eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻil illavē yilli. avaṉ, vantēṉ, maram, pōṉōm mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa melliṉa meykaḷē, cōmpalāṟ cevviyāyp palukkap peṟāmal mūkkoliyaḷavāy olikkiṉṟaṉa. iv voliyi īṟṟayaluyir mēlēṟṟi, ataṉi mūkkoliyuyir eṉpatu tiripuṇarcciyē. avar, tāy, makaḷ, vantāl mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa iṭiyiṉa mey, ōriṭattum mūkkoliyāy oliyāmiyi olittuk kāṇka.
moḻimutal ukaram itaḻkuvittē olikkappeṟuvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram coṉmutaṟkaṇ varavē varātu. tiraviṭappulavar cilar karutukiṟapaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟumaṉṟu. olikkuṟukkatti allatu tiripik kāṭṭutaṟkē, ataṉmītu puḷḷi yiṭappeṟṟatu.

āytam kakaravoliyic cārnta teṉpati, atu toṉṟu toṭṭu olikkappaṭṭuvarum muṟimiyiṉālum, ēṉaccāriyi ēṟkumpōtu aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattip paṟṟukkōṭākak koḷvatālum, kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum neṭuṅkaṇakkilum kakara meykku muṉ vikkappaṭṭiruppatālum, paṇṭi neṭuṅ kaṇak kēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil ka-ḵ eṉa variyappeṟṟamiyālum aṟiyalām.
āytam varum coṟkaḷiyellām āyntu pārppiṉ, atu perumpālum lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟatu. iti nōkkumiṭattu, kumarikkaṇṭattil ōriṭattu olittiri pākavō puṇarccit tiripākavō āytam tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu  terikiṉṟatu.

muntunili yeḻuttukaḷ
tamiḻ ulakap perumoḻikaṭkuḷ mika muntit tōṉṟiya iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl, atiṟ piṟa moḻikaḷiṟpōl ellā eḻut tukaḷum coṉ mutaliṭikaṭiyākiya mūviṭattum varuvatilli.
mutaṉili yeḻuttukaḷ
coṉmutalil uyirum uyirmeyyuntāṉ varum; āytamum meyyum varavē varā.
ṅa, ṭa, ṇa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum oṉpatumeyyum uyirōṭukūṭi uyirmeyyākavum coṉmutal varā. uyirōṭu kūṭic coṉmutal varum meykaḷuḷḷum, kacatanapama eṉṉum āṟē paṉṉīruyiroṭuṅ kūṭivarum. ēṉiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyi roṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum kūṭivarum. aviyāvaṉa:
ña, ñā, ñe, ño; ya (yavaṉar), yā; va, vā, vi, vī, ve, vē, vi, vu.
iṭinili yeḻuttukaḷ
colliṭiyil āytamum meyyum uyirmeyyumē varum; taṉiyuyir varātu. uyiruḷ, kuṟilmaṭṭum aḷapeṭik kuṟiyāka varum.
k, c, t, p ākiya nāl valliṉa meykaṭkuppiṉ, taṉṉuyir meyyēyaṉṟi vēṟṟuyirmey varātu. itu uṭaṉili meym mayakkam eṉappaṭum. piṟa meykaṭkuppiṉ piṟa vuyirmeykaḷ cilavum palavum varum. itu vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam eṉap paṭum. mayakkam-kūṭṭam.
r, ḻ ākiya irumeyyum iraṭṭā; ēṉiya iraṭṭum.
y, r, ḻ ākiya mummeykkuppiṉ taṉimey varalām; piṟavaṟṟiṉ piṉ varā.
e-ṭu: vāyppu, tērcci, cīḻkki.
mra, mla; vra, vla eṉṉum kūṭṭukaḷ tamiḻil evvakiyilum nikaḻā.

viḻuttam (Accent)
oru colliṉ ōr aciyil viḻum oliyuṟippu viḻuttam eṉappaṭum. tamiḻil viḻuttam (1) iyalpu viḻuttam, (2) coṟ piriviḻuttam, (3) colvaki viḻuttam eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
(1) iyalpu viḻuttam
iyalpu viḻuttam iyalpāka nikaḻvatu. atu eṉṟum colliṉ mutalil viḻum.
e-ṭu: koṟṟaṉ, vantāṉ.
oliyuṟippu varavarak kuṟiyumātalāl, coṉmutalil eṭuttalum (Acute), iṭiyil nalitalum (Circumflex) iṟutiyiṟ paṭuttalum (Grave),   nikaḻum.
īruyirc collāyiṉ, eṭuttalum paṭuttalum maṭṭum nikaḻum.
e-ṭu: vantāṉ, makaṉ
(2) coṟpiri viḻuttam

	kuṉṟēṟāmā	=	kuṉṟēṟā mā
(3) colvaki viḻuttam

āriyar teṉṉāṭuvantu tamiḻ kaṟṟut tamiḻāciri yarāṉapiṉ, iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol eṉṉum moḻiyiyalvakic coṟkaḷoṭu vaṭacolliyum cērttukkoṇṭatu pōṉṟē, peyarccol, viṉic col, iṭiccol eṉṉum ilakkaṇa vakic coṟkaḷoṭum uriccol eṉpatic cērttu, iruvaki       yilum coṟkaḷi nannāṉ kākkiṉar.
uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭkēyuriya col. col eṉpatu col vaṭiviyuṅ kuṟikkum. āriyar ayalārāṉa tiṉāl, tamakkut teriyāta aruñcoṟkaḷit tokuttup poruḷ urittuk koṇṭaṉar. itaṉ viḷakkam piṉṉark kāṇka.
(1) peyarccol
oru poruḷiṉ peyarē peyarccol. peyvatu peyar; peytal - iṭutal. peyariṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
peyarccol poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṉum aṟuporuḷpaṟṟi, aṟuvakippaṭum; irutiṇipaṟṟi, uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi, viravuttiṇi eṉa mūvakippaṭum; mūviṭam paṟṟi, taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkki eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ciṉi =  uṟuppu.
irutiṇikkum potuvāṉa peyar viravuttiṇip peyar allatu viravuppeyar eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu: āṇ, peṇ, tāy, piḷḷi.
taṉmi muṉṉilippeyarkaḷ, eṇpaṟṟi orumi paṉmi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, pālpaṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aivakippaṭum.
1. mūviṭap pakarappeyar (Personal Pronouns)
taṉmippeyar: 	orumi    paṉmi	iraṭṭippaṉmi
mutalnili	ēṉ	ēm	ēṅkaḷ	
2ām nili	yāṉ	yām	yāṅkaḷ	
3ām nili	nāṉ	nām	nāṅkaḷ
taṉmip peyariṉ vēṟṟumi yaṭikaḷ
ēṉ	ēm	ēṅkaḷ
yāṉ  - eṉ	yām -em	yāṅkaḷ-eṅkaḷ   
nāṉ-naṉ	nām-nam	nāṅkaḷ-naṅkaḷ 
yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum taṉittaṉmip paṉmip peyar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatiṉāl, iṉṟu ataṉ iṭattil nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmippeyar taṉ uḷappāṭṭup poruḷiyiḻantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ē eṉpatu uyarcci kuṟittalāl, iyalpāka māntaṉuk kuḷḷa nāṉ eṉṉum cerukku allatu taṉṉalampaṟṟi, ati aṭiyākak koṇṭu taṉmip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām. vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇiyar taṉmiyiṭatti uttama puruṭaṉ eṉṟum, āṅkilar ataṉi First- Person eṉṟum kuṟittatiyum nōkkuka.
ēpeṟ ṟākum.	(tol. uri. 7)
ekku, eṭṭu, eṇ, empu, evvu, eḻu, ēṇ, ēntu, ēr, ēṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷil ekara ēkāram uyarcci kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉṟu vaḻakkilillāta vaṭivaṅkaḷellām paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉa aṟika.
muṉṉilip peyar: orumi     paṉmi      iraṭṭippaṉmi
mutalnili	ūṉ)	(ūm)	(ūṅkaḷ)
2ām nili	(nūṉ)	(nūm)	(nūṅkaḷ)
3ām nili	nīṉ	nīm	nīṅkaḷ
4ām nili	nī	nīyir(nī + ir)	nīṅkaḷ    
piṟikkōṭṭiluḷḷavi vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
nī eṉpatu nīṉ eṉpataṉ kaṭikkuṟi. atu ir īṟupeṟṟu nīyir eṉṟākip piṉpu nīvir eṉat tirintu, nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ iṉṟum nelli vaṭṭā rattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilip peyariṉ vēṟṟumi yaṭikaḷ
(ūṉ)-uṉ	ūm)-um	(ūṅkaḷ)-uṅkaḷ
(nūṉ)-(nuṉ) 	(nūm)-num	(nūṅkaḷ)-nuṅkaḷ
nīṉ-niṉ	nīm-(nim)	nīṅkaḷ-(niṅkaḷ)
iṉṟu 4ām nili eḻuvāy vaṭivaṅkaṭkum mutalnili vēṟṟumi yaṭikaḷē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ ū eṉṉum muṉmic cuṭṭaṭiyāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
mutaṟkālap paṭarkkic cuṭṭuppeyar
orumi    	paṉmi      iraṭṭippaṉmi
mutalnili  (āṉ)    	(ām)              	(āṅkaḷ)
2ām nili  tāṉ     	tām             	tāṅkaḷ
paṭarkkip peyariṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷ
tāṉ-taṉ tām-tam tāṅkaḷ-taṅkaḷ
ā eṉṉum cēymic cuṭṭaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantuḷḷa tāṉ, tām eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, avaṉ, avaḷ mutaliya aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāy (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiviṭṭaṉa. `Auto' eṉṉum kirēkkac collum, mutaliṟ cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāy māṟiyuḷḷami iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ikkālap paṭarkkic cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ (Demonstrative Pronouns)  
cēymic cuṭṭuppeyar  : avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi
aṇmic cuṭṭuppeyar  : ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivi
muṉmic cuṭṭuppeyar  : uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi
ivi muṉṉark kāṭṭiya av, iv, uv, al, il, ul eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyaviyākum.
al-atu, il-itu, ul-utu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭip peyarkaḷ iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa; āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷ, ivarkaḷ, uvarkaḷ eṉpaṉa iraṭṭip paṉmi vaṭi vaṅkaḷākum.
makkaḷ nākarikamaṭintapiṉ, akavi aṟivu atikāram mutaliyavaṟṟil iḻintōṉik kuṟikka orumip peyariyum, ottōṉik kuṟikkap paṉmip peyariyum, uyarntōṉik kuṟikka iraṭṭip paṉmippeyariyum, muṉṉiliyilum paṭarkkiyilum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itu ulakavaḻakkēyaṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu.
taṉmiyiṭattiṟku iraṭṭippaṉmi vēṇṭā viṭiṉum,  oppumipaṟṟi atilum amintuḷḷatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya mūviṭappeyarkaḷuḷ, aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyar tavira ēṉiya vellām, ṉakara  meyyīṟṟāl orumi yiyum makara mey yīṟṟāṟ paṉmiyiyum, oḻuṅkāy uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivvīṟukaḷ tōṉṟiya vaki varumāṟu:
ollutal = poruntutal, oṉṟātal.
ol-oṉ = oṉṟu. oṉ-aṉ. o.nō: E one-an.
oṉ eṉṉuñ collē aṉ eṉṟu tirintu yāṉ (nāṉ), nīṉ, tāṉ, eṉṉum peyarkaḷil ṉakara meyyaḷavāka niṉṟu orumiyuṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṉṉīṟu uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē+aṉ = ēṉ, ū-aṉ = ūṉ, ā+aṉ = āṉ
o-nō: maka+aṉ = makaṉ. kō+aṉ = kōṉ (kōvaṉ)
ummutal-kūṭutal. um eṉṉum viṉi iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṉ aḷḷit tiripāṉa am eṉṉum collum, ataṉ vaḻiyaṭiyāṉa   kum eṉṉuñ collumē vaḻakkiluḷḷaṉa.
am-amal-amali = tiraḷi. amal-amar. amartal =  poruntutal. am-ami. amital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal.
am-ampu-ampal=kuvital, kuviyum mukiḻ. ampal-ampalam = avi, kūṭṭam, maṉṟam.
ma.ampalam, ka. ampala, tu. ampila.
ampalam-va. ampara. ciṟṟampalam-va. citampara (Cidambara). 
um-kum. kummutal = kūṭutal. kum-kummal. kum-kumpu-kumpal.
um eṉṉum col kūṭutalik kuṟittataṉālēyē, atu eṇṇupporuḷ allatu kūṭṭupporuḷiṭic col lākac koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu: eḻuttum collum poruḷum.
nāṉum varuvēṉ = nāṉkūṭa varuvēṉ, iti oru ciṟu piḷḷiyum ceytuviṭum = iti oru ciṟupiḷḷikūṭac ceytu viṭum.

iv vummic collē yām, nīm, tām eṉṉum peyarkaḷil makarameyyaḷavāka niṉṟu paṉmiyuṇarttu kiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār karuttum ituvē. ammīṟu pōṉṟē ummīṟum, uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē+am = ēm, ū+am = ūm, ā+am = ām
o-nō: maṇa+am = maṇam, kā+am = kām (āci)
aimpālīṟukaḷiṉ varalāṟṟip piṉṉark kāṇka.
mūvakic cuṭṭuppeyar muṟiyilēyē e, ē, yā, eṉṉum viṉā vaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu aimpāl viṉāppeyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. 
2. viṉāppeyarkaḷ (Interrogative Pronouns)
e: evaṉ, evaḷ, evar (evarkaḷ), etu, evi

3. paṭarkkippeyarp pākupāṭukaḷ
taṉmiyum muṉṉiliyumaṉṟip paṭarvatu paṭarkki. paṭartal = parantu cellutal.
(1) aṟuporuṭpeyar
ellāp poruḷkaḷum poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvakippaṭum. ātalāl avaṟṟiṉ peyarum aṟuvakiyām.
poruḷ eṉṟatu. kāṭcipporuḷākavō karuttup poruḷākavō uḷḷa oru taṉipporuḷi.
ētēṉum oru poruḷiruppiṉ, atu irukka iṭamum kālamum vēṇṭum. poruḷkaḷ iyaṟkiyāyiṉum ceyaṟ kiyā yiṉum, perumpālum pala vuṟuppukaḷiyuṭiyaṉa. ovvoru poruṭkum cila taṉmikaḷuṇṭu. at taṉmi kaḷē uṟuppiyum uṭaliyum tuṇikkoṇṭu toḻilāka veḷippaṭum. iṅṅaṉam āyntu poruḷkaḷi āṟāka vakuttatum avaṟṟi muṟip paṭuttiyatum, viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟivaṉ muṉivaṉāṉa mutaṉūlāciriyaṉiṉ, meypporu ḷaṟiviyum tarukkat tiṟaṉiyum teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum.
kuṟiyīṭṭip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kuṇam eṉiṉum paṇpu eṉiṉum okkum.
muttiṇippeyar
uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi, av viraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa viravuttiṇi eṉat tiṇi mūvakippaṭum.
e-ṭu: 	uyartiṇippeyar-avaṉ, māntaṉ, murukaṉ
        	aḵṟiṇippeyar -atu, maram, kal
      	viravuttiṇippeyar-tāy, piḷḷi, āṇ, peṇ
	tiṇ-tiṇi = tiraṭci, tokuti, vakuppu.
aimpāṟpeyar
āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl eṉa uyartiṇippāl mūṉṟum, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aḵṟiṇippāl iraṇṭum ākap pāl aintām.
paku-pakal-pāl = pirivu.
āṇpāl, peṇpāl eṉṉum iraṇṭum orumip pālkaḷ. āṇpeṇ vēṟupāṭu aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷuḷḷum iruppiṉum, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ ataṟku ilakkaṇattil iṭan taravilli. ēṉeṉiṉ, moḻiyi amitta potu makkaḷē aṅṅaṉam amittuviṭṭaṉar. ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ ilak kaṇam iyamputalē nūlāciriyaṉ ceyal.
kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaḷ kūrmatiyum uyariya oḻukkamum uṭiyarātaliṉ, nallatik kaṭippiṭittut tīyati nīkkum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta pakuttaṟivi aḷaviyākak koṇṭu, atiyuṭiya makkaḷi uyartiṇi yeṉṟum, aḵtil lātavaṟṟi yellām uyiriruppiṉum illāviṭiṉum aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟum vakuttu, ataṟkēṟpa, makaṉ varukiṟāṉ, makaḷ varukiṟāḷ eṉṟu uyartiṇiyiṟ pāl pirittum, kāḷi varukiṟatu, āvu varukiṟatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇiyiṟpāl pirikkātum, ellāp peyarc coṟkaḷiyum viṉiccoṟ kaḷiyum amittu viṭṭami, eṉṟum tamiḻaṉ eṇṇi makiḻtaṟkum taṉ muṉṉōrippaṟṟip perumi pārāṭṭutaṟkum uriya toṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam vēṟem moḻiyilu millāta ciṟanta ilakkaṇavamippu, iṟṟip pula makkaḷiṉum aṟṟip potu makkaḷ aṟivāṟṟalilum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭilum cālac ciṟantavareṉpataṟku takka moḻic cāṉṟām.

(3) kālap peyar
viṭiyaliliruntu oru nāḷ pappattu nāḻiki koṇṭa āṟu ciṟupoḻutākavum, mēḻa (cittiri) mātattiliruntu ōr āṇṭu ivviru mātaṅkoṇṭa āṟu perum poḻutākavum, vakukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟupoḻutu
kāli, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu (cāyuṅkālam), māli, yāmam, vikaṟi.
naṇpakal naṭuviṟku uccivēḷi, urumam eṉpaṉa tūya ulaka vaḻakkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷ. uccivēḷikku muntiya pakal muṟpakal; pintiya pakal piṟpakal. 
perumpoḻutu
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār (maḻi), kūtar (kuḷir), muṉpaṉi,    piṉpaṉi. vēṉil eṉpatu kōṭi.
(4) ciṉippeyar (Names of organs)
nilattiṇi: vēr, aṭi, kiḷi, ili, pū, kāy, kaṉi mutaliyaṉa.
iyaṅkutiṇi: pātam, kāl, ari, vayiṟu, mārpu, kaḻuttu, tali mutaliyaṉa.
(5) kuṇappeyar (Abstract Noun)
vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, aḷavu, cuvi, ūṟu, nāṟṟam, iyal, ceyal eṉak kuṇam eṇvakippaṭum.
vaṇṇam
veḷḷi, karuppu, nīlam, pacci, mañcaḷ, civappu, kattari (violet) eṉa vaṇṇam eḻuvaki.
nīr-nīl-nīlam = kaṭalnīriṉ niṟam. nīr = kaṭal. taliciṟanta nīr
niliyum piṟa nīrnilikaṭku mūla māyiruppatu kaṭalē.
nīrolit taṉṉa		(maturik. 369)
kāṉak kōḻiyu nīrniṟak kākkiyum                	(cilap. 10:116)
kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ = tirumāl.
nīḷ-nīr. nilam niṟpatu; nīr nīḷvatu. kattari (ppū) eṉpatu 	       ulaka vaḻakku. cilar ati īral niṟam eṉpar.
kāḷivakiyil pukar (kapilam), pulli, mayili, karumayili mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; kōḻivakiyil cāmpal, kamparici mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; āṭivakiyil kempu, kāvi, nīrkkāvi, paḻukkāvi, (orange), tuvar, kaḷip pākku, veṅkāyam, māntuḷir, kiḷippacci, pācippayaṟu, īyam, iḷanīlam, mayiṟkaḻuttu, vāṉavil, mukilvaṇṇam (mēkavarṇam) mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; makkaḷ mēṉi vakiyil taṅkam, pacali, putuniṟam, mā mutaliya niṟap peyar kaḷum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar eḵkucceviyum kūrṅkaṇṇum nuṇmatiyum uṭiyarātaliṉ, vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyellām nuṭpa māy vakuttu avaṟṟiṟku vevvēṟu peyariṭṭiruntaṉar. paṇṭi yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷvaṭivilēyē iruntati ṉālum, aviyum aṭiyōṭu iṟantupaṭṭamiyālum, pala vaṇṇap peyarkaḷum iṟantoḻintaṉa. iṉṟum mīṉpeyarkaḷil veṇ ṇiṟavakikaḷik kuṟikka veḷḷi, veḷḷi, veṇṇey, veḷuvi mutaliya coṟkaḷ āḷappeṟṟiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
vaṭivu
vaṭṭam, cataram, mukkōṇam mutaliya parappu vaṭivaṅkaḷum, uruṇṭi, uruḷi, kūmpu mutaliya kaṉavaṭivu kaḷum, palvakiya  vaṭṭam, caturam eṉpaviyum teṉ coṟkaḷē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu' eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkappeṟum.
aḷavu
eṇṇal, eṭuttal, mukattal, nīṭṭal, eṉa aḷavi nālvaki.
eṇṇal
eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ ciṟṟilakkam (kīḻvāyilakkam), pēri lakkam (mēlvāyilakkam) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. ciṟṟilakkamum kīḻvāy, mēlvāy eṉa iru tiṟattatu.

toṇṭutali yiṭṭa pattukkuṟi yeḻunūṟṟu 	(tol. 1358)
``toṭittirivaṉṉa toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ         		(malipaṭu. 21)
toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ muṇṭaka nalyāḻ     (cilap. pa. 221. kuṟippuri)
toṇṭu eṉṉuñcol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōkavē, aḵtirunta oṉṟām iṭattiṟkut toṇpatu (oṉpatu) eṉṉum iraṇṭām iṭappeyarum, iraṇṭām iṭattiṟkut toṇṇūṟu eṉṉum mūṉṟām iṭappeyarum, mūṉṟām iṭattiṟkut toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum nālām iṭappeyarum vantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nālām iṭappeyarukku mēliṭaccol iṉmiyāl, toṇpatu eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa oṉpatu eṉṉuñ colloṭu āyiram eṉpatic cērttu, oṉpatāyiram allatu oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉṟu collavēṇṭi yatāyiṟṟu.
oṉṟu mutal pattuvariyuḷḷa peyarkaḷellām taṉic collāyiruppatiyum, oṉpatu eṉpatu kūṭṭuccollāyum patu (pattu) eṉṟu muṭivatāyum iruppatiyum, nōkkuka.

ilakkam	:	ilakku=kuṟi. ilakku-ilakkam = kuṟi, 	eṇkuṟi, 	eṇ, pēreṇ.
kōṭi	:	kuṭu-kuṭumi = ucci, taliyucci, āṭavar tali mayirkkaṟṟi, paṟaviccūṭṭu, makuṭam, māṭavucci, maliyucci, nuṉi. kuṭu-koṭu. koṭumuṭi = mali yucci. koṭu-	kōṭi = nuṉi, muṉi, kaṭici, elli.
		kaṭikōṭi eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. terukkōṭi viṟkōṭi eṉpaṉavum muṉiyik kuṟittal kāṇka. 	kōṭi kaṭici yeṇṇāta lāl appeyar koṇṭatu.
tāmari, kuvaḷi, neytal, āmpal mutaliya malarp peyarkaḷ ovvōr itaḻukkum; caṅkam (caṅku) eṉpatu ovvoru purikkum (vaḷiviṟkum); oru peruntokiyāka uṟiyiṭal muṟiyilum; nāṭu, vāraṇam, veḷḷam eṉpaṉa pēraḷavupaṟṟiyum; kaṇikam(nūṟukōṭi) eṉpatu kaṇippuppaṟṟiyum; palvēṟu aṭukkiya kōṭip peyarāyiṉa. eṭṭut tāmari koṇṭatu oru nāṭu.
paratam = ilakkam kōṭik kōṭākōṭi. itu 1-ṉ piṉ 24 cuṉṉaṅkoṇṭatu. paratam eṉpatu kaṭal vaṇikattiṉ peyar.
paṭavaṉ-paravaṉ-paratavaṉ-paratavam-paratam. iṉi, parata kaṇṭam eṉiṉumām. parataṉ tiṅkaṭkulattic cērnta oru paḻam pāṇṭiyaṉē.

cuvippeyar
kippu (kacappu), kārppu (uṟippu), iṉippu, puḷippu, uvarppu , tuvarppu eṉac cuvi āṟām.
ūṟṟup peyar
vemmi, taṇmi, vaṉmi, meṉmi, noymmi (paḷuviṉmi), cīrmi (paḷuvu), iḻumeṉal (vaḻuvaḻuppu), caruccari (curacurappu) eṉa ūṟu eṭṭām.
nāṟṟap peyar
naṟu nāṟṟam, tī nāṟṟam eṉa nāṟṟam iraṇṭām.
iyaṟpaṇpup peyar
e-ṭu: naṉmi, tīmi, matimi, maṭami.
ceyaṟpaṇpuppeyar
e-ṭu: aṉpu, paki, aṭakkam, ciṉam, poṟumi, poṟāmi.      viṉiyāka veḷippaṭuvatum, viṉippeyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭuvatum ceyaṟpaṇpām.
(6) toḻiṟpeyar (Verbal Noun)
toḻiṟpeyar eṉiṉum viṉippeyar eṉiṉum okkum. ciṟuviṉi peruviṉi eṉa viṉi (toḻil) iruvakip paṭum. varutal, pōtal, uṇṭal, uṭuttal, pōṉṟavi ciṟuviṉi; uḻavu, necavu, taccu, kalvi, āṭci pōṉṟavi peruviṉi.
ellāp peyarkaḷum piṉvarumāṟu vevvēṟu vakiyil ivviru pāṟpaṭum.
(1) potup peyar x ciṟappup peyar
pala poruṭkup potuvāṉa peyar potuppeyar; oṉṟaṟkē ciṟappāka vuriyatu ciṟappup peyar.
e-ṭu: āṟu, nakar, pulavaṉ-potuppeyar (Common Noun)
	     kāviri, maturi, nakkīraṉ- ciṟappup peyar (Proper Noun)
aracaṉ allatu vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyari nōkka. pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatu potuviṟ ciṟappum, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarinōkka, taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟappiṟ ciṟappum ākum.
(2) iyaṟpeyar x ākupeyar
iyalpāka oru poruṭku uṇṭāṉa peyar iyaṟpeyar; oru poruḷiṉ peyar iṉṉoṉṟaṟku ētēṉumoru toṭar pil āki varuvatu ākupeyar.
e-ṭu: 	kāḷi (māṭu), poṉ (kaṉiyam), vaṟṟal(viṉi)-		        			iyaṟpeya r.
	kāḷi (maṟavaṉ),poṉ(poṟkācu), vaṟṟal				(vaṟṟiya kāykaṉi)-ākupeyar (Metonymy, Syn	ecdoche)
(3) taṉinilippeyar  x kuḻūup peyar
oru taṉippaṭṭa poruḷiṉ peyar  taṉinilippeyar; pala poruḷ cērnta kūṭṭattiṉ peyar kuḻūuppeyar.
e-ṭu: kāy, āṭu, tamiḻaṉ -taṉinilippeyar
kuli, manti, kaḻakam -kuḻūup peyar (Collective Noun)
iṭukuṟippeyar eṉpatē tamiḻil illi. ellāp peyarum karaṇiyap peyarē. itaṉ viḷakkatti eṉ paṇṭit tamiḻ nākarikamum paṇpāṭum eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē	(640)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviyum nōkkuka. tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyātalālum potumakkaḷ amitta vaḻakku moḻi yātalālum, ataṉ ellāc coṟkaḷum karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷākavē amintuḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻi tirimoḻiyātalālum pulamakkaḷ amitta ceyaṟki nūṉmoḻi yātalālum, atil iṭukuṟip peyarkaḷ pala amintuḷḷaṉa.
oruvar tam makkaḷukkiṭum āṭpeyar teyvappeyarkaḷum, tamakkuc ciṟantāriyum tam vaḻipaṭu teyvattiyum niṉivukūrtaṟ poruṭṭēyeṉa aṟika.
(2) viṉiccol
viḷi - viṉi. o.nō : vaḷi - vaṉi, muḷi - muṉi (kalit. 4)
viḷivatu allatu viḷintu payaṉ (naṉṟu allatu tītu) taruvatu  viṉi.
muṟpakaṟ ceyyiṟ piṟpakal viḷiyum.	(ko.vē. 74)
viṉiviḷi kāla mātaliṉ	(cilap. 16:148)
nalviṉi tīviṉiyik kuṟitta ciṟappuccol, ilakkaṇat til, nallatum tīyatum iraṇṭumallatum ciṟiyatum periyatumāṉa ellāc ceyalkaṭkum potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
mutaṟkālattil, viṉimutaṉiliyē irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭa mukkālap potuviṉiyākavum toḻiṟpeyarākavum, iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. akkālattu, muṟṟu eccam eṉṉum vēṟupāṭiruntirukka muṭiyātu.
e-ṭu : 	cey = ceyki. peruñcey yāṭavar   (neṭunal. 171)
il = illi. pittariṟ pētiyār il (nālaṭi. 52)
karayuṉṉa kuṭṭikkē pāl uḷḷu, vampaṉōṭu vaḻutu nallu eṉṉum maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil uḷ, nal eṉṉum kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟaṭikaḷē, uṇṭu, naṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka. iṉṟum aṭi, kuti, kol, ciri, tāṅku, naṭi, paṟi, miti, viḻi mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta viṉi mutaṉilikaḷ mukkālattiṟkum potuvāṉa toḻiṟ peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippuviṉi pōṉṟē, mutaṉili yaḷavāyuḷḷa mutaṟkāla viṉiyum muṉpiṉ varum coṟṟuṇikkoṇṭu mukkālamum kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum.

poruṇmāli yāḷari yuḷḷa maruṇmāli
  māyumeṉ māyā vuyir		(1230)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷkaḷil, `malarum', `māyum' eṉpaṉa malarkiṉṟatu, māy kiṉṟatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka. āyiṉum, tolkāppiyar kālattil mukkāla viṉiyum nikaḻkāla viṉit taṉi vaṭivum illi pōlum eṉa mayaṅkaṟka.

 tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷcey kunavum	(735)
aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭalum	(1177)
eṉṟu ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkki. nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉ marūu vaṭivākiya ceykuna eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷi avarē āḷutalāṉum, avar kālattum avi uṇṭeṉat teḷika.
3 ām nilippaṭṭa ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu, muṟiyē ceykuṉṉu, ceyyuṉṉu eṉat tiriyum. aṅṅaṉamē, 4 ām nilippaṭṭa ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟum, muṟiyē ceykuṉṉāṉ, ceyyuṉ ṉāṉ, ceyyuṉaṉ eṉat tiriyum. āyiṉ, iṟutivaṭivil īṟṟayaleḻuttut tannakaramāka eḻutappeṟum. ataṉāl ceyyunaṉ eṉṟākum. atu nikaḻkāla viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar.
pālaṟi marapiṟ porunar kaṇṇum	(102)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya aṭiyiluḷḷa porunar eṉpatu iṅṅaṉam aminta viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarē. makiḻnaṉ, vāḻnaṉ (vāṇaṉ)  eṉpaṉavum ittakiyavē.
vīḻunar vīḻap paṭuvārk kamiyumē
 vāḻunam eṉṉuñ cerukku		(kuṟaḷ. 1193)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, vīḻunar eṉpatu nikaḻkāla viṉiyā laṇiyum peyar; vāḻunam eṉpatu taṉmip paṉmi nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu. vāḻkiṉṟām - vāḻuṉṉām - vāḻuṉṉam - vāḻunam.
ceyyuṉṉu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu vaṭivam, iṉṟum paḻañcēranāṭṭut tamiḻt tiripākiya maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
3ām nilippaṭṭa ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉimuṟṟu, maliyāḷattiṟpōṉṟē tamiḻilum irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭap potuviṉiyāyiruntu, piṉpu taṉmi muṉṉiliyiṭaṅkaṭkum palarpāṟ paṭarkkikkum vilakkappaṭṭatu.
3ām niliyiṟ pālīṟu peṟātirunta mukkāla viṉi muṟṟukaḷ 4 ām niliyil avaṟṟip peṟṟaṉa.

i.kā.	ni.kā.	e.kā.
ceytāṉ 	ceykiṉṟāṉ  	ceyvāṉ
ceytāḷ  	ceykiṉṟāḷ 	ceyvāḷ
ceytār   	ceykiṉṟār 	ceyvār
ceytatu     	ceykiṉṟatu   	ceyvatu
ceyta         	ceykiṉṟa     	ceyva
ceytāṉ eṉpatu ceytaṉaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivuṅ koḷḷum. ceytaṉaṉ eṉpatiluḷḷa aṉaṉ eṉṉum īṟṟi aṉ + aṉ eṉṟu pirittu, muṉṉatic cāriyiyākavum, piṉṉatip pālīṟākavuṅ koḷvar. iṅṅaṉam koḷḷātu aṉaṉ eṉpatē ōr īṟeṉṟu koḷḷiṉum poruntum. aṉṉaṉ, aṉṉavaṉ, aṉṉāṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷ attakiyaṉ eṉṟu maṭṭumaṉṟi avaṉ eṉṟum poruḷ paṭum. ātalāl, aṉaṉ eṉpatē īṟākavu mirukkalām.
e-ṭu : 
i.kā.	ni.kā.	e.kā.
ceytaṉaṉ	ceykiṉṟaṉaṉ	ceyvaṉaṉ
ceytaṉaḷ	ceykiṉṟaṉaḷ	ceyvaṉaḷ
ceytaṉar	ceykiṉṟaṉar	ceyvaṉar
ceytaṉṟu	...................
ceytaṉa	ceykiṉṟaṉa	ceyvaṉa
`niṉivaṉaḷ' eṉṉum kalittokic colliyum (44), `pārppaṉappakkam' eṉṉum tolkāppiyat toṭariyum (1021) nōkkuka.
ceytaṉatu eṉṟirukkavēṇṭiya oṉṟaṉpāl iṟantakāla viṉimu ṟṟu, iṉṉōcipaṟṟic ceytaṉṟu eṉat tokkatu. ṉakaram takarattōṭu puṇariṉ ṟakaramām. aṉatu eṉpatu ōr īṟāyiṉum, aṉ + atu eṉṟu piriyum. aṉ' cuṭṭaṭiyum atu' oṉṟaṉpālīṟum ākum. aṉ + tu - aṉṟu. im muṟiyilēyē, aṇintaṉṟu (1), iḻintaṉṟu (77), iṟantaṉṟu (217), kaṇṭaṉṟu (61), kaḷintaṉṟu (77), ciṟantaṉṟu (75), ceyirttaṉṟu (226), ceṟṟaṉṟu (226), tīrntaṉṟu (42), makiḻntaṉṟu (192), malintaṉṟu (77), viyantaṉṟu (77) eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟuc coṟkaḷellām tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iv vīṟupeṟṟa nikaḻkāla etirkāla oṉṟaṉpāl viṉimuṟṟukaḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa.
1. iṟantakāla viṉi
iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟu iṟṟi viṉiyecca vaṭivil niṉṟē pālīṟu peṟṟatiṉāl, atip pakukkumpōtum viṉiyecca vaṭivum pālīṟumākavē pakuttal vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu : 	ceytāṉ = ceytu + āṉ
       		kaṇṭāṉ = kaṇṭu + āṉ
        		ceṉṟāṉ = ceṉṟu + āṉ
tūṅkiṉāṉ, ōṭiṉāṉ eṉpavi, muṟiyē, tūṅkiyāṉ (tūṅki + āṉ), ōṭiyāṉ (ōṭi + āṉ) eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripākum. yakaram nakara (ṉakara) mākat tirital iyalpē.
o.nō : yāṉ-nāṉ, ōṭiya-ōṭiṉa
tūṅkiṉaṉ, ōṭiṉaṉ eṉpaṉa, tūṅkiṉāṉ, ōṭiṉāṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkalākum.
ceytaṉṟu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc collāṉa ākiṉṟu eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭuc col (148) ikaram tokkatu.
āṉāṉ, pōṉāṉ eṉpavi āyāṉ (āy + āṉ), pōyāṉ (pōy + āṉ) eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripākum. ceytāṉ eṉṉum viṉi muṟṟu vāypāṭṭi āyāṉ eṉpatu ottiruppatu pōṉṟē, ceyta eṉṉum peyarecca vāypāṭṭiyum āya (āy + a) eṉpatu ottiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi āyiṉāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaviyum āyiyāṉ, pōyi yāṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripē.
āy, pōy eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ mutalil āyi, pōyi eṉa ikaravīṟṟaṉavā yiruntu, piṉpu yakaramey yīṟṟaṉavāka maruviyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
o.nō : nā + i = nāi - nāyi - nāy = nīṇṭu toṅkum nāviyuṭiyatu. ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum eṉṉum tol kāppiya nūṟpāviṟku, iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum `nāy, nāi' eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruttal kāṇka. ivvakiyilēyē, pā + i = pāi -pāyi - pāy (parantatu) eṉa varum. ivi peyarāyiṉum, i - y eṉat tirinta muṟi oṉṟē.
āyiṉ, ā, pō eṉṉum viṉimutaṉilikaḷ ikarattōṭu variṉ vakara vuṭampaṭumeyyaṉṟō varumeṉiṉ, āyiṭi, māyiru, pāyiruḷ, kōyil eṉa varupavaṟṟi nōkkit teḷika. vakara vuṭampaṭumey vaṭiviṉum yakara vuṭampaṭumey vaṭivē palukkaṟ keḷitāyiruttal kāṇka.
pōyiyatu eṉpatu, pōyiṉatu - pōyiṉṟu - pōyiṟṟu eṉat tiriyum. atu eṉṉum īṟu tu' eṉak kuṟukumpōtu, puṇarcciyāl ṟu, ṭu eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu :	nal + tu = naṉṟu,     pāl + tu = pāṟṟu
       	uḷ + tu = uṇṭu,    tāḷ + tu = tāṭṭu
       	maṉ + tu = maṉṟu,     aṉ + tu = aṟṟu
       	viṇ + tu = viṇṭu,   kaṇ + tu = kaṭṭu
	maṉṉutal = kūṭutal, poruntutal.
colliyāṉ, colliyatu eṉpaṉa, colliṉāṉ, colliṉatu eṉṟu tirintu, piṉpu coṉṉāṉ, coṉṉatu eṉa maruvum. col liṉatu eṉpatu, colliṉṟu-colliṟṟu eṉat tiriyum.
pōya-pōṉa, eṇṇiya-eṇṇiṉa, pōyatu-pōṉatu, ēviyōṉ-ēviṉōṉ eṉap pala viṉiccoṟkaḷ iruvaṭivuṅ kāṭṭi niṟpiṉum, pōyiyāṉ, tūṅkiyāṉ mutaliya vaḻakkaṟṟa āṉīṟṟu viṉimuṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iyaṟkikku māṟākat tōṉṟalām. itaṟku avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkaṟṟa taṉmiyē karaṇiyam. muṅkiṉāl eṉpatupōl tamiḻil ṉakaravaṭivil varum viṉiyec caṅkaḷellām, cēranāṭṭu allatu maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil yakara vaṭivilēyē iṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
e-ṭu: 
āṇāyāl oru peṇṇu vēṇṭē!
	āḻam muṅṅiyāl kuḷirilla.
	ellārum taṇṭil kayaṟiyāl eṭuppāṉ āḷvēṇṭē!
	ēṟak kiḻakkōṭṭup pōyāl paṭiññāṭṭu.
	kakkuvāṉ tuṭaṅṅiyāl nilkkuvāṉ parikkōṇam.
	koñcaṉ tuḷḷiyāl muṭṭōḷam, ēṟa tuḷḷiyāl caṭṭiyil.
	cakkara kūṭṭiyāl kampiḷiyum tiṉṉām.
	cāṇ veṭṭiyāl muḻam nīḷām.
	ceṟuviral vīṅṅiyāl peruvaralōḷam.
	talayaṇa māṟiyāl talakkēṭu poṟukkumō?
	tūpam kāṭṭiyālum pāpam pōkā.
	pāṟṟi tuppiyāl paḷḷiyāyilum tuppām.
	poṉ cūci kuttiyālum kaṇṇupōm.
	mūvar kūṭiyāl muṟṟam aṭikkā.
	vaḷarccuk keṭṭiyāl etti nōkkum.
iṉi, tamiḻilum tūṅkiyāṉ, tāviyāṉ eṉavarum āṉīṟṟu viṉimuṟṟukaḷ, tūṅkiyōṉ, tāviyōṉ eṉa ōvīṟṟu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷāyiṉ iyalpā yiruttaliyum,
eṇṇiyār eṇṇam iḻappar iṭaṉaṟintu
 tuṉṉiyār tuṉṉic ceyiṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 494)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil viṉimuṟṟāka āḷappaṭakkūṭiya eṇṇiyār, tuṉṉiyār eṉṉum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷ yakaraṅ koṇṭa vaṭivil vantiruntaliyum, kaṇṭu teḷika.
iṉi, ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu ukaravīṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattiṉ amippi nōkkiṉ, atu tuvvīṟṟoṭu kūṭiya viṉimutaṉili yeṉṟē terikiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu : 	cey+tu=ceytu, paṭi+tu=paṭittu
		koḷ+tu=koṇṭu, uṇ+tu=uṇṭu
		kēḷ+tu=kēṭṭu
		nil+tu=niṉṟu, eṉ+tu=eṉṟu
		kal+tu=kaṟṟu.
it tuvvīṟu, atu eṉṉum īṟṟiṉ mutaṟkuṟiyē. akaram cēymic cuṭṭātalāl, atu iṟantakālatti uṇarttiṟṟu.
cila taṉikkuṟi laṭutta ku, ṭu, ṟu īṟṟu viṉimutaṉilikaḷ, iṟutivali yiraṭṭittu iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	puku-pukku, pukka, pukkāṉ
	      	cuṭu-cuṭṭu, cuṭṭa, cuṭṭāṉ
	      	aṟu-aṟṟu, aṟṟa, aṟṟāṉ
iṅṅaṉam viṉimutaṉilikaḷ tu, i, y īṟupeṟṟum, iṟuti valiyiraṭṭittum, iruvakiyāka iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭum.
ākavē,
taṭaṟavoṟ ṟiṉṉē aimpāl mūviṭat
 tiṟanta kālan taruntoḻi liṭinili		(naṉ.142)
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatu, moḻiyārāycci mikka ikkālattiṟ kēṟkāteṉa aṟika.
iraṇṭām mūṉṟām nilikaṭkuriya ceytu eṉṉum vāy pāṭṭup paṇṭi iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟu, piṟkālattil cōḻapāṇṭi nāṭukaḷil ulakavaḻakkaṟṟuppōy, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil taṉmi yiṭattiṟkē variyaṟukkappaṭṭu, paṉmikku ummīṟu peṟṟatu.
e-ṭu : 	vantu  = vantēṉ,		vantum = vantōm
	kaṇṭu = kaṇṭēṉ,  	kaṇṭum = kaṇṭōm
	ceṉṟu = ceṉṟēṉ,  	ceṉṟum = ceṉṟōm
iṭu eṉṉum viṉipōṉṟē, ī eṉṉum viṉiyum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattōṭu kūṭit tuṇiviṉiyāy varum.
e-ṭu: 	vantiṭu, vantiṭār.
		aṟintīyār=aṟiyār (puṟam. 136).
īntār eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟu īyiṉār eṉṟum varum. o. nō: pōntār, pōyiṉār.
aṟintīyiṉār-aṟintīyiṉōr (viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar)
-aṟintīciṉōr-aṟinticiṉōr.
eṉṟīyiṉār-eṉṟīyiṉōr-eṉṟīciṉōr-eṉṟiciṉōr.
yakaram cakaramākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e-ṭu: ī+al=īyal-īcal. iḷ-(iy)-ī-īyam=eḷitāy iḷakum kaṉiyam (ulōkam). īyam-sīsam (va.). neyavu-necavu. nēyam-nēcam. piyaṉ-payaṉ-pacaṉ. vāyil-vāyal-vācal. mayaṅku-macaṅku.
piṟkālattār, colvaralā ṟaṟiyātu aṟinticiṉōr, eṉṟi ciṉōr eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iciṉ eṉṉum iṭippakutiyip pirit teṭuttu, ati iṟantakāla iṭinili eṉṟaṉar.
(2) nikaḻkāla viṉi
iṟantakālattiṟkum etirkālattiṟkum iruppatupōl tiṭṭamāṉa ellivarampu nikaḻkālattiṟku iṉmiyāṉum, mikak kuṟukiya kāla aḷavil nikaḻkālattiṟkē iṭamiṉmi yāṉum, etirkālattōṭu ataṟku neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷa miyāṉum, ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉimuṟṟē nikaḻ kālattiyum mutalil uṇartti vantatu.
piṉpu, kil eṉṉum āṟṟal viṉiyiṉ iṟantakāla viṉi yeccamākiya kiṉṟu eṉpatu, viṉimutaṉiliyoṭu kūṭi aimpāl mūviṭap potuvāṉa nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟā yamintatu.
e-ṭu:
orutaṉi vikiṟ pulampā kiṉṟē.	(kuṟun. 166)
kiṟṟal=āṟṟutal, ceyyamāṭṭutal. ceyyakiṟpēṉ= ceyyamāṭṭuvēṉ, eṉṉāl ceyyamuṭiyum. mutalām puṟappāṭ ṭil varum ākiṉṟu eṉpatu, kiṉṟu eṉṉum īṟu koṇṭataṉṟu, āyiṟṟu eṉṟu iṟantakālap poruḷ tarum āyiṉṟu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭatu. āyiṉatu-āyiṉṟu-āyiṟṟu. ākiyatu-ākiṉatu-ākiṉṟu.
ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum iṭikkāla nikaḻkāla viṉi muṟṟu, piṉpu, pālīṟupeṟṟuc ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu ceyyuṉṉu eṉa maruviṟṟu. ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum, ceyyuṉṉāṉ-ceyyuṉaṉ-ceynaṉ eṉṟum, ceykuṉṉāṉ-ceykuṉaṉ-ceykunaṉ eṉṟum maruvum. aṟinaṉ-(aṟiñaṉ) eṉpatum it tiripē.
ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu ceykiṟāṉ eṉat tokkapiṉ, kiṟu eṉṉum vaṭivum nikaḻkāla iṭiniliyākak koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
36ām puṟappāṭṭil, aṭuni, viṭuni eṉpavi nikaḻkālac collēyāyiṉum, nikaḻkālattiṟkum etirkālat tiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpupaṟṟiyum, etirkāla viṉiyi nikaḻkālac collāṟ kuṟikkum vaḻakkup paṟṟiyum, avaṟṟiṟku etirkālap poruḷ kūṟap paṭṭatu. nāḷi varukiṟēṉ, aṭutta āṇṭu tarukiṟēṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
aṭukiṉṟi-aṭukuṉṉi-(aṭuṉṉi)-aṭuni.
āniṉṟu eṉpatu uṇmiyāṉa nikaḻkāla iṭinili yaṉṟu. atu ceytuniṉṟāṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ceyyā niṉṟāṉ eṉṉum viṉiyeccat toṭarukku nikaḻkāla viṉi muṟṟup poruḷ kaṟpittu, ataṉ iṭippakutiyip pirittuk koṇṭa uriyāciriyar paṭippē. atit taḻuviyē, naṉṉū lārum nikaḻkāla viṭi nilikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka ataṉic cērttuk koṇṭār.
(3) etirkāla viṉi
ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉ īṟāṉa um eṉpatu muṉmic cuṭṭāṉa ukara vaṭiyiṟ piṟantu, kāla muṉṉāṉa muṟkālattiyum iṭamuṉṉāṉa etirkālattiyum uṇarttum colluṟuppākum.
ummi viṉivan turutta loḻiyātu 	(maṇimē. 26: 32)
eṉpatil muṟpiṟappiyum.
ummi-erivāy nirayattu vīḻvarkol 	(nālaṭi. 58)
eṉpatil maṟumiyiyum, ummiccol uṇarttutal kāṇka.
ceyyum eṉpatu piṉpu pālīṟu peṟṟu, ceyyumāṉ-ceyyu vāṉ-ceyvāṉ eṉat tirintatu. im muṟiyil aminta mūviṭa aimpāl viṉimuṟṟukaḷāvaṉa:
taṉmi orumi: 
(ceyyumēṉ)-ceyyuvēṉ-ceyvēṉ-ceyveṉ -ceyvaṉ-ceyval.
paṉmi:(ceyyumēm)-ceyyuvēm-ceyvēm-ceyvem.
(ceyyumām)-ceyyuvām-ceyvām-ceyvam.
(ceyyumōm)-ceyyuvōm-ceyvōm.

pe.pā:

(uṇṇuma)-uṇṇuva
(naṭakkuma)-naṭakkuva-naṭappa
ceymār eṉṉum palarpāl etirkāla viṉimuṟṟu, piṟkālattil muṟṟeccamākavē āḷappeṟṟatu.
mārik kiḷaviyum pallōr paṭarkki
 kālak kiḷaviyoṭu muṭiyum eṉpa.	(tol. 692)
e-ṭu: uṇmār vantār.
eṉmarum uḷarē eṉṉum naṉṉūl toṭaril (421) uḷḷa eṉmar eṉṉum col, ceymar eṉṉum vāypāṭṭa tākum.
eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṉum tolkāppiyat toṭaril (6) uḷḷa eṉmaṉār eṉṉum col, makaṉār eṉṉum uyarvup paṉmip peyarpōl ārīṟēṟṟa ceymaṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc collāy, eṉpa eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻakkampaṟṟi vanta kālavaḻuvamiti.
ceypa eṉṉum palarpāl etirkāla viṉimuṟṟu, ceyva eṉṉum palaviṉpāṟ colliṉiṉṟu tirikkappeṟṟatākum.
vakarattiṟku iṉamāṉa pakaram, ciṟupāṉmi vakarampōṟ kakarattiṟkup pōliyāka varum.
e-ṭu: iṟakka-iṟappa. viḻikka-viḻippa
ceyyumār eṉṉum palarpāl viṉimuṟṟu viṉiyāla ṇiyum peyarākumpōtu ceyyumōr eṉṟun tiriyum.
e-ṭu: vēṟṟumi teripa uṇaru mōrē.	(tol. 580)
	maṭalvaṉ pōntiyiṉ niṟku mōrkkē	(puṟam. 297)
ceyvēṉ eṉṉum taṉmiyorumi etirkāla viṉi muṟṟu, pōlivakiyil ceykēṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
va-ka, pōli.o.nō: civappu-cikappu, tuvar-tukir, āvā-ākā   		(iṭiccol).
ceykēṉ eṉṉum vaṭivil īṟu nīṅkiyapiṉ eñci niṟkum ceyku eṉpatum, taṉmiyorumi viṉimuṟṟāy āḷap peṟṟatu. atu ceykum eṉa ummīṟu peṟṟup paṉmi yāyiṟṟu. ivi yiraṇṭum  muṟṟāyum muṟṟeccamāyum varum.
muṟṟu: ceyku = ceyvēṉ, ceykum=ceyvōm.
muṟṟeccam: ceykuvantēṉ, ceykum vantōm.
avaṟṟuḷ,
``ceykeṉ kiḷavi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum
 avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar		(689)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ukaraccuṭṭaṭip piṟantu kālatti yuṇarttum utu eṉṉum īṟupeṟṟum, viṉimutaṉilikaḷ taṉmiyorumi yetirkāla viṉimuṟṟām. ataṉoṭu ummīṟu cēriṉ paṉmi yām. ukaram muṉmiccuṭṭātalāl, utu etir kālatti uṇarttum.
e-ṭu: kūṟutu = kūṟuvēṉ; kūṟutum=kūṟuvōm.
atu eṉṉum īṟupōṉṟē utu eṉṉum īṟum, tuvveṉa mutaṟkuṟiyāy niṉṟu allatu tokku, puṇarcciyil ṟu, ṭu eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu:	cel-cel+tu = cēṟu (celvēṉ)
	cēṟu+um = cēṟum (celvōm)
	koḷ-kōḷ+tu = kōṭu (koḷvēṉ)
	kōṭu+um = kōṭum (koḷvōm)
lakara ḷakaram cēṟal, kōṭal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyariṟ pōṉṟē, īṇṭum tirintaṉaveṉa aṟika.
ceyti eṉṉum muṉṉili yorumi etirkāla viṉi muṟṟu, utu eṉṉum iṭinili peṟṟatākum.
cey+utu+ī = ceyyutī-ceyyuti-ceyti = ceyvāy.
``vaḻipaṭu vōri vallaṟi tīyē''			    (puṟam.10)
cey+utu+ir = ceyyutir-ceytir = ceyvīr.
i orumiyīṟu; ir paṉmiyīṟu. nī-ī-i. nīyir-nīr-īr-ir.
ceyti, ceytir eṉṉum etirkāla muṟṟukaḷ, piṉpu ēvalākavum āḷappaṭṭaṉa.
ceyti eṉpatil t eḻuttuppēṟu eṉpatu moḻi yārāycci yillār kūṟṟām.
ammi, akkālam eṉṉum cēymiccuṭṭukaḷ paṇṭik kālattiyum, ummi eṉṉum muṉmiccuṭṭu etirkālatti yum, uṇarttuvatāl, atu eṉpatu iṟantakāla īṟākavum, utu eṉpatu etirkāla īṟākavum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
cey+atu=ceyyatu-ceytu (i.kā. muṟṟum eccamum)
cey+utu=ceyyutu (e.kā. muṟṟu)
ceytu, ceyyutu eṉṉum iruvaki muṟṟukaḷum piṟ kālattil taṉmikkē variyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa.
kālavakip pirivukaḷ
viṉiccol kāṭṭum iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam, etirkālam ākiya mūṉṟum taṉittaṉi taṉippu, toṭarcci, niṟivu, niṟivut toṭarcci eṉa nālvakippaṭum.
iṟantakālap pirivukaḷ
	e-ṭu: 	vantāṉ-iṟantakālat taṉippu (Past Indefinite)
		vantukoṇṭiruntāṉ-iṟantakālattoṭarcci (Past 			Continuous)
		vantiruntāṉ-iṟantakāla niṟivu (Past perfect)
		vantukoṇṭiruntiruntāṉ-iṟantakāla 				niṟivut toṭarcci (Past perfect continuous)
nikaḻkālap pirivukaḷ
		varukiṟāṉ-nikaḻkālat taṉippu (Present Indefinite)
		vantukoṇṭirukkiṟāṉ-nikaḻkālattoṭarcci 				(Present Continuous)
		vantirukkiṟāṉ-nikaḻkāla niṟivu (Present Perfect)
		vantukoṇṭiruntirukkiṟāṉ -nikaḻkāla 				niṟivut toṭarcci (Present Perfect Continuous)
etirkālap pirivukaḷ
		varuvāṉ-etirkālat taṉippu (Future Indefinite)
		vantukoṇṭiruppāṉ-etirkālattoṭarcci 				(Future Continuous)
		vantiruppāṉ-etirkāla niṟivu (Future Perfect)
		vantukoṇṭiruntiruppāṉ-etirkāla niṟivut 				toṭarcci (Future Perfect Continuous)
4. viṉivakikaḷ
viṉikaḷ, vevvēṟuvakiyil, ovvoṉṟākavum ivviraṇṭākavum paṟpalavākavum vakukkappeṟum.
(1) teriniliviṉi X kuṟippuviṉi
ceyaluṅkālamum veḷippaṭiyāyt teriya niṟpatu terinili viṉi; avaṟṟik kuṟippākak kāṭṭuvatu kuṟippuviṉi.
e-ṭu:	vantāṉ, pōy, irunta - teriniliviṉi.
	uṇṭu, illi, vēṟu - kuṟippuviṉi.
kuṟippuviṉi
kuṟippuviṉi, poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉṉum aṟuvakip poruṭpeyari aṭiyākak koṇṭu tōṉṟi uṭimi, urimi, ceyki eṉṉum mūvakip poruḷ nilikkaḷattil niṉṟu, iruttal allatu ātal viṉiyik kuṟippāka uṇarttum.
iruttalviṉi mukkālattiṟkum potuvākavō, oru kālattiṟkē ciṟappāka vuriyatākavō, irukkum. ātal viṉi eṉṟum oru kālattiṟkuc ciṟappāka vuriyatākavē yirukkum.
e-ṭu:
poruḷnilikkaḷam     	aṭippeyar        	kuṟippuviṉi
uṭimi                   	kuḻi (poruḷ)    	kuḻiyaṉ
urimi                     	maturi (iṭam)      	maturiyāṉ
     ''   	               	mūtiri (kālam)    	mūtiriyāṉ
uṭimi                   	ki (ciṉi)          	kiyaṉ
                            	peru (mi)          	periyaṉ
                                 	oppumi (kuṇam)   	aṉṉaṉ
ceyki                   	naṭi (toḻil)    	naṭiyaṉ
nēṟṟuvari poṉṉaṉ (poṉṉaṉāyiruntāṉ)-i.kā. 
iṉṟu poṉṉaṉ (poṉṉaṉāyirukkiṉṟāṉ-ni. kā. 
iṉimēṟ poṉṉaṉ (poṉṉaṉāyiruppāṉ)- e.kā.

maḻipeytu    	 payir nalla (nallavāyiṉa)-i.kā.
ivaṉ poṉṉaṉ eṉpatil, poṉṉaṉ eṉpatu āḷik kuṟippiṉ, peyarppayaṉiliyām.
		mūtiri = ātiri (va.)
kaṭavuḷ........bgÇa‹’ eṉpatil periyaṉ eṉpatu mukkālattiṟkum potuvāyiṉum, periyaṉā yirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu nikaḻkāla vaṭivilēyē kūṟappeṟum.
mukkā lattiṉum ottiyal poruḷic
 ceppuvar nikaḻuṅ kālattāṉē		(naṉ. 389)
(2) muṟṟuviṉi x eccaviṉi
muṟṟiya viṉiccol vaṭivil eḻuvāykku muṟṟup payaṉi liyāka varuvatu muṟṟuviṉi; muṟṟāta viṉiccol vaṭiviṟ peyariyāvatu viṉiyiyāvatu taḻuvi eccap payaṉiliyāy varuvatu eccaviṉi.
e-ṭu:	vantāṉ, illi-muṟṟuviṉi (Finite verb) vantu, pōṉa, uḷḷa, iṉṟi-eccaviṉi (Relative or verbal participle)
eccaviṉi peyareccam, viṉiyeccam eṉa iruvakip paṭum.
e-ṭu: vanta, nalla-peyareccam (Relative Participle)
	       vantu, iṉṟi-viṉiyeccam (Verbal Participle)
(3) mūviṭa viṉi
taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkki ākiya mūviṭaṅkaṭkuriya viṉikaḷ mūviṭaviṉiyām.
v-ṭu: 	vantēṉ, vantōm-taṉmiviṉi
	vantāy, vā, varātē-muṉṉiliviṉi
	vantāṉ, varuvāṉ-paṭarkkiviṉi
nikaḻcciviṉi, ēvalviṉi eṉa muṉṉiliviṉi iruvakippaṭum.
e-ṭu: vantāy, varuvāy-nikaḻcciviṉi
	        vā, varuka - ēvalviṉi (Imperative Mood)
ēvalviṉiyum, kaṭṭaḷi, viyaṅkōḷ eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. kaṭṭaḷi atikāra ēval; viyaṅkōḷ vēṇṭukōḷ allatu matippāṉa ēval.
e-ṭu: vā, vāruṅkaḷ - kaṭṭaḷiviṉi
	        varuka - viyaṅkōḷviṉi
ēvalviṉi mīṇṭum tūṇṭal, vilakkal eṉa  iruvakip paṭum.
e-ṭu:	vā, varuka-tūṇṭuviṉi
	varātē, varaṟka-vilakkuviṉi (Prohibitive Mood)
ēval palamaṭiyākavum varum.
e-ṭu:	varuvi, pōkku-irumaṭi yēval
	varuvippi, pōkkuvi-mummaṭi yēval
	varuvippippi, pōkkuvippi-nāṉmaṭi yēval
taṉiyēval pōṉṟē paṉmaṭi yēvalkaḷum viṉimutaṉili yāka amiyum.
e-ṭu: varuvittāṉ, pōkkuvippittāṉ
(4) uṭaṉpāṭṭuviṉi x etirmaṟiviṉi
viṉi nikaḻcciyi uḷḷatākak kuṟikkum viṉi uṭaṉ pāṭṭu viṉi;  illatāka maṟukkum viṉi etirmaṟi viṉi.
e-ṭu:	vā, vantāṉ, uṇṭu-uṭaṉpāṭu (Affirmative or 				Positive verb)
       	varātē, vantilaṉ, illi-etirmaṟi (Negative verb)
(5)	ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉi x ceyappaṭuporuḷ    kuṉṟāviṉi	
ivi muṟiyē ceyappaṭuporuḷ koḷvatum koḷḷā tatumām.
e-ṭu: 	naṭakkiṟāṉ, varukiṟāṉ-ce. po. kuṉṟiyaviṉi 
		(Intransitive verb)
	ceykiṟāṉ, uṇpāṉ-ce. po. kuṉṟāviṉi 				(Transitive verb)
(6) taṉviṉi x piṟaviṉi
oruvaṉ tāṉē ceyvatu taṉviṉi; piṟaṉik koṇṭu ceyvikkum viṉi piṟaviṉi.
e-ṭu:	vantāṉ, kaṟṟāṉ-taṉviṉi
	varuvittāṉ, kaṟpittāṉ-piṟaviṉi (Causal or Causative		Verb)
	varuvittāṉ, kaṟpippittāṉ-paṉmaṭip piṟaviṉi
(7) ceyviṉi x ceyappāṭṭuviṉi
viṉimutalē eḻuvāyāy niṉṟu oṉṟic ceytalik kuṟikkum viṉi ceyviṉi; ceyappaṭuporuḷ eḻuvāyāki viṉimutalāl tāṉ ētēṉum ceyyappaṭutalik kuṟikkum viṉi ceyappāṭṭuviṉi.
e-ṭu:	nāy cōṟṟit tiṉkiṟatu-ceyviṉi (Active Voice)
    	cōṟu nāyāl tiṉṉappaṭukiṟatu ceyappāṭṭu 			viṉi (Passive Voice)
	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ iṟṟit tamiḻit 					tūymippaṭuttiṉārkaḷ - ceyviṉi
	iṟṟit tamiḻ maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāl tūymip 				paṭuttap peṟṟatu - ceyappāṭṭuviṉi
	akattiyar tolkāppiyarukkut tamiḻ kaṟpikka 				villi - ceyviṉi
akattiyarāl tolkāppiyarukkut tamiḻ kaṟpikkap paṭavilli - ceyappāṭṭuviṉi
(8) talimiviṉi x tuṇiviṉi
kīḻē irukkiṟāṉ, kūṭṭam muṭikiṟatu-ivaṟṟil iru, muṭi eṉṉum iraṇṭum talimi viṉi (Main verb).
avaṉ vantirukkiṟāṉ, eṉakku naṭakkamuṭikiṟatu-ivaṟṟil iru, muṭi eṉṉum iraṇṭum tuṇiviṉi (Auxilliary verb).
(9) niṟiviṉi x kuṟiviṉi
īreṇ mūviṭattum mukkālattilum puṭipeyarum viṉi niṟiviṉi; aṅṅaṉam puṭipeyarāviṉi kuṟiviṉi.
e-ṭu: vā, pō-niṟiviṉi (Perfect verb)
māṭṭu, vēṇṭum-kuṟiviṉi (Defective verb)
(ceyya) māṭṭuvēṉ, (eṉakku) vēṇṭum eṉpaṉa vā, pō eṉpaṉapōl, iṟantakālattilum varāmi kāṇka.
(10) toṭaṅkalviṉi x muṭittalviṉi
toṭaṅkalviṉi toḻiṟpeyaruṭaṉ uṟu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi koḷḷum; muṭittal viṉi iṟantakāla viṉiyeccat tuṭaṉ viṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi koḷḷum. viṭu-iṭu.
e-ṭu:	collaluṟṟāṉ - toṭaṅkalviṉi (Inceptive or 
          Inchoative verb)
	eḻutiviṭṭāṉ, vantiṭṭāṉ-muṭittal viṉi.
(11) taṟporuṭṭu viṉi x maṟporuṭṭu viṉi
oruvaṉ tāṉē taṉakkuc ceytukoḷḷum viṉi taṟ poruṭṭu viṉiyām (Reflexive verb or Middle voice). itu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ koḷ eṉṉum tuṇi viṉi cērntuvarum. oruvaṉ piṟaṉukkuc ceytu koṭukkum viṉi maṟporuṭṭu viṉiyām. itu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ koṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntuvarum.
e-ṭu:
eḻutikkoḷ,mukamvaḻittukkoṇṭāṉ taṟporuṭ viṉi.
eḻutikkoṭu, kaṟṟukkoṭuttāṉ-maṟporuṭṭuviṉi.
iṉi, oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu etirāṉa iṇiyaṉṟit taṉittaṉi vevvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟi varum pala viṉikaḷumuḷa. aviyāvaṉa:
(1) vāḻttuviṉi (Optative Mood)
oruvari allatu oru poruḷi allatu ōr amippi, nīṭuniṟkumāṟu allatu taḻittōṅkumāṟu vāḻttuvatu vāḻttu viṉiyām. itu taṉmiyiṭattil varātu.

(7) ceyaviṭal viṉi
paṭarkkiyāṉi oṉṟu ceyyaviṭumāṟu muṉṉili yāṉi ēvuvatu ceyaviṭal viṉiyām. iti icivippu viṉi eṉṟum collalām. itu akaravīṟṟu viṉi yeccattuṭaṉ aṭṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntatu. itu etirmaṟiyil varātu.
e-ṭu:
		orumi: avaṉ varaṭṭu = nī avaṉi varaviṭu.
		paṉmi: avaṉ varaṭṭum = nīr avaṉi varaviṭum.
varaṭṭum eṉpatu varavoṭṭu eṉpataṉ tokuttal. oṭṭutal icital. oṭṭu-aṭṭu. vara+aṭṭu = varaṭṭu. vara+aṭṭum = varaṭṭum, ceyyavoṭṭu eṉṉum tiriyā vaṭivam viṉiyāyiṉ mūviṭattum varum. atu perumpālum etirmaṟiyilēyē varum.
e-ṭu: 
	tūṅkavoṭṭēṉ, tūṅkavoṭṭāy, 					tūṅkavoṭṭār.
(8) taṭi nīkkaviṉi

e-ṭu:
	nīṅkaḷ pōkalām.
(9) āṟṟalviṉi (Potential Mood)
oṉṟic ceyya muṭitalik kuṟikkum viṉi āṟṟal viṉiyām. itu mūvakiyil amiyum.
1. 	akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattōṭu iyal (ēl), oṇṇu, ollu, māḷ, muṭi, kūṭu, mutaliya tuṇiviṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟucērtal.
	kūṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi uṭaṉpāṭṭil maṭṭum varum, 	ēṉiya uṭaṉpāṭu etirmaṟi ākiya iruvaṭivilum varum.
	iviyellām tamittut talimi viṉiyākavum varum.
e-ṭu: 
		varaviyalum, taravoṇṇum, ceyyavollum, 			ceyyamāḷum, eḻutamuṭiyum, tēṟakkūṭum.
atu eṉṉāl iyalum, oṇṇum, ollum, māḷum, muṭiyum; kūṭum.
varaviyalātu, taravoṇṇātu, ceyyavollātu,        	muṭiyātu. ceyya māḷātu. eḻuta muṭiyātu.
atu eṉṉāl iyalātu, oṇṇātu, māḷātu, muṭiyātu.
2. 	akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattōṭu kil allatu māṭṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉiyiṉ muṟṟuccērtal.
kil eṉpatu mukkālattilum varum; māṭṭu eṉpatu etirkālattil maṭṭum varum.
māṭṭu eṉpatu māḷ eṉpataṉ piṟaviṉi. māḷutal muṭital; māṭṭutal muṭiyacceytal.
e-ṭu:
ceyyakiṉṟēṉ, ceyyakiṟkiṉṟēṉ, ceyyakiṟpēṉ. 	ceyyamāṭṭuvēṉ = muṭiyacceyvēṉ, eṉṉāṟ 	ceyya muṭiyum.
3.	akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattōṭu paṭavilli eṉṉum kūṭṭu viṉiccol cērtal. itu uṭaṉpāṭṭil varātu.
e-ṭu:
		(eṉṉāl) eḻuntirukkappaṭavilli = 					eḻuntirukkamuṭiya villi.
kūṭu, paṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉikaḷ etirmaṟi vaṭivil vilakku viṉiyām.
e-ṭu:
		nī pōkakkūṭātu, nī pōkappaṭātu.
māṭṭu, muṭi eṉṉum tuṇiviṉikaḷ taṉmi etirmaṟi vaṭivil maṟuppu viṉiyām.
e-ṭu: nāṉ varamāṭṭēṉ, nāṉ varamuṭiyātu.
(10) kēḷvippāṭṭu viṉi
piṟar vāyilāyk kēḷvippaṭṭatik kuṟikkum viṉi kēḷvippāṭṭu viṉiyām. itu viṉimuṟṟōṭum peya rōṭum ām eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntuvarum.
e-ṭu: 
nēṟṟuvantāṉām, nāḷikku viṭumuṟiyām.
(11) uyttuṇarviṉi
itu mūvakiyil varum.
1.	viṉimuṟṟuṭaṉ pōlum allatu pōlirukkiṟatu 		eṉṉum coṟcērtal.
	e-ṭu: 
		avaṉ tēṟiviṭṭāṉpōlum! iṉṟu maḻivarum 		pōlirukkiṟatu.
2.	viṉimuṟṟuṭaṉ ām kāṭṭiyum eṉṉum īriṭic coṟ cērtal.
	e-ṭu: nēṟṟuvantāṉāṅ kāṭṭiyum.
3.	viṉimuṟṟuṭaṉ ākkum eṉṉum coṟcērtal. ākum-ākkum.
	e-ṭu: avaṉ vīṭṭililliyākkum.
(12) uyarvu kuṟittal viṉi
peyaroṭu ākkum eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntu oruvariṉ uyarvu kuṟippatu uyarvukuṟippu viṉiyām.
e-ṭu: 
	avar yār teriyumā? amerikkac celvaruḷ 				talimiyāṉa varākkum!
(13) tēṟṟaviṉi
oru viṉinikaḻcciyi uṟutippaṭuttuvatu tēṟṟa viṉiyām. tēṟutal teḷital. tēṟṟutal teḷivittal. tēṟṟu vatu tēṟṟam. itu aṟuvakiyil amiyum.
	1. ēkāraviṭic colloṭu puṇarnta akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṉpiṉ muṟṟuviṉi.
		e-ṭu: varavē varuvāṉ.
	2. tāṉ eṉṉum iṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta akaravīṟṟuviṉiyeccattiṉpiṉ muṟṟuviṉi.
			e-ṭu: varattāṉ ceyvāṉ.
	3. ēkāra viṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta iṟantakāla viṉiyeccat tiṉpiṉ viṉimuṟṟu.
			e-ṭu: vantē tīrvāṉ.
	4. ēkāra viṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta viṉimuṟṟu.
			e-ṭu: vantāṉē!
	5. viṉimuṟṟiṉpiṉ tāṉ eṉṉum iṭiccol.
			e-ṭu: varuvāṉtāṉ.
			itu ceṉṉivaḻakku; attuṇic ciṟantataṉṟu.
	6. viṉimuṟṟiṉpiṉ maṉṟa eṉṉum iṭiccol.
			e-ṭu: maṭavi maṉṟa	(naṟ. 94)
	itu ceyyuḷ vaḻakku.
(14) aruḷviṉi
	iṟivaṉum muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻumuṉivarum ceyyum ceyalik kuṟikkum viṉi aruḷviṉi. itu iṟanta kāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ aruḷ eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntu varum.
		e-ṭu:	eḻuntaruḷiṉār, colliyaruḷiṉār
				tiruvāymalarntār, tiruvurukkarantār 
eṉpavaṟṟit tiruviṉi eṉṉalām.
(15) utaviviṉi
	iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ ī, tā, koṭu eṉṉum viṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟucērntu, ilavacac ceyalik kuṟippatu utavi viṉiyām.
		e-ṭu: ceṉṟī, vantī, collittā, eḻutikkoṭu. 
			eḻutikkoṭuttal kimmāṟu karutiṉ 				maṟporuṭṭu viṉiyām.
(16) ārvaviṉi (Desiderative Verb)
	viṉinikaḻcciyiṉmītu ārvaṅkāṭṭum viṉi ārva viṉiyām. itu aṟuvakiyil amiyum.
	1. ēṉ eṉṉum iṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta ēval viṉi.
			e-ṭu: vāruṅkaḷēṉ.
	2. vēṇṭum eṉṉum tuṇiviṉiyoṭu kūṭiya toḻiṟ peyar allatu akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam.
			e-ṭu: 	tāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷ illattiṟkut tappātu 				varal vēṇṭum. iṟiva, nī eṅṅaṉamum 					eṉakku aruḷavēṇṭum.
	3. taṉmi vāḻttu.
			e-ṭu: yāṉ vāḻka!
	4. ēyiṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta ēvalviṉi.
			e-ṭu: nillumē-niṉmē, varaṭṭumē.
	5. ōviṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta ēvalviṉi.
			e-ṭu: moḻiyumō-moḻimō.
	6. tilliṭiccollōṭu kūṭiya viyaṅkōḷviṉi.
			e-ṭu: varukatil.
	ivaṟṟuṭ piṉ mūṉṟuñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉa aṟika.
(17) aṇiyaviṉi
	oṉṟic ceyvataṟku aṇiyamāy (āyattamāy) irukkum niliyik kuṟikkum viṉi aṇiya viṉiyām.
itu iruvakiyil amiyum.
1. akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṉ pō eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi.
		e-ṭu: collap pōṉāṉ.
	2. nālām vēṟṟumit toḻiṟpeyar allatu akara vīṟṟu                	   viṉiyeccattiṉ piṉ iru eṉṉum tuṇi viṉi.
			e-ṭu: eḻutuvataṟkiruntāṉ, eḻutaviruntāṉ.
(18) toṭarcciviṉi
	viṉittoṭarcciyik kuṟikkum viṉi toṭarcci viṉiyām. itu iruvakiyil amiyum.
	1. iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattiṉ piṉ allatu nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṉ (Present participle) piṉ vā eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi.
e-ṭu: eḻutivarukiṟāṉ, eḻutikkoṇṭu varukiṟāṉ.
	2. ēyiṭiccolloṭu puṇarnta nikaḻkāla viṉiyec cattiṉ          	 piṉ pō eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi.
			e-ṭu: paṭittukkoṇṭē pōkiṟāṉ.
(19) ātalviṉi
	oṉṟic ceyvataṟku āṉa nilimiyi uṇarttum viṉi ātal viṉiyām.
			e-ṭu: varalāṉāṉ, eḻutalāṉēṉ.
(20) iraṭṭikkiḷaviviṉi (Frequentative Verb)
	eṉṟum aṭukkuttoṭarāṉa irukuṟiliṇiyākavē varum viṉi iraṭṭikkiḷavi viṉiyām.
			e-ṭu: kuṟukuṟukkum, calacalakkiṟatu.
(21) mikuppuviṉi (Intensive Verb)
	oru ceyali allatu nikaḻcciyi mikuttuk kāṭṭum viṉi mikuppu viṉiyām.
e-ṭu	:	potuviṉi          	mikuppuviṉi
		ārikkiṟatu            	āravārikkiṟatu
		āyntāṉ            	ārāyntāṉ
		kāppāṉ            		pātukāppāṉ
ārittal olittal. ārtal niṟital. āra = nirampa, mika. āra-ār. pātu = ōmpum veṭci ānirip pakuti.
(22) vaḻuvamiti viṉi (Anomalous Verb)
	vaḻuvāyiṉum vaḻuvaṟṟatupōl ilakkaṇiyarāl amikkap peṟṟaviṉi vaḻuvamiti viṉiyām.
	uṇṭu (uḷ+tu) eṉṉum kuṟippuviṉimuṟṟu, mutaṟkaṇ paṭarkki oṉṟaṉpāl viṉiyāyiruntu, piṉpu aimpāl mūviṭa īreṇ potuviṉiyāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
uṇṭu eṉpataṟku etirviṉi iṉṟu eṉpatu. atu iṉṟum oṉṟaṉpāl viṉiyākavē vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
uṇṭu = uḷḷatu (kuṟippuviṉiyālaṇiyum peyar). uṇṭu paṇṇutal uḷḷatāyc ceytal. uṇṭākkutal uḷḷatākkutal.
vēṇṭum, vēṇṭām, vēṇṭā
vēṇṭum eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi, vēṇṭum eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ (ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu) etirkāla muṟṟām. iv vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu, iṟṟi maliyāḷattiṟpōl tamiḻilum mutaṟ kālattil aimpāl mūviṭa īreṇ potu viṉiyāyiruntatu. atu piṉṉarc ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil, palar pāloḻinta nāṟpāṟ paṭarkkikkē variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. itu
pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi
 avvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻuṅ kālattuc
 ceyyum eṉṉum kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā.      (tol. viṉi. 30)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl aṟiyappaṭum. ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu nikaḻkālattiṟkum etir kālattiṟkum potuveṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟ karutappaṭṭatiṉāl, avar ati nikaḻuṅ kālattuc ceyyum eṉṉum kiḷavi eṉṟār.
iv vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu, ulakavaḻakkiṟ palarpāṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi āṇpāl peṇpāṟkum vilakkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl, atu iṉṟu aḵṟiṇi yirupālilēyē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu: māṭu varum, māṭukaḷ varum.
avaḷ varuvāḷ eṉṉum peṇpālīṟṟuk kūṟṟu iḻivākak karutappaṭuvatāl, matippuvāynta peṇṭirip paṟṟik kūṟu miṭattu `anta ammā varum' eṉṟu ceyyum eṉṉum vāy pāṭṭu viṉi koṭuttuk kūṟuvatu, iḻivu nīkkapporuṭ ṭēyaṉṟi iyalpāṉa vaḻakkup paṟṟiya taṉṟu.
vēṇṭu eṉṉum viṉiccolliṉ iyaṟporuḷ virumpu eṉpatē. vēḷ-vēṇṭu. vēṭṭal virumputal. vēṭki viruppam.
vēṇṭutalvēṇ ṭāmi yilāṉaṭi cērntārkku 
 yāṇṭum iṭumpi yila 	(kuṟaḷ. 4)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, vēṇṭutal vēṇṭāmi eṉṉun toṭar viruppu veṟuppik kuṟittal kāṇka.
oruvariṉ utaviyi mika virumpiṉāl, avarik keñcik kēṭkanērum. ataṉāl, vēṇṭaṟ collukku maṉṟāṭaṟ poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu. vēṇṭukōḷ, vēṇṭikkoḷ, iṟi vēṇṭal mutaliya coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum ip poruḷa ṉavē.
potuvāka, tēviyāṉa poruḷē virumpappaṭum. oru poruḷ oruvarāl virumpappaṭiṉ, perumpālum atu avarkkut tēvi 
eṉpatiyē uṇarttum. itaṉāl, virumpaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu tēvik karuttup piṟantatu. āṅkilat tilum want eṉṉum col viruppattiyum tēviyiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
vēṇṭi = virumpi, tēvippaṭṭu. vēṇṭātu = virum pāmal, tēviyiṉṟi. vēṇṭiyatik kēḷ, vēṇṭiyamaṭṭum eṭuttukkoḷ, eṉṉum toṭarkaḷum ivviru karuttiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
āyiṉ, ip poruḷilum itaṉ vaḻipporuḷkaḷilum, vēṇṭaṟcol varum coṟṟoṭaramippu māṟiviṭṭatu. nāṉ kuṭikkat taṇṇīr vēṇṭum. (taṇṇīri vēṇṭu kiṟēṉ) eṉṟu irukkavēṇṭiya uyartiṇip payaṉili muṭipu, eṉakkuk kuṭikkat taṇṇīr vēṇṭum eṉṟu, aḵṟiṇip payaṉili muṭipāy māṟiṟṟu. ‘Ú v‹d nt©L »whŒ?’ v‹gJ, ‘cd¡F v‹d nt©L«? eṉṟu māṟiṟṟu. nāṉ taṇṇīr kuṭikka vēṇṭum eṉpatum, nāṉ taṇṇīr kuṭikka virumpukiṟēṉ eṉṉum poruḷatē.
mikat tēviyāṉa ceyalik kaṭṭāyamāyc ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, tēvikkaruttiṉiṉṟu kaṭṭāyak karuttup piṟantatu.
e-ṭu: 	kaṭal vaṇikar kappaliṟ koṇṭuvarum 			carakkukaṭku āyam (vari) celuttavēṇṭum.
kaṭṭum āyam kaṭṭāyam. kaṭṭāyaviṉi atikāratti yaṉṟi aṉpip poṟuttatāyiṉ ārvaviṉiyām.
e-ṭu:	eṉ makaṉ tirumaṇattiṟkut tāṅkaḷ 						kaṭṭāyam varalvēṇṭum.
kaṭṭāya viṉi perumpālum tavaṟātu ceyyappaṭu taliṉ, vēṇṭaṟcol tavaṟillā uṇmiyiyum uṇartta lāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiyaṉ eṉṉum peṟṟōṉ peyar, tāḻttappaṭṭa 	vakuppiṉariṭiyum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatāl, atu tūya tamiḻc collāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
iv vuṇmi yuṇarttaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟē uṟavumuṟi yuṇarttaṟ karuttum kiḷittatu.
e-ṭu: avaṉ uṉakku eṉṉa vēṇṭum?
ïJ, ‘mt‹ cd¡F v‹d cwîKiwahjš nt©L«? eṉpataṉ tokuttalām.
iṉi, vēṇṭum eṉṉum col kaṭṭāya viṉiyāy allatu viruppa viṉiyāy varum toṭariyam, oruvar viṉiyiyēyaṉṟi iruvar viṉiyik koṇṭatākavum irukkum.
ituceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ kiḷavi
 iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭā kummē
 taṉpā lāṉum piṟaṉpā lāṉum	(tol. viṉi.46)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviyum ataṟku, itu ceyal vēṇṭumeṉpatupaṭa varuñcol taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum eṉa īriṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇ miyi yuṭittām eṉṟavāṟu.
tāṉeṉṟatu ceyalatu viṉimutali.
ōtal vēṇṭum eṉṟavaḻi, vēṇṭumeṉpatu ōtaṟku viṉi mutalāyiṉāṟkum avaṉōtali virumpum tantikkum ēṟṟavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu cēṉāvariyar uritta uriyiyum nōkkuka.
ākavē, nīṅkaḷ tamiḻiṟ peyar tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu nīṅkaḷ tamiḻiṟ peyar tāṅkuvati nāṉ virumpu kiṉṟēṉ eṉṟum poruḷpaṭuvatu kāṇka. itaṉāl vēṇṭaṟ colliṉ aṭippaṭip poruḷ veḷippaṭu vatiyum nōkkuka.
coṟkaḷ tam iyaṟporu ḷaṭippaṭiyil palvēṟu putup poruḷkaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. peyarkaḷuḷ ākupeya riruppatu pōṉṟē, viṉikaḷuḷḷum ākuviṉikaḷ uḷaveṉa aṟika.
vēṇṭum eṉpataṉ etirmaṟi vēṇṭām eṉpatu. itu ceyyām eṉṉum vāypāṭṭut taṉmippaṉmi etirkāla etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟu. vēṇṭām = vēṇṭōm. kāṇēṉ eṉṉum orumipporuḷil kāṇōm eṉṉum paṉmic col vaḻaṅkivaruvatu pōṉṟē, vēṇṭēṉ eṉṉum orumip poruḷilum vēṇṭām eṉṉum paṉmiccol vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟateṉa aṟika. vēṇṭēṉ piṟantakat tīṇṭiya vāḻvē eṉṉum taṉippāṭalaṭiyi nōkkuka.
vēṇṭā eṉpatu, vēṇṭām eṉpataṉ īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivum vēṇṭāta eṉṉum etirmaṟi viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariṉ, īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivum, āka iruvakiyil niṟkum.

virutuppeyar 142
viḻuttam 130
veṭṭeḻuttu 2,121
vittūṟṟi  5
pāvāṇar poṉmoḻikaḷ
māntaṉeṉak kumarimali maruviyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
māṇpuṭiya nākarikam malarntavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
moḻivaḷarcci mutaṉmutalāy muṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
mōṉiyuṭaṉ ciṟantaceyyuḷ pēciyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
tuṟinakarāl kaṭalvaṇikam tōṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
piṟanilattu vaṇikariyum pēṇiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
toṉmiyoṭu muṉmi; toṉmiyoṭu naṉmi;
tāymiyoṭu tūymi; taḻuviḷami vaḷami.
pakuttaṟivē māṉamuṭaṉ paṭittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivāl tiṇivakutta paṇpuṭiyāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
paṉimaliyi mutaṉmutaṟkip paṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palamuṟimīṉ pulivilatiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
palakaliyum palanūlum payiṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palapoṟiyum matilaraṇiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
irutiṇikkum īntuvakkum iṉpamuṟṟāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
ītalici yāviṭattē iṟantavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
kaṭalnaṭuvē kalañceluttik karikaṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
kalappaṭiyāl kuṇattīvik kāttavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivic caṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttār kalvi
mikuttataṉāl uṇṭō payaṉ?
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ.
tamiḻā uṉṟaṉ muṉṉavaṉē
taliyāy vāḻnta teṉṉavaṉē
amiḻtām māri aṉṉavaṉē
aḻakāy mutaṉūl coṉṉavaṉē.
paḵṟuḷi nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉām
pakuttaṟivup paṇpiṟ ciṟantavaṉām
pakiyām maliyi uṟaḻntavaṉām
pālum puliyiṟ kaṟantavaṉām.
aṉpeṉpatu ēcuvum puttarumpōl ellāriṭattum kāṭṭum nēyam.
āṭci oppumi naṭpuṟaviṟkē aṉṟi aṭimittaṉattiṟku ētuvākātu.
ōyvakaviyit tīrmāṉikkum aḷaviyāy irukka vēṇṭiyatu paṇit tiṟamiyēyaṉṟi akavi varampaṉṟu.
tuṟavu tammāliyaṉṟavari potumakkaṭkut toṇṭu ceyvatu ciṟanta tuṟavākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiyāvil vilaṅkiṉam irukkum nilimiyi uṇartta avaṟṟiṉ kaḻuttuppuṇṇum vilāvelumput tōṟṟamum pōtum.
kaṭṭuppāṭṭillāviṭiṉ kāvalaṉuṅ kāvāṉātalālum ceṅkōlāṭciyoṭu kūṭiya irukaṭciyaracē kuṭiyaraciṟ kēṟṟatām.
piṟamoḻi pēcum ciṟupāṉmiyar piḷḷikaḷum avvan nāṭṭup perumpāṉmi moḻiyiyē kaṟṟal vēṇṭum.... 
kāl moḻivāri mānilap pirivu eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatum payaṉaṟṟatumām.
māntaṉ vāḻkki, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉa iruvakittu. maṉiviyōṭu kūṭi illattiliruntu ataṟkuriya aṟañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki illaṟam; ulakap paṟṟit tuṟantu ataṟkuriya aṟattōṭu kūṭik kāṭṭil tavañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki tuṟavaṟam.
oruvaṉ illattil iruntu maṉiyāḷōṭu kūṭivāḻiṉum aṟañ ceyyātu iruppiṉ avaṉ vāḻkki illaṟamākātu veṟumaṉāṉa ilvāḻkkiyām.
ilaṅkiyil iṭarppaṭum makkaḷ perumpālum tamiḻarāyiruttaliṉ avarkaḷiṉ urimiyip pēṇikkāttaṟku aṅkuḷḷa intiyat tūtāṇmik kuḻuttalivar tamiḻarākavē iruttal vēṇṭum.
karuttuvēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭantantu oru cārāri orucārār paḻikkātum pakikkātum iruppatē uṇmiyāṉa pakuttaṟivām.
iv vulakil tamiḻaṉip pōl muṉpu uyarntavaṉu milli; piṉpu tāḻntavaṉum illi.
iṟṟit tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārum tammit tāmē tāḻttuvatilum, iṉattārip pakittup pakivari vāḻavippatilum, pakivar maṉaṅkuḷirat tam muṉṉōrip paḻippatilum, tammarumit tāymoḻiyip puṟakkaṇittup pakivariṉ aricceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyip pōṟṟuvatilum oppuyarvaṟṟavarāy uḻalkiṉṟaṉar.
tantiyum aracum
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta piḷḷikaṭkellām ūṇuṭi yuṟiyuḷ aḷikkat tanti kaṭamippaṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟē, oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta kuṭikaṭkellām vēliyum pātukāppum aḷikka aracu kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kōvil vaḻipāṭum iruvakic caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻil naṭipeṟṟāloḻiya, tamiḻ viṭutaliyaṭintu taṉ paḻamperumiyi mīḷap peṟa muṭiyātu. tamiḻuyarntāltāṉ tamiḻaṉ uyarvāṉ.
antaṇaṉ aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻakat tuṟaviyariyē kuṟittatu pōṉṟu pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyiyē kuṟittatu.
tamiḻaṉ eṉṉum iṉam tamiḻpaṟṟiyatē yātalāl. tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭamē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamām. atu teṉvāriyil mūḻkip pōṉa kumarināṭē.
2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta mukamatiyar ciṟupāṉmiyarēṉum āṅkilar nīṅkiyavuṭaṉ tamakkeṉak kōṉmi (Soveriegnity) koṇṭa taṉināṭu peṟṟuviṭṭaṉarē. nūṟāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉamē tōṉṟi orukāl nāvalantēyam muḻutum āṇṭa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻar ēṉ tam nāṭṭiyum peṟavilli?..... itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, mukamatiyarkkuḷḷa ōriṉa vuṇarcciyum oṟṟumiyum maṟamum māṉamum iṟṟit tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārkku eḷḷaḷavum iṉmiyēyām.
ellāk kēṭukaḷum pāṭukaḷum tuṉpaṅkaḷum tollikaḷum nīṅki ellārum iṉpamāka vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṉum iṉṉaruḷ nōkkam koṇṭē teḷḷiya maṉamum oḷḷiya aṟivum tiṇṇiya neñcum nuṇṇiya matiyum koṇṭa tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷi iyaṟṟiṉār.
paṇanāṭṭamiṉṟi, ellārum tattamakkiyaṉṟa paṇiyic ceytu, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum otta urimiyōṭu payaṉpaṭutti oṟṟumiyāka oruṅkē vāḻum kuṭumpa allatu kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyē potuvuṭimiyākum.
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum eṅṅaṉam tiṟamikkut takka paṇiyum tēvikkut takka nukarcciyum uṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum iruttal vēṇṭum. ituvē pāttuṇṭal eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭimi.
tamiḻ viṭutaliyē tamiḻaṉ viṭutali.
aintu airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi tamiḻē. avaṟṟiṉ koṭumuṭiyē camaṟkirutam. ākavē airōppiya moḻiyamippiṉ allatu varalāṟṟiṉ tiṟavukōl tamiḻilēyē āḻap putintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. itik kaṇṭupiṭikkumvari mēliyar moḻiyārāycci yellām viḻalukku nīriṟittalum vāṉattu mīṉukku vaṉtūṇṭil iṭutalumē yākum.
tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kulippavar ellām vāḷpōṟ pakivarum kēḷpōṟ pakivarumē.
evarettu (Everest) eṉṉum veḷḷimali pōluyarntum, amēcāṉ (Amazon) eṉṉum amerikka āṟupōl akaṉṟum, amitivāriyiṉ (Pacific Ocean) teṉṉakaḻipō lāḻntum piṟaṅkit tōṉṟiya perumpulami vāyntavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷē eṉpatu mikiyaṉṟu.
paṉimalipōlap parantum nīṇṭum uyarntum taliciṟantu viḷaṅkiya tamiḻp pulavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ oruvarē.
iṉam moḻi ilakkiyam nākarikam paṇpāṭu eṉṉum aiṅkūṟaminta tamiḻam muḻuvatiyum āriyam viḻuṅkak kavviviṭṭatu.
aḵṟiṇi pōlirunta tamiḻaṉip paṭikka vittut taṉmāṉamūṭṭi mīṇṭum uyartiṇippaṭuttiyavaṉ āṅkilēyaṉē.
ïªâahš jÄœ bfL« v‹w¿ªnj ‘ïªâ bghJbkhÊah? eṉṉum cuvaṭiyi veḷiyiṭṭār tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ. peṟṟavarukkuttāṉ teriyum piḷḷiyiṉ arumi; kaṟṟavarukkuttāṉ teriyum tamiḻiṉ arumi. paṟṟum pulamiyum aṟṟa maṟṟavarkkut teriyumā naṟṟamiḻp perumi.
poruḷāṭcit tuṟiyil ettuṇi muṉṉēṟṟa māyiṉum intiyoṭu kalantatu, nañcoṭu kalanta pālē.
tamiḻ allatu tamiḻar tōṉṟiya iṭam teṟkē mūḻkip pōṉa kumarināṭē. ātalāl, kumarināṭṭi, aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷi varital vēṇṭum.
